Conclusion: 连续潜空间攻击框架可与学得语言生成分布直接耦合，在保持原有采样/优化范式的同时生成自然提示；映射方式的选择对风险估计影响显著，基于随机映射的风险评估不具有自然提示基础。

Abstract: \emph{Continuous-space} attacks find prompts that trigger malicious behaviors, such as overthinking, in black-box LLMs by sampling or optimizing over a low-dimensional Gaussian latent space and decoding it to text through a \emph{random mapping} and nearest-token lookup. Because the random mapping is not learned from language, its prompts can be highly unnatural, and as such easily detectable through input guardrails, and the risk estimates they yield are not grounded in natural prompts. In this paper, we demonstrate that the same continuous latent-space framework can be coupled directly to a learned generative distribution over language, a continuous diffusion language model (dLLM), which removes the artificial random mapping while preserving the standard-Gaussian latent formulation and the downstream sampling or optimization procedure. We evaluate this approach under two settings: sampling-based estimation using \emph{RareTrap} and optimization-based search using \emph{ThinkTrap}. We find that character-level perplexity drops from $10^3$--$10^7$ to 3--5 in RareTrap and from 20--28 to 4.3--5.4 in ThinkTrap. We also find that model risk estimates can differ by an order of magnitude across the two mappings.

</details>


### [105] [When to Follow the Teacher: Advantage-Based On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31563)
*Mahdi Sabbaghi,George Pappas,Hamed Hassani,Adel Javanmard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出优势加权的在线策略蒸馏AOPD，用学生采样的标准rollout与终端奖励即可无偏估计教师动作的对偶优势，无需额外rollout，在数学、代码、MedQA任务上优于蒸馏与RL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准在线策略蒸馏（OPD）在轨迹每个状态上都强制学生模仿教师，隐含假设“教师指导总是有益的”。但教师偏好的动作未必真正有助于当前学生成功，因此需要区分“教师偏好”与“对学生实际有助”的动作。

Method: AOPD对教师动作按其相对于学生的反事实优势进行加权：提升学生期望回报的动作被强化，其余被降权。直接计算该优势需在每步尝试替代教师动作并重新rollout，代价高。作者证明该目标仅用学生标准rollout及其终端奖励即可估计——在教师重要性权重下对学生在采样动作上进行优势加权，从而得到无偏监督，且不增加rollout或奖励评估。

Result: 在数学、编程与MedQA任务上，在外部教师蒸馏和自蒸馏两种设定下，AOPD较蒸馏基线及RL基线均取得性能提升，同时保持标准OPD的rollout效率。

Conclusion: 以反事实优势重加权教师监督可使在线蒸馏聚焦于真正帮助当前学生的动作，且可通过重要性加权实现零额外采样成本；该思路在多种任务与教师设定下有效。

Abstract: Online Policy Distillation (OPD) often outperforms offline methods such as Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) by training on trajectories sampled directly from the student. However, standard OPD trains the student to follow the teacher at every state of the trajectory, assuming that teacher guidance is always beneficial. We instead distinguish between what the teacher prefers and what actually helps the current student succeed. We introduce Advantage-Based On-Policy Distillation (AOPD), which weights teacher actions by their counterfactual advantage under the student. Teacher actions that improve the student's expected outcome are reinforced, while those that do not are downweighted.
  Directly computing these advantages requires trying alternative teacher actions at each step and running new rollouts from each. Instead, we show that the AOPD objective can be estimated using only standard student rollouts and their terminal rewards. Our estimator uses teacher importance weights on the student-sampled actions to provide unbiased advantage-weighted supervision, without any additional rollouts or reward evaluations. This allows AOPD to focus teacher supervision on actions that help the current student while preserving the rollout efficiency of standard OPD. Experiments on math, coding, and Medqa show gains over distillation and RL baselines in both external-teacher and self-distillation settings.

</details>


### [106] [On the Token Collapse Phenomenon in Textual Graph Soft Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31572)
*Andrea Giuseppe Di Francesco,Rishabh Jain,Franco Maria Nardini,Nicola Tonellotto,Pietro Liò,Fabrizio Silvestri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文深入分析G-Retriever的图软提示机制，发现"token collapse"（图token塌缩）病理现象：graph token在不同输入间几乎不可区分，未传递图内容而仅起通用软提示作用；并提出两阶段流程与评估图token对齐性的实用方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图软提示（graph soft prompting）旨在用GNN学习捕捉实体间关系信息的图特定token，G-Retriever进一步结合同一图的言语化描述来增强文本提示信号。作者质疑这些图token是否真正编码并传递了图结构信息，还是仅充当无关的软提示，因此展开系统性分析。

Method: 在3个基准、3种图编码器、2个LLM家族、冻结与LoRA适配两类设置下检验G-Retriever的图token可区分性；开展替换实验（数据集均值token、跨样本token、损坏图token）；对比从不访问图的学习常数提示；通过干预与解码探针（interventions、decoding probes）判断token所反映的内容来源；并提出产生非塌缩token的两阶段流程。

Result: G-Retriever的graph token在各类配置下几乎不可区分（token collapse），该失效被后续方法（即使采用multi-token pooling）继承；替换token对答案级准确率无显著影响；常数提示在三个基准上匹配或超越G-Retriever；两阶段流程可产生非塌缩token并在多数情况下保持竞争性性能；解码探针显示token可能反映问题、答案先验或LLM未必使用的图衍生内容。

Conclusion: 塌缩的token并非传递图内容，而是充当通用软提示；token变异性本身不能证明模型使用了图信息。应通过token对齐评估（干预与探针）而非仅依赖任务准确率来推断图信息利用，作者给出可复现的实用评估流程。

Abstract: Recent methods for injecting graph structure into LLM prompts include graph soft prompting, in which a graph neural network (GNN) learns graph-specific tokens that capture relational information among entities in textual prompts. G-Retriever combines graph soft prompts with a verbalized description of the same graph to strengthen the textual prompt signal. We conduct an in-depth analysis of G-Retriever and uncover a pathological behavior we term token collapse. Across three benchmarks, three graph encoders, two LLM families, and both frozen and LoRA-adapted models, G-Retriever's graph tokens become almost indistinguishable across inputs. We provide evidence that the same failure is inherited by subsequent methods, even with multi-token pooling. Under the standard setup, swapping a token with the dataset mean, a token from another example, or one derived from a corrupted graph produces no resolved answer-level effect in accuracy. Moreover, a learned constant prompt that never accesses the graph matches or surpasses G-Retriever on all three benchmarks. These findings indicate that, rather than conveying graph content as intended, the collapsed token functions primarily as a generic soft prompt. We also examine conditions linked to this collapse and propose a two-stage procedure that yields non-collapsed tokens while maintaining competitive performance in most cases. However, token variability alone does not prove that the model uses graph information: via interventions and decoding probes, we show that depending on the configuration, a token can reflect the question, an answer prior, or graph-derived content that the LLM may or may not use. Overall, our results offer a practical approach to evaluating graph-token alignment rather than inferring it from task accuracy alone. Code to reproduce our experiments is available at https://github.com/difra100/Token_Collapse_KGQA.

</details>


### [107] [Rethinking Sequence Compression with Test-time Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31574)
*Sihan Yang,Jie Huang,Hanxuan Li,Yifan Guo,Weigao Sun,Yiran Zhong,Weiyang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TTR-Compressor，将序列压缩重新表述为测试时回归问题：输入token定义回归问题，其闭式解系数作为压缩表示，回归目标端到端可学，在前向中高效闭式求解，作为池化/投影压缩的通用替代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列压缩是高效语言与多模态模型的关键信息瓶颈，但现有基于池化或学习投影的方法性能仍不理想。

Method: 以测试时回归视角重构压缩：输入token构造回归问题，拟合系数构成压缩表示；回归问题通过反向传播端到端学习，系数在前向传播中以闭式解高效计算。在两类场景验证：块稀疏注意力中的压缩器、多模态大模型的视觉token合并器。

Result: 块稀疏注意力上，TTR-Compressor在RULER上大幅优于基于注意力的压缩器；视觉token合并上，持续优于标准MLP合并器，并以少55.6%的视觉token达到同等性能。

Conclusion: 测试时回归可作为高效语言与多模态模型序列压缩的有效基本原语。

Abstract: Sequence compression is a fundamental operation and a critical information bottleneck in efficient language and multimodal models, and yet prevailing approaches based on pooling or learned projections remain underperforming. We revisit sequence compression through the lens of test-time regression: input tokens define a regression problem whose fitted coefficients form the compressed representation. Importantly, the regression problem is learned end-to-end via backpropagation, while its coefficients are computed efficiently in closed form during the forward pass. This leads to TTR-Compressor, a general alternative to conventional pooling and projection-based compression. We evaluate TTR-Compressor as the compressor for block-wise sparse attention and the visual token merger for multimodal large language models. On block-wise sparse attention, TTR-Compressor improves RULER performance over attention-based compressors by a large margin. For visual token merging, it consistently outperforms the standard MLP merger and matches its performance with 55.6% fewer visual tokens. These results establish test-time regression as an effective primitive for sequence compression in efficient language and multimodal models.

</details>


### [108] [Action Learning for Scientific Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31585)
*Sarah Liaw,Yuanqi Du,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出“动作学习”（action learning）问题——从轨迹观测中恢复随机作用量，并给出 LEAP（Learned Energy on Action Paths）方法：将未知作用量锚定于可解的参考过程，通过比较观测轨迹与参考轨迹学习修正项，从而在路径空间上得到可微目标，可捕捉局部动力学结构与跨时间的非可加依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解并控制系统演化需要基于完整轨迹进行推理，但现有方法存在局限：学习局部动力学或以单个转移构造的目标会忽略跨时间依赖；生成式轨迹模型不必然提供可评估与优化任意路径的可解目标。因此需要一个统一、可处理的轨迹级目标。

Method: 提出 action learning：从轨迹观测恢复随机作用量。LEAP 将未知作用量锚定到一个可处理的参考过程（tractable reference process），通过比较观测轨迹与参考轨迹学习剩余修正项，在路径空间上构造可微目标，从而同时刻画局部动力学结构与非可加的跨时间依赖。学到的同一作用量可复用于路径排序与推断、机制引导、约束优化与采样。

Result: 在随机物理系统、多体轨道动力学与分子模拟中，LEAP 能恢复潜在动力学、捕捉跨时间依赖，并在不同推断目标与物理条件之间实现迁移。

Conclusion: action learning 是将轨迹观测转化为可复用目标的有前景框架，可用于科学推断与控制。

Abstract: Understanding and controlling how systems evolve is central to science and engineering, where many problems require reasoning about complete trajectories. Existing approaches typically learn local dynamics or generate trajectories, but objectives constructed from individual transitions can miss dependencies across time, while generative models do not necessarily provide a tractable objective for evaluating and optimizing arbitrary paths. We study $\textit{action learning}$, the problem of recovering a stochastic action from trajectory observations, yielding a common objective for reasoning about dynamical systems. We introduce $\textbf{LEAP}$ ($\textbf{L}$earned $\textbf{E}$nergy on $\textbf{A}$ction $\textbf{P}$aths), which anchors the unknown action to a tractable reference process and learns the remaining correction by comparing observed trajectories with reference trajectories. This construction yields a differentiable objective on path space that can capture both local dynamical structure and nonadditive dependencies across time. Once learned, the same action can be flexibly reused for path ranking and inference, mechanism steering, constrained optimization and sampling. Across stochastic physical systems, many-body orbital dynamics and molecular simulations, LEAP recovers underlying dynamics, captures dependencies across time and enables transfer across inference objectives and physical conditions. We show that $\textit{action learning}$ is a promising framework for turning trajectory observations into reusable objectives for scientific inference and control.

</details>


### [109] [CRANE: Post-Hoc Counterfactual Retrieval for Multimodal Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31594)
*Franco Rugolon,Ioanna Miliou,Panagiotis Papapetrou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CRANE：首个后验、模型无关的多模态反事实解释框架，通过检索真实训练样本生成候选解释以保证合理性；其中MC-R（多模态共识检索）在四个数据集中的三个取得最佳或并列最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实解释方法仅面向单模态（表格、时序、文本、图像），而多模态分类器缺乏专门的后验解释方法；现有多模态可解释性方法对每种模态独立做归因，既难以产生连贯解释，也无法迁移到任意预训练多模态架构。

Method: CRANE 采用模型无关的最近邻检索式反事实生成：直接从目标类别的真实训练样本中检索候选作为解释，从而在构造上保证解释的合理性。核心策略 MC-R（Multimodal Consensus Retrieval）对各模态分别检索邻居集合并取交集，从而找到在每一模态上都与查询样本接近的实例。

Result: 在四个异构数据集与三种融合策略上进行评估：MC-R 在四分之三的数据集上取得最佳或并列最佳，且每条解释都锚定于联合训练分布；当某一模态主导决策边界时，单模态策略仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: CRANE 填补了多模态分类器反事实解释的空白，提供了首个后验、模型无关的检索式框架；MC-R 是整体最强的多模态策略，而策略选择应视模态主导性而定。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanation methods have been introduced for unimodal settings, with dedicated frameworks for tabular, timeseries, text, and image data, yet no post-hoc method has been designed for classifiers that learn from multimodal inputs. Existing explainability methods for multimodal classifiers apply modality-specific attribution independently to each input, which fails to yield coherent explanations and does not transfer to arbitrary pre-trained multimodal architectures. To address these shortcomings, we present CRANE (Counterfactual Retrieval via Agnostic Nearest-neighbour Explanations), the first post-hoc, model-agnostic framework for nearest-neighbour multimodal counterfactual generation that retrieves real training examples from the target class as candidate explanations, ensuring plausibility by construction. Across four heterogeneous datasets and three fusion strategies, we show that Multimodal Consensus Retrieval (MC-R), which intersects per-modality neighbour sets to identify instances jointly close to the query in every modality, emerges as the strongest multimodal strategy, achieving the best or joint-best outcome on three of four datasets while grounding every explanation in the joint training distribution. Unimodal strategies remain competitive when a single modality dominates the decision boundary.

</details>


### [110] [Efficient and Optimal Learning of Discrete Distributions with Person-Level Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31612)
*Gautam Kamath,Mahbod Majid,Ankur Moitra,Argyris Mouzakis,Jonathan Ullman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究批处理（batched）设置下、用户级差分隐私约束的离散分布估计问题。给出纯DP与近似DP下的多项式时间算法，纯DP样本量达到对数因子内匹配信息论最优的界；核心技术是新的6次平方和（SoS）估计器及其迹有界对角majorizer证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在用户级差分隐私下，每个用户贡献多个样本（batched），需以总变差误差α估计[\(k\)]上的分布μ。既有结果或非多项式时间、或未达最优样本复杂度，尤其在纯DP与极小δ的近似DP下存在空白，作者希望给出多项式时间、接近信息论最优的算法。

Method: 提出用于从不可信批次中学习的6次平方和（degree-6 SoS）估计器。关键构件是：对“修复后批次均值的经验协方差”与“由均值预测的多项式协方差”之差，构造一个迹有界对角majorizer。通过半正定规划对偶性，并借助Friedland与Lim给出的更强对称形式Grothendieck不等式，证明干净数据满足该证书。证书保留多项式批次均值的均值相关方差，从而得到近最优鲁棒速率O(η√(log(1/η)/m))，并提供隐私归约所需的score。另证一个统计查询（SQ）下界。

Result: 纯(ε,0)-DP：n = Õ(k/(α²m) + k/(α√m·ε) + k/ε)，为首个在多项式时间内达到（对数因子内）信息论最优的纯DP保证。近似(ε,δ)-DP：n = Õ(k/(α²m) + k/(α√m·ε) + k + log(1/δ)/ε)；当δ=2^{-Θ(k)}时，对固定α,ε,m及失败概率达到Õ(k)。鲁棒速率为O(η√(log(1/η)/m))。SQ下界表明在固定多项式SQ资源约束下无法实现均匀更小的误差阶。

Conclusion: 本文在批处理用户级DP离散分布估计上取得接近信息论最优的多项式时间结果，其6次SoS估计器与迹有界对角majorizer证书（结合对称Grothendieck不等式）是核心贡献；SQ下界刻画了统计查询模型下的内在限制，但算法与下界在模型间存在差距。

Abstract: We study learning a discrete distribution under person-level differential privacy in the batched setting. Given $n$ users, each contributing $m$ i.i.d. samples from an unknown distribution $μ$ over $[k]$, we seek an estimate of $μ$ with total-variation error $α$. We give polynomial-time algorithms under both pure and approximate differential privacy. Under pure $(\varepsilon,0)$-DP, we achieve $n = \widetilde O(k/(α^2 m)+k/(α\sqrt{m}\varepsilon)+k/\varepsilon)$, the first polynomial-time pure-DP guarantee matching the information-theoretic optimum up to logarithmic factors. Under approximate $(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP, we achieve $n = \widetilde O(k/(α^2 m)+k/(α\sqrt{m}\varepsilon)+k+\log(1/δ)/\varepsilon)$. At $δ=2^{-Θ(k)}$, this is $\widetilde O(k)$ for fixed $α,\varepsilon,m$ and failure probability. Our algorithms use a new degree-6 sum-of-squares estimator for learning from untrusted batches. Its key ingredient is a trace-bounded diagonal majorizer for the difference between the empirical covariance of the repaired batch averages and the multinomial covariance predicted by their mean. We prove that clean data admit this certificate through semidefinite-programming duality and a new application of the stronger symmetric form of Grothendieck's inequality due to Friedland and Lim. The certificate preserves the mean-dependent variance of multinomial batch averages, yielding the near-optimal robust rate $O(η\sqrt{\log(1/η)/m})$ and providing the score required by the privacy reduction. We also prove a statistical query lower bound ruling out a uniformly smaller order of error under fixed polynomial SQ resource bounds.

</details>


### [111] [OP-Print: Tracing Membership Imprints in On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31616)
*Qi Li,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出首个针对on-policy自蒸馏的成员推断攻击研究，揭示"监督印记"稀疏且集中于不确定性敏感修正点的特性，据此构建统一攻击框架OP-Print，在灰盒与黑盒设置下均有效，而所有基线攻击即使在灰盒下也仅相当于随机猜测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: on-policy蒸馏缓解了off-policy蒸馏的分布不匹配问题，而无教师的on-policy自蒸馏（借助额外上下文或示范增强学生自身输出作为监督）虽实用，但其隐私风险几乎未被探索；尤其同一训练样本的两个耦合视图可能加剧记忆化，因而需要系统评估其成员推断风险。

Method: 构建统一攻击框架OP-Print，基于"监督印记在on-policy自蒸馏中稀疏且结构化、集中于不确定性敏感修正位点而非整段rollout"的发现，设计两种访问级别下的攻击信号：灰盒设置下沿同一rollout直接聚合特权教师-学生残差修正；黑盒设置下以基于重启的输出盆地稳定性作为隐藏修正过程的可观测代理。

Result: 在多种模型与蒸馏方案上，所有基线攻击即使在灰盒访问下也基本停留在随机猜测水平，而OP-Print在灰盒与黑盒设置中均被广泛验证为有效。

Conclusion: on-policy自蒸馏虽免除了外部教师，但存在可被利用的成员隐私泄露；稀疏结构化的监督印记为攻击提供了关键信号，且在受限黑盒条件下仍可近似观测，表明该训练范式需纳入隐私防护考量。

Abstract: On-policy distillation has been widely adopted for alleviating the distribution mismatch of off-policy distillation. Self-distillation further removes the need for an external teacher by using the student's own outputs, enhanced with extra context or demonstrations, as supervision. This teacher-free design makes on-policy self-distillation practical, yet its privacy risks remain largely unexplored. In particular, using two coupled views of the same training example may strengthen its memorization. Motivated by this, we present the first study of membership inference attacks against on-policy self-distillation. We investigate both gray-box and black-box threat models and propose a unified attack framework, named OP-Print. OP-Print builds on our finding that the supervision imprint in on-policy self-distillation is sparse and structured, concentrating on uncertainty-sensitive correction sites rather than across the entire rollout. Exploiting this property, we construct attack signals tailored to the two access levels: in the gray-box setting, OP-Print directly aggregates the privileged teacher-student residual correction along the same rollout; in the black-box setting, it measures restart-based output-basin stability as an observable surrogate for the hidden correction process. Across diverse models and distillation schemes, all baseline attacks remain essentially at random guessing even under gray-box access, whereas OP-Print is broadly validated to be effective in both gray-box and black-box settings.

</details>


### [112] [Questioning Instrumental Convergence in RLVR: Checkpoint Evidence from OLMo-3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31624)
*Barış Alparslan,Sandy Tanwisuth,Caleb Petterson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文以轨迹视角研究RLVR是否加剧工具性收敛行为：OLMo-3-7B-Think在SFT时工具性行为率为25.0%，RLVR约200步升至46.1%，最终回落至11.8%，呈非单调变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具性收敛常被视为能力型智能体追求自我保护、资源获取、欺骗与规避监督等子目标的普遍理由，对安全构成威胁；RL是后训练中直接针对目标优化行为的阶段，因而最可能出现此类行为。作者欲检验可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）是否使模型更倾向表现工具性收敛行为，还是取决于训练制度、评估框架与后训练阶段。

Method: 将问题建模为以检查点为条件的行为轨迹，而非单一的前后安全评分对比。使用InstrumentalEval评估OLMo-3-7B-Think，覆盖SFT、DPO、13个中间RLVR检查点及最终RLVR模型；采用配对条目级检验与逐步回归；并以有害提示指示变量加其他框架效应、以及迭代零空间投影（INLP）剥离框架方向，检验框架解释力；另报告32B端点的初步结果。

Result: 轨迹非单调：SFT的25.0%升至RLVR约200步的46.1%，最终检查点降至11.8%；配对条目级检验与逐步回归支持SFT到最终RLVR的下降。提示框架无法解释该模式：有害提示指示变量与其他框架效应平均仅解释检查点内得分方差的15%，INLP去除可检测的框架方向后SFT到最终RLVR的差距不变。32B端点初步结果为35.53%（SFT）降至22.37%（RLVR最终）。

Conclusion: 在该设定下RLVR并未单调提升工具性行为：早期上升，随后降至低于SFT基线。结论限于该模型族与基准，且评判者一致性中等，绝对比率应谨慎解读。

Abstract: Instrumental convergence is often invoked as a general reason to expect capable agents to acquire or express power-seeking subgoals: self-preservation, resource acquisition, deception, or resistance to oversight which poses an important threat to the society. For LLMs, reinforcement learning is the first stage of post-training that directly optimizes behavior against an objective; therefore, it is the stage where convergent instrumental behavior, on IC grounds, likely to appear. Does reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) make a model more likely to express instrumental-convergence behavior, or does this depend on the training regime, evaluation frame, and stage of post-training? We study this question as a checkpoint-conditioned behavioral trajectory rather than as a single before/after safety score. Using InstrumentalEval, we evaluate OLMo-3-7B-Think across SFT, DPO, 13 intermediate RLVR checkpoints, and the final RLVR model. The trajectory is nonmonotonic: instrumental rate rises from 25.0% at SFT to 46.1% near RLVR step 200, then falls to 11.8% at the final checkpoint. Paired item-level tests and step-wise regression support the final SFT-to-RLVR reduction. Prompt framing does not explain this pattern: a harmful-prompt indicator combined with other framing effects explains only 15% of within-checkpoint score variance on average, and Iterative Null-space Projection (INLP) removes a detectable framing direction without changing the SFT-to-final-RLVR gap. Together with preliminary 32B endpoint results (35.53% SFT to 22.37% RLVR final), these findings indicate that RLVR does not monotonically increase instrumental behavior in this setting: the rate rises early, then falls below the SFT baseline. The result is specific to this model family and benchmark, and judge agreement is moderate, so absolute rates should be read cautiously.

</details>


### [113] [BeNice! Closing Looping's Inference-Memory Gap via KV-Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31633)
*Hayden Prairie,Jacob M. Ryan,Taylor Berg-Kirkpatrick,Daniel Y. Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究循环（looped）架构中 KV-cache 的跨递归共享问题，提出两阶段方法（预训练阶段使用随机深度诱导收缩性残差动力学 + 后训练方法 BeNice!），使模型在保持质量的前提下实现 KV 共享，并与多种 KV 压缩技术互补，显著提升长上下文推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环架构通过重复传递激活来扩展质量而不增加参数，但推理时每次递归产生独立 KV-cache，导致显存线性增长。已有工作提出的 KV 共享（跨深度共享 KV-cache）在某些情况下可行，但机制不明，甚至同样训练的模型也不稳定具备该性质，因此需研究如何在循环模型中可靠实现 KV-shareability。

Method: 提出两阶段方法。第一阶段：预训练时采用随机深度（stochastic depth），诱导收缩性残差动力学，使各次递归的表示趋向共同极限。第二阶段：提出后训练方法 BeNice!，将最后一次递归的 KV-cache 广播到第一次递归，训练其产生兼容的残差，同时保持原模型质量。

Result: BeNice! 实现了 KV-shareability，且不共享时的验证困惑度与标准微调持平。KV 共享与替代性 KV 压缩方法互补，可与内存高效序列混合器（如 multi-latent attention、Kimi delta attention）及低精度 KV-cache 量化（fp8、mxfp8、nvfp4）组合。在模拟低显存长上下文推理中，缓存命中率提升至多 31.4%，吞吐提升至多 23.6%，p90 首 token 时延降低至多 83.6%。

Conclusion: 随机深度诱导的收缩动力学是 KV-shareability 的关键使能机制，BeNice! 可在不损失质量下实现 KV 共享，并与现有 KV 压缩手段正交组合，为循环架构的内存高效长上下文推理提供可行方案。

Abstract: Looped architectures scale quality while keeping parameter count fixed by repeatedly passing activations through the same block of layers. During inference, each recurrence generates independent KV-caches, causing memory to grow linearly. To address this challenge, prior work has proposed KV-sharing (i.e., sharing KV-caches across depth), finding that, in some cases, the KV-cache of the final recurrence can be shared with the earlier recurrences without quality loss, a property we denote as KV-shareability. However, the mechanisms underlying KV-shareability remain poorly understood, with even identically trained looped architectures not consistently exhibiting it. In this paper, we study how KV-shareability can be enabled in looped models. We propose a two-stage method: in the first stage, we use stochastic depth during pretraining, which induces contractive residual dynamics that drive representations toward a common limit across recurrences. In the second stage, we propose BeNice!, a post-training method that broadcasts the final recurrence's KV-cache into the first recurrence, training it to produce compatible residuals while preserving the original model's quality. As a result, BeNice! enables KV-shareability while matching standard finetuning's unshared validation perplexity. Using BeNice!, we show that KV-sharing is complementary to alternative KV-compression methods, composing with memory-efficient sequence mixers (e.g., multi-latent attention, Kimi delta attention) and low-precision KV-cache quantization (e.g., fp8, mxfp8, nvfp4). Finally, in simulated low-memory, long-context inference, KV-sharing improves cache hit rates by up to 31.4%, increases throughput by up to 23.6%, and reduces p90 time-to-first-token by up to 83.6%.

</details>


### [114] [Causal Chain of Thought: Mitigating Attention Leakage via Context Isolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31639)
*Gabriele Dominici,Teague McMillan,Fazl Barez,Marc Langheinrich,Martin Gjoreski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CausalCoT 推理框架，通过将问题分解为原子子问题组成的 DAG，并在仅含直接因果父节点的隔离上下文中逐一求解，使推理步骤真正因果驱动最终答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT prompting 生成的逐步推理看似解释了预测，但无法保证这些步骤因果地驱动最终答案。标准自回归生成中密集注意力使每一步可访问完整上下文，模型可能通过直接查表得出答案，再生成貌似合理的后验辩护。

Method: CausalCoT 将问题分解为原子子问题构成的有向无环图，每个节点仅在其直接因果父节点构成的隔离上下文中评估。这种马尔可夫结构切断了指向非父内容的注意力通路，使下游预测依赖于局部推理而非全局上下文泄漏；依赖图还支持测试时干预，局部修正可沿下游可预测地传播。

Result: 在多样化推理基准上，CausalCoT 保持有竞争力的预测性能，同时提升解释忠实性，中间推理步骤的因果敏感性相比最强基线平均提升 12 个百分点，最高达 21 个百分点。

Conclusion: 以因果图加隔离上下文评估的结构化推理范式，可在不牺牲性能的前提下显著增强 CoT 的解释忠实性与可干预性，为可信推理提供可行路径。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting elicits step-by-step rationales that appear to explain model predictions, but there is no guarantee these steps causally drive the final answer. In standard autoregressive generation, dense attention allows each reasoning step to attend to the full context, enabling the model to derive answers through direct lookup while generating plausible-sounding but post-hoc justifications. We introduce CausalCoT, an inference framework that enforces causal transparency by decomposing problems into a directed acyclic graph of atomic sub-questions, evaluating each node in an isolated context containing only its direct causal parents. This Markovian structure removes attention pathways to non-parent content, making downstream predictions sensitive to local reasoning rather than global context leakage. Across a diverse suite of reasoning benchmarks, CausalCoT preserves competitive predictive performance while improving explanation faithfulness, increasing causal sensitivity of intermediate reasoning steps by an average of 12 and up to 21 percentage points over the strongest baseline. The structured dependency graph additionally enables test-time interventions in which local corrections propagate predictably through downstream reasoning.

</details>


### [115] [A Fix for Softmax and Cross-Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31642)
*Jonathan T. Barron,Dan Gnanapragasam,Michael Tschannen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 指出 softmax/log-softmax/交叉熵标准实现中的两类数值 bug（Softmax Collapse 与 Softmax Cancellation），给出简单修正实现，并展示其消除 Slingshot 现象、加速 grokking、防止精度崩塌、稳定蒸馏等效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 softmax、log-softmax 与交叉熵实现在数值上存在两个缺陷：其一，当目标 logit 远大于其他 logit 时，其梯度被舍入为零，导致梯度不再零和；其二，当学生 logit 接近教师 logit（甚至相等）时仍产生非零梯度。这些缺陷会引发训练中观察到的一系列异常现象，因此需要从实现层面加以修正。

Method: 作者分析两类数值 bug 的成因，提出 log-softmax、softmax 与交叉熵的简单修正实现，使目标 logit 梯度不被截断为 0、并保证学生与教师 logit 相等时梯度为零。随后在多个训练场景（grokking、权重衰减/无权重衰减、知识蒸馏、记忆后 logit 增长）中验证修正前后的行为差异。

Result: 修正实现消除了此前观察到的“Slingshot”现象；使带权重衰减的 grokking 加速；在无权重衰减时防止精度崩塌；阻止蒸馏中学生网络偏离教师；并表明记忆完成后 logit 的增长由权重衰减与 Adam 的 ε 超参数之间的平衡所界定。

Conclusion: 标准 softmax 与交叉熵实现中的数值 bug 会实质影响训练动力学与蒸馏稳定性，简单且正确的实现即可修复；同时 logit 增长的上界可由权重衰减与 Adam ε 的均衡刻画。

Abstract: Standard implementations of softmax, log-softmax, and cross-entropy have two numerical bugs. The first bug, Softmax Collapse, rounds the target logit's gradient to zero if that one logit is much larger than the others, resulting in gradients that no longer sum to zero. The second bug, Softmax Cancellation, occurs when student logits are close to teacher logits, and produces non-zero gradients even when the student and teacher logits are equal. We present simple implementations of log-softmax, softmax, and cross-entropy that fix both bugs. Our fix eliminates the previously-observed "Slingshot" phenomenon, speeds up grokking with weight decay, prevents accuracy collapse without weight decay, stops student networks from drifting away from the teacher during distillation, and shows that after memorization, logit growth is bounded by an equilibrium between weight decay and Adam's $ε$ hyperparameter.

</details>


### [116] [METTA: Memory-Enhanced Test-Time Adaptation for Meta-Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31656)
*Ali Najar,Hosein Hasani,Amir Kooshan Fattah,Yasamin Rajabi,Mahdieh Soleymani Baghshah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出METTA，将元强化学习的快速适应分解为因果神经记忆（情节内细粒度、线性复杂度）与情节级摘要（长程跨情节任务推断），实现高效长上下文元强化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 元强化学习需在长交互历史中表征任务证据，但经典循环智能体将经验压缩进隐状态，Transformer类智能体则以二次内存与计算代价检索过去轨迹，效率受限。

Method: 将快速适应阶段解耦为两个时间尺度：因果神经记忆在情节内进行细粒度适应，序列复杂度为线性；同时用紧凑的情节级摘要支持跨情节长程任务推断，避免对完整交互历史做注意力计算。神经记忆还赋予智能体充足表达力与测试时训练能力。

Result: 在未见过的Meta-World ML10/45测试环境中成功率超过50%，且训练预算低、墙钟时间短；在长上下文稀疏奖励PointMaze任务上优于Transformer基线，表现出更具结构化的探索行为。

Conclusion: METTA为更具适应性与可扩展性的长上下文元强化学习提供了一条高效路径。

Abstract: Meta-reinforcement learning aims to train agents that adapt quickly to unseen tasks from limited interaction, but this requires representing task evidence over long interaction histories. Classical recurrent agents compress experience into hidden states, while transformer-based agents retrieve from past trajectories at quadratic memory and computation cost. In this work, we propose Memory-Enhanced Test-Time Adaptation (METTA) for meta-reinforcement learning. It decomposes the fast adaptation phase into two temporal scales: a causal neural memory performs fine-grained within-episode adaptation with linear sequence complexity, while compact episode-level summaries support long-range cross-episode task inference without attending over the full interaction history. Moreover, equipping agents with neural memory provides sufficient expressive capacity and test-time training capability, enabling effective adaptation to unseen held-out tasks and task families at meta-test time. On unseen held-out Meta-World ML10/45 test environments, METTA achieves a success rate over $50\%$ while maintaining a low training budget and short wall-clock time. On long-context sparse-reward PointMaze tasks, it outperforms transformer-based baselines by exhibiting more structured exploration behavior. These results show that METTA provides an efficient path toward more adaptive and scalable long-context meta-RL.

</details>


### [117] [Cost-Aware Abstention for LLM-Based IT Ticket Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31657)
*Sachin Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将成本感知弃权作为离线、成本条件的就绪性评估：在校准集上按显式成本选策略，冻结后仅在留出测试集上评估一次。在47,837条工单、八类公开语料上，比较4个非LLM分类器与4个LLM（零/少样本），TF-IDF+逻辑回归在阈值0.60下把测试成本从0.763降至0.472（相对降38.1%，95% CI [35.7%,40.4%]），错误路由率从15.3%降至3.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动路由错误（错分IT支持工单）的代价可能高于人工分诊，因此需要按显式成本评估“是否弃权/转人工”的部署就绪性，而非仅追求准确率或ECE。

Method: 成本感知弃权的离线评估框架：在校准数据上依成本选路由策略，冻结后单次测试。对比4个训练型非LLM分类器与4个LLM（零样本/少样本），采用示例成本(c_ok,c_triage,c_wrong)=(0,1,5)，结合阈值选择、等渗校准、margin/熵拒绝基线及LLM-only一致性对照；指标包括成本、错误路由率、正确性AUROC、tie-aware risk-coverage面积与ECE，并做事后成本参数敏感性分析（16种设定）。

Result: TF-IDF+LR在0.60阈值下测试成本0.763→0.472（相对降38.1%，配对ratio-bootstrap 95% CI [35.7%,40.4%]），错误路由15.3%→3.5%；冻结的校准策略在14/16设定中优于always-route与always-defer。最强提示配置在1,000条分层子集上准确率0.477，微调DistilBERT同子集为0.879；等渗降低ECE但不稳定提升判别力（GPT-4o-mini少样本重校准前后仅一个置信值，正确性AUROC=0.500）。在解析得出的盈亏平衡概率0.80处，7个提示配置全部弃权，Haiku少样本仅路由19/1,000。

Conclusion: 框架有效区分“校准”与“判别”：应同时报告正确性AUROC与tie-aware risk-coverage面积，并以成本条件策略选择作为弃权部署前评估。结论受限于该数据集、提示设计、模型端点与示例成本，属评估框架而非新弃权算法或通用部署认证。

Abstract: Wrong automatic routing of an IT support ticket can cost more than human triage. We evaluate cost-aware abstention as an offline, cost-conditional readiness assessment: select a routing policy on calibration data under explicit costs, then evaluate that frozen policy once on held-out test data. On a public 47,837-ticket, eight-class corpus, we compare four trained non-LLM classifiers with four large language models under zero- and few-shot prompting. Under the illustrative cost model (c_ok, c_triage, c_wrong) = (0, 1, 5), TF-IDF with logistic regression and a threshold of 0.60 selected on calibration reduces test cost from 0.763 to 0.472, a 38.1% relative reduction (paired ratio-bootstrap 95% CI [35.7%, 40.4%]), while lowering wrong routes from 15.3% to 3.5%. Frozen calibration-selected policies also beat both always-route and always-defer on held-out test data in 14 of 16 settings in a post-hoc cost-parameter analysis. The strongest prompted configuration reaches 0.477 accuracy on a stratified 1,000-ticket test subset, versus 0.879 for fine-tuned DistilBERT on the same tickets. Isotonic lowers expected calibration error without reliably improving discrimination: GPT-4o-mini few-shot has one confidence value before and after recalibration and correctness AUROC 0.500. We therefore report correctness AUROC and tie-aware risk-coverage area alongside ECE. At the analytically derived break-even probability 0.80, seven prompted configurations defer all tickets; Haiku few-shot routes only 19/1,000. An LLM-only agreement control and margin/entropy rejection baselines further separate calibration from discrimination. The findings are scoped to this dataset, prompt design, model endpoints, and illustrative costs; the work is an evaluation framework, not a new abstention algorithm or universal deployment certification.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [The Cost of Saying Hello: An Empirical Study of Handshake Overhead in AI Agent Communication Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30151)
*Pranav Varshney*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 首个对AI智能体协议握手阶段进行实证、分阶段分解的基准测试：对MCP（2025 initialize与2026 discover两个版本）、A2A、ACP用官方SDK插桩测量往返次数、各阶段延迟、线上字节与token成本，发现往返次数主导握手开销，且元数据被LLM阅读时成本随能力数量线性扩张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体间每次交互均以握手开始（定位对端、了解能力、建立会话），协议综述虽将握手开销列为效率指标却无人给出实测数据，存在测量空白。

Method: 基于三个协议官方SDK对每个请求插桩，统计往返次数、分阶段延迟、wire bytes与token成本；覆盖冷启动、热重复、能力不匹配、元数据降级四类场景；每格N=50，在loopback与模拟50 ms RTT两种网络条件下测试。

Result: 往返次数主导握手成本：legacy MCP握手需3次往返、50 ms RTT下230 ms；A2A仅1次往返、61.0 ms。当LLM阅读对端元数据时，成本随能力清单规模扩张，约24–107 tokens/能力，200能力的对端在读取自身任务前已占用200k上下文窗口的11%。MCP 2026版将握手从3次往返降至2次，配合era pinning在热重连时降至0次。

Conclusion: 握手开销以往返次数为主导，但元数据token成本构成不可忽视的第二项账单；MCP协议自身的演进（往返数下降）印证了所测成本的真实性。作者公开完整测试框架、原始数据与分析。

Abstract: Every interaction between AI agents begins with a handshake: the initiating agent must locate its counterpart, learn what it can do, and establish a session before the first useful message can be sent. Surveys of agent communication protocols name handshake overhead as an efficiency criterion, yet none report measurements. This paper presents the first empirical, phase-decomposed benchmark of the handshake in three major agent protocols: MCP (in both its 2025 initialize and 2026 discover eras), A2A, and ACP. Using each protocol's official SDK, we instrument every request for round trips, per-phase latency, wire bytes, and token cost across cold-start, warm-repeat, capability-mismatch, and degraded-metadata scenarios (N=50 per cell, on loopback and under an emulated 50 ms RTT). Round-trip count dominates handshake cost. A legacy MCP handshake takes three round trips and 230 ms at 50 ms RTT; A2A needs one round trip and 61.0 ms. Round trips are not the whole bill: when an LLM reads the counterpart's metadata, handshake cost scales with the counterpart's capability inventory at 24-107 tokens per capability, so a 200-capability counterpart spends 11% of a 200k-token context window before the agent reads its own task. MCP's own 2026 revision cuts its handshake from three round trips to two and, with era pinning, to zero on warm reconnects, protocol evolution that corroborates the cost we measure. We release the full harness, raw data, and analysis at https://github.com/pvarshh/handshake-benchmark.

</details>


### [119] [MCP vs CLI for AI Agents: A Comparative Analysis of Tool Integration Strategies for Autonomous Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30157)
*Arun Malik,Nitish Talasu,Bradley Klemick,Vineet Tushar Trivedi,Prachi Shah,Sanay Devi,Junjie Zhang,Kunal Sonawane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对比分析 AI 智能体的两种外部系统交互范式——CLI 与 MCP，从 14 个维度、安全合规、经济模型进行评估，结论是 CLI 在单用户本地场景效率更优，MCP 满足 CLI 无法实现的细粒度管控需求，建议生产系统采用混合架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 智能体需要与文件、API、数据库、云服务等外部系统交互，目前存在两种竞争性范式：直接执行 shell 命令的 CLI 与提供受治理、强类型工具访问的结构化 JSON-RPC 协议 MCP。二者取舍缺乏系统性比较依据，本文旨在给出面向落地的对比分析。

Method: 构建 14 个评估维度的比较框架，辅以具体案例（worked examples）；依据既有合规框架（NSA 正式指南 CSI U/OO/6030316-26、OWASP 安全标准）开展安全评估；结合 BCG 与 Gartner 的经济模型进行成本效益分析；并考察渐进式披露（progressive disclosure）技术对 MCP 上下文窗口开销的影响。

Result: CLI 在单用户、纯本地工作流中效率占优；MCP 则解决 CLI 本质上无法满足的需求，包括细粒度访问控制、凭证隔离、结构化审计与多租户治理。渐进式披露可消除 MCP 历史上上下文窗口开销大的弱点，使其 token 效率与 CLI 相当，同时保留其架构优势。

Conclusion: CLI 与 MCP 并非简单替代关系，而是各有适用边界；生产系统推荐采用混合架构，按场景组合使用以兼顾效率与治理能力。

Abstract: AI agents require interfaces to interact with external systems such as files, APIs, databases, and cloud services. Two competing paradigms have emerged: CLI (Command Line Interface), where agents execute shell commands directly, and MCP (Model Context Protocol), a structured JSON-RPC protocol providing governed, typed tool access. This paper presents a comparative analysis across 14 evaluation dimensions, supported by worked examples, security assessment against established compliance frameworks, and economic modeling. Our analysis indicates that CLI provides superior efficiency for single-user, local-only workflows, while MCP addresses requirements that CLI fundamentally cannot satisfy, including fine-grained access control, credential isolation, structured audit, and multi-tenant governance. We further examine how progressive disclosure techniques eliminate MCP's historical weakness (context window overhead), yielding token efficiency comparable to CLI while preserving its architectural benefits. A hybrid architecture is recommended for production systems. This analysis draws upon academic, government, and industry sources including NSA formal guidance (CSI U/OO/6030316-26), OWASP security standards, and economic modeling from BCG and Gartner.

</details>


### [120] [Accelerating Wildfire Hazard Mapping Across Canada with an AI Surrogate Modelling Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30170)
*Hager Radi Abdelwahed,Toluwaleke Olutayo,Chirag Goel,Jana Osea,Aldo Zaimi,Tom Swystun,Sandy Erni,Francisco Emiliano Lopez Saavedra,Yan Boulanger,Gaétan Marceau Caron*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出用深度学习代理模型替代计算昂贵的基于过程的野火模拟器（BurnP3+），构建标准化训练评估框架并在全国尺度数据上训练多任务模型，以秒级速度近似烧毁概率、平均火强度和平均蔓延速率，实现806倍CPU加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加拿大野火活动加剧，亟需常态化国家尺度灾害制图以支撑燃料管理、土地利用规划、基础设施保护和疏散路径设计。但BurnP3/BurnP3+等基于过程的模拟框架依赖计算密集的蒙特卡洛方法，处理单个区域建模单元需数天至数周，形成计算瓶颈。

Method: AI研究者与野火建模专家合作提出三项贡献：(a) 标准化框架，用于训练、微调并对照过程式模拟器评估AI代理模型；(b) 基于全国尺度数据训练的多任务深度学习代理模型，融合燃料、地形、点火概率等地理空间数据及区域气象，同时模拟烧毁概率、平均火强度与平均蔓延速率；(c) 领域专用指标与反事实评估协议，用于检验分布外泛化能力。

Result: 模型在一致性、秩相关、误差和空间重叠等指标上表现优异，保留了下游灾害分类结果，并对专家设计的反事实干预产生物理上合理的响应。在代表性区域上实现端到端CPU加速806倍，主机内存占用仅为BurnP3+的13%。

Conclusion: 深度学习代理模型可在保持模拟精度与物理合理性的前提下大幅降低野火灾害制图的计算成本，支持快速情景分析，为主动式野火减缓决策提供可行工具。

Abstract: Canada's increasing wildfire activity requires frequent national hazard mapping to guide proactive mitigation, including fuel management, land-use planning, infrastructure protection, and evacuation routing. However, process-based wildfire simulation frameworks (e.g., BurnP3 and BurnP3+) rely on computationally intensive Monte Carlo simulations to model burn probability, fire intensity, and rate of spread. These workflows can take days to weeks to process a single regional modelling unit. To overcome these computational bottlenecks, we present three contributions developed through collaboration between AI researchers and wildfire modelling experts: (a) a standardized framework for training, fine-tuning, and evaluating AI surrogate models against process-based wildfire simulators; (b) a multi-task deep learning surrogate trained on national-scale data to emulate burn probability, mean fire intensity, and mean rate of spread at a fraction of the computational cost (seconds); and (c) domain-specific metrics and counterfactual evaluation protocols to probe out-of-distribution generalization. Integrating geospatial data (fuel, terrain, ignition likelihood) and regional weather, our model approximates BurnP3+ simulation outputs, achieving strong performance across agreement, rank-correlation, error, and spatial-overlap metrics. It preserves downstream hazard classifications and produces physically plausible responses to expert-informed counterfactual interventions. On a representative region, the surrogate achieves 806-fold end-to-end CPU acceleration and uses 13% of BurnP3+'s host memory, facilitating rapid scenario analysis.

</details>


### [121] [Neural Networks with Differentiable Belief-Function Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30173)
*Antonin Sulc*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于反向传播梯度中Dempster归一化因子(1−κ)⁻¹会引入(1−κ)⁻²发散项这一观察，提出用Yager 1987年版⊕组合规则构建深度证据网络，使链上所有mass均为合法D-S质量函数；输出m(Θ)可作为"我不知道"信号，在视觉与语言两种模态上对人类分歧排序，并在OOD检测上具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yager的⊕最初基于认知论理由提出：高冲突时应将质量重分配给无知（ignorance）而非在类单纯形上重归一化。作者指出反向传播同样需要这一性质——Dempster归一化(1−κ)⁻¹在梯度中产生(1−κ)⁻²项，恰在证据源冲突时发散；而Yager的⊕是双线性的、无此发散项。因此动机是从梯度稳定性角度为深度证据学习选择一个理论上更合适的组合规则。

Method: 构建端到端深度证据流水线：从多头（heads）到输出，链条中每个mass均为合法Dempster–Shafer质量函数，并统一用Yager⊕组合；以输出在无知集上的质量m(Θ)作为网络"我不知道"信号。网络仅用硬标签训练，从未接触标注者分布。与Dempster归一化版本对照，并在视觉（CIFAR-10H）和语言两种模态上评估m(Θ)对人类分歧的排序能力，同时考察OOD检测性能及梯度稳定性。

Result: m(Θ)在视觉与语言两种模态上均能对人类分歧进行排序；Dempster版本也上升且排序能力相近，但其归一化把冲突"除掉了"：在最受争议的CIFAR-10H图像上，Yager的vacuous质量比Dempster大9倍，故只有Yager版无需重校准即可被读作"无知量"。OOD检测上与强单次前向基线相当；评分更高但含clamp的Dempster变体在移除clamp后梯度不稳定。

Conclusion: 组合规则的选择不仅影响语义解释，也影响可训练性：Yager⊕因其双线性性质避免了冲突时的梯度发散，使m(Θ)成为校准良好、可直接解读的无知度量。含clamp的Dempster变体虽指标更高却依赖数值裁剪、牺牲稳定性。作者主张深度证据学习应默认采用Yager规则以求梯度稳定与无需重校准的可解释性。

Abstract: Yager's 1987 rule of combination $\boxplus$ was proposed on epistemic grounds: under high conflict, mass should be redistributed to ignorance rather than renormalised onto the class simplex. We observe that backpropagation needs the same rule: Dempster's normaliser $(1{-}κ)^{-1}$, where $κ$ is the conflict mass, puts a $(1{-}κ)^{-2}$ term in the gradient that diverges exactly when evidence sources disagree, while Yager's $\boxplus$ is bilinear and has no such term. Building on this, we construct a deep evidential pipeline in which every mass in the chain from heads to output is a valid Dempster--Shafer mass function, combined by Yager's rule. The output mass on the ignorance set, $m(Θ)$, serves as the network's ``I do not know'' signal. The network is fit to hard labels and never sees an annotator distribution, yet $m(Θ)$ orders human disagreement in two modalities, visual and linguistic. Dempster's rises too and ranks contested inputs about as well, but its normaliser divides the conflict out: Yager's vacuity is $9\times$ larger on the most contested CIFAR-10H images, so only Yager's is readable as an amount of ignorance without recalibration. For out-of-distribution detection it is competitive with strong single-pass baselines, while the clamped Dempster variant that scores higher becomes gradient-unstable once the clamp is removed.

</details>


### [122] [When Stable and Preferred Semantics Agree: A Path-Based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30176)
*Yan Zhang,Kehan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于攻击图中的路径集及其诱导的二元关系，给出偏好语义与稳定语义一致的路径型判据，统一并严格推广了文献中两条主要结构性判据（Dung有限争议性与SCC对称性），并构造两类无限路径集序列得到若干一致性条件及稳定外延存在性的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抽象论辩框架中偏好语义与稳定语义何时一致是核心问题，已有判据（Dung的limited controversiality、SCC-symmetry）较为零散且互不统摄，缺乏统一的、可扩展的结构性刻画框架。

Method: 选取攻击图中适当路径集，由路径诱导二元关系，建立语义一致的一般路径判据；以良基性表述充分条件，并用不同路径类进行实例化（如偶数路径集复原Dung结果）；进而引入两个无限路径集序列，分别考察Collapse Theorem与不塌缩情形。

Result: 所得条件严格推广limited controversiality与SCC-symmetry两条主要判据，Dung结果为偶数路径集的特殊情形；第一序列经Collapse Theorem将无穷多个良基性条件归约为三个本质不同条件，第二序列给出真正互异且无类似归约的条件；结论同时适用于有限与无限框架，有限情形可简化，并给出稳定外延存在的充分条件。

Conclusion: 路径集视角为偏好与稳定语义一致性提供了统一、可推广的结构性框架，涵盖并超越既有判据，且自然延伸至无限框架与稳定外延存在性问题。

Abstract: We investigate structural conditions under which preferred and stable semantics agree in abstract argumentation frameworks. Our approach is based on suitably chosen sets of paths in the attack graph and binary relations induced by them. We establish a general path-based criterion for semantic agreement and instantiate it with several classes of paths, obtaining sufficient conditions expressed in terms of well-foundedness of the corresponding relations.
  The resulting conditions strictly generalize two principal structural criteria for preferred and stable agreement in the literature: Dung's limited controversiality condition and the SCC-symmetry condition. In particular, Dung's result is recovered as a special case by taking the relevant path set to be the set of even paths. We further introduce two infinite sequences of path sets, each yielding a family of agreement conditions. For the first sequence, a Collapse Theorem reduces the infinitely many associated well-foundedness conditions to three essentially distinct ones, whereas the second sequence gives rise to genuinely distinct conditions with no analogous collapse. Our results apply to both finite and infinite argumentation frameworks, admit simplified formulations in the finite case, and also yield sufficient conditions for the existence of stable extensions.

</details>


### [123] [Feedback That Backfires in Games: Do LLM Players Repeat the Move They Just Watched Fail?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30205)
*Esmail Gumaan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文检验LLM智能体在游戏中失败后"把失败动作写入记录并要求重试"这一常见harness做法是否适得其反：在Sokoban、Connect Four、Othello的固定探针上，失败记录会提高模型重犯失败动作的概率（纠正增益G=-1.38 nats/token），且这种反转由动作的表面形式驱动，并可通过省略动作字符串或DPO阶段缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工具调用场景中，把失败动作与报错信息写回transcript会诱导小模型重复失败动作；作者想在可精确验证的游戏中检验这一"记录反噬"现象，并厘清其机制与训练阶段来源。

Method: 构造由扰动正确动作得到的固定探针；在8个指令微调模型（135M–1.7B）上测量纠正增益G（每token nats）；区分"非法动作"与"合法但致败动作"两类失败；做探索性回归分析记录后失败动作log-prob对其原先后验的依赖（斜率0.18）；对比省略动作字符串的失败描述、干净重试、"不要重复"指令、解码器禁止重复等harness变体；沿OLMo 2 1B后训练阶梯与RAGEN默认模型/格式考察反转来源；全部测量在免费Colab GPU上以float32进行，并先复现原文数值。

Result: 池化8个模型后记录反而使失败动作更可能（G=-1.38 nats/token），该效应通过动作表面形式起作用，仅出现在非法动作而非致败动作上，且并非所有模型都如此；记录后失败动作的log-prob几乎不依赖其先验（斜率0.18），本不会走的动作变可能，贪婪解码开始产出它们，原本将要走的动作变不可能但贪婪解码仍重复38%；省略动作字符串的失败描述保留了模型偏好；沿OLMo 2 1B阶梯，反转在base模型已存在、在DPO阶段基本消除；RAGEN默认模型在其自有Sokoban格式中在任何RL之前即出现反转；"不要重复"指令无效，干净重试是最差harness，解码器禁用仅阻止完全重复而不阻止非法走子。

Conclusion: 把失败动作写入transcript并重试是一种有害的harness设计：它通过表面形式诱导小模型重复失败动作，且难以用简单指令或解码约束修复；缓解方向是省略动作字符串的失败反馈，并注意base/RL前阶段已存在、DPO可部分消除这一反转。

Abstract: When an LLM agent's move fails, the harness writes the move and the failure message into the transcript and asks again. In tool calling, this record makes small models more likely to repeat the failed action. We test whether the same holds in games (Sokoban, Connect Four, Othello), where a move can fail by being illegal or by being legal but losing, both verified exactly. On fixed probe items built by perturbing a correct move, the record backfires: pooled over 8 instruction-tuned models (135M-1.7B) it makes the failed move more likely (corrective gain G = -1.38 nats per token), through the move's surface form, for illegal moves but not for losing ones, and not for every model. An exploratory analysis shows what sets the sign: after the record, the failed move's log-probability barely depends on how likely it was before (slope 0.18). Moves the model would not have played become more likely, and greedy decoding starts to produce them; moves it was about to play become less likely, but greedy decoding still repeats 38% of them. A description of the failure that omits the move string keeps the model's preference instead. Along the OLMo 2 1B post-training ladder the inversion is present in the base model and largely removed at the DPO stage. RAGEN's default models invert in RAGEN's own Sokoban format before any RL. A "do not repeat" instruction does nothing, a clean retry is the worst harness, and a decoder ban stops exact repeats but not illegal play. All measurements ran in float32 on a free Colab GPU, which first reproduced the original paper's number.

</details>


### [124] [Breaking the high-degree barrier in Lehmer's problem with a human-AI discovery loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30277)
*Yuri Chervonyi,Sergei Gukov,Thang Luong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出人-AI协作发现闭环，结合对话式提示、快速数值管线、精确符号验证与大规模分布式搜索，导出五参数"广义Boyd算法"，对每个目标偶数度构造Mahler测度严格低于Boyd极限点α₁≈1.255434的不可约整系数多项式，消除了Boyd族的度缺失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lehmer问题（1933）关注整系数多项式的Mahler测度能否任意小，其研究依赖大规模计算探索与解析综合揭示的结构。已有系统构造仅覆盖偶数度d≤180；更高次数上Boyd的单参数解析族常发生可约化，跳过近50%的目标度，缺乏对任意d的通用构造。

Method: 构建人-AI发现回路：AI代理在本地原型化数值与符号求解器，将对话式提示扩展为快速数值管线、精确符号验证器及分布式集群上的大规模并行搜索，并由人类研究者协同提取与验证数学假设，最终归纳出五参数广义Boyd算法。

Result: 对所有偶数目标度消除度跳过，构造出Mahler测度严格低于α₁≈1.255434的不可约多项式；在至2000的全部偶数度上评估，改进6个已发表数值，并保持99.6%的已知最优记录。

Conclusion: 五参数广义Boyd算法为任意目标度提供通用构造，并发布覆盖所有研究偶数度的最小Mahler测度多项式验证数据集。

Abstract: While artificial intelligence has made rapid progress in mathematical reasoning, many open problems depend on structure that is revealed only through large-scale computational exploration and analytical synthesis. One such example is Lehmer's problem from 1933. It asks whether the Mahler measure of an integer polynomial, which measures how far its roots stray from the unit circle, can be made arbitrarily small. Previously, constructing irreducible polynomials with integer coefficients and small Mahler measure has been carried out systematically only for even degrees $d \le 180$. At higher degrees, Boyd's analytical one-parameter family frequently factors, skipping nearly 50% of target degrees and leaving no general construction for arbitrary $d$. Here, we address the high-degree regime through a human-AI discovery loop, in which we extend the conversational prompting with a fast numerical pipeline, an exact symbolic verifier, and large-scale distributed search. The AI agent prototypes numerical and symbolic solvers locally, launches parallel searches across distributed clusters and works with the human researcher to extract and verify mathematical hypotheses. Through this interactive loop, we derive a five-parameter construction, which we call the Generalized Boyd algorithm. It eliminates degree-skipping and constructs irreducible polynomials with Mahler measure strictly below Boyd's limit point $α_1 \approx 1.255434$ for every target degree. Evaluated across all even degrees up to 2000, our approach improves upon six previously published values and holds 99.6% of all best known records. With this work we release the verified dataset of polynomials with minimal Mahler measure across all studied even degrees.

</details>


### [125] [Route-and-Generate: KL-Regularized Router Improvement for Reasoning Routing in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30351)
*Tien-Phat Nguyen,Truong Nguyen,Thanh-Thien Le,Hoang Phan,Thien Huu Nguyen,Linh Ngo Van,Trung Le*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Route-and-Generate (Rouge) 分层训练框架，让 LLM 按输入难度自适应决定是否推理以及推理量，从而改善准确率-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型往往不加区分地使用复杂推理，导致简单任务"过思考"、困难任务"思考不足"，缺乏按需分配推理算力的机制。

Method: 将推理投入分配与答案生成解耦：引入路由策略，在多种推理格式间分配固定采样预算；基座模型用 GRPO 及逐样本奖励优化；采用基于排序的奖励偏好更短的正确答案；路由策略通过闭式 KL 正则化策略改进步骤更新，并蒸馏进神经路由器。

Result: 在多个推理基准上取得更优的准确率-效率权衡：简单问题上答案更简洁，困难问题上保留更长推理。

Conclusion: 通过分层路由与奖励设计实现按需推理，是缓解过思考与思考不足、提升推理效率的有效途径。

Abstract: Large language models often apply complex reasoning indiscriminately, leading to overthinking on simple tasks and underthinking on harder ones. We present Route-and-Generate (Rouge), a hierarchical training framework that enables LLMs to adaptively decide when and how much reasoning is required for each input. Our approach decouples reasoning effort allocation from answer generation by introducing a routing policy that allocates a fixed sampling budget across multiple reasoning formats, while the base model is optimized with GRPO using per-sample rewards. To promote efficient reasoning, we employ a rank-based reward that favors shorter correct solutions, and update the routing policy via a closed-form KL-regularized policy improvement step distilled into a neural router. This design encourages concise answers on easy problems while reserving longer reasoning for harder ones, achieving improved accuracy-efficiency trade-offs across multiple reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [126] [Soft Ontology Priors over Latent Concept Posteriors for Weakly Supervised Neuro-Symbolic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30391)
*Tengwei Song,Robert Hoehndorf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文针对神经符号(NeSy)预测器的"推理捷径"问题，提出一种软本体先验+软编译为概率电路的方法，在保持硬结构约束的同时，通过有界扰动保证概念可识别性，并在BDD-OIA基准上验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅用任务标签监督的NeSy预测器存在推理捷径：不同的概念映射可达到相同最大似然，导致恢复出的概念不可识别，难以安全地用于下游复用。

Method: 在训练目标中注入关于潜在概念的软本体先验，并与任务约束一起编译为平滑、可分解的概率电路；采用软编译策略，将专家公理放入电路权重而非支撑集（由数据生成过程保证的任务-结构公理仍保持硬约束）。在独立伯努利感知下，神经叶权重先折叠进电路，使结构化概念后验的归一化子化为与编译电路规模成线性的加权模型计数。

Result: 刻画了先验如何通过本体违背能量的差异重新加权标签受限的捷径质量；证明软先验在有界公理噪声下仅产生有界对数密度扰动，而硬支撑版本可被单条损坏公理导致退化；在真实BDD-OIA驾驶基准的六概念审阅视图及受控NeSy任务上验证了上述预测。

Conclusion: 软本体先验结合概率电路软编译可有效缓解推理捷径、提升概念可识别性与鲁棒性，且计算代价随电路规模线性增长，兼顾理论保证与实证有效性。

Abstract: Neuro-symbolic (NeSy) predictors that supervise only the task label suffer from reasoning shortcuts: distinct concept mappings achieve the same maximum likelihood, leaving the recovered concepts unidentifiable and unsafe for downstream reuse. We attack this failure by injecting a soft ontology prior over the latent concepts into the training objective and compiling it together with the task constraint into a smooth, decomposable probabilistic circuit. Under independent Bernoulli perception, the normalisers of the resulting structured concept posterior reduce to weighted model counts that are linear in the compiled circuit size, because the neural leaf weights fold into the circuit before evaluation. The soft compilation strategy places expert axioms in the circuit's weights rather than its support (task-structural axioms guaranteed by the data-generating process stay hard), so noisy expert axioms only redistribute prior mass and cannot empty the support. We characterise how the prior reweights label-restricted shortcut mass through differences in ontology violation energy, show that soft priors incur bounded log-density perturbations under bounded axiom noise, and prove that the hard-support counterpart can be made degenerate by a single corrupted axiom. We validate these predictions on an audited six-concept view of the real-world BDD-OIA driving benchmark and on controlled NeSy tasks.

</details>


### [127] [RoleFlow: Self-Evolving Multi-Role Agentic Collaboration via Structural Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30429)
*Yi Ge,Pan Wang,Xuehao Cui,Zian Zeng,Xu Hu,Heng Huang,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RoleFlow 通过反馈驱动的原子编辑与按需角色合成实现自演化多角色智能体协作结构，并提出 SECPO 以单步反事实信用分配训练该结构自适应能力，在七个基准上平均性能领先最强对手 4.7 分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的多智能体系统依赖预定义协作结构，难以适应动态变化的任务需求；已有的动态编排方法虽引入轨迹级奖励，但将单一结果分配给每个决策，难以识别单个结构变更的贡献，导致信用分配粒度不足。

Method: 提出 RoleFlow 自演化多角色智能体系统：以反馈驱动的原子编辑和按需角色合成来迭代精化协作结构；并设计 Single-Edit Counterfactual Policy Optimization（SECPO），在同一链状态下将每次编辑与其“不编辑”对照进行比较，为编辑专属 token 分配定向信用，使结构自适应过程可学习。

Result: 在覆盖四个领域的七个基准上取得最优平均性能，超过最强基线 4.7 分；在三个多跳问答基准上以 3.2–7.5 F1 领先，并在数学推理、网页交互与代码生成上同样占优。分析进一步验证了跨基准迁移性、训练数据效率与跨执行器泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将协作结构演化建模为可学习的原子编辑序列并结合单步反事实信用分配，可有效提升多智能体系统的自适应性与整体性能，且具备良好的迁移与泛化特性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) based multi-agent systems often rely on predefined collaboration structures, limiting their adaptability to evolving task requirements. Although recent approaches enable dynamic orchestration, trajectory-level rewards they apply assign one outcome to each decision, ofen struggling to identify the contributions of individual structural changes. We propose RoleFlow, a self-evolving multi-role agentic system that refines collaboration structures through feedback-driven atomic edits and on-demand role synthesis. To make structural adaptation learnable, we introduce Single-Edit Counterfactual Policy Optimization (SECPO), which evaluates each edit against its no-edit counterpart at the same chain state and assigns targeted credit to edit-specific tokens. Across seven benchmarks spanning four domains, RoleFlow achieves the optimal average performance, surpassing the strongest competitor by 4.7 points. It leads on all three multi-hop QA benchmarks by 3.2-7.5 F1 points and also shows supremacy on mathematical reasoning, web interaction, and code generation. Further analyses demonstrate cross-benchmark transferability, training-data efficiency, and cross-executor generalization.

</details>


### [128] [Secure Agentic AI and Multimodal Intelligence in 6G Communication Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30436)
*Satyam Gaba,Devanshu Motwani,Siva Sai,Vineet Suri,Vikas Ranjan,Vinay Chamola*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该综述从隐私与安全视角系统梳理面向6G的分层通信系统中的多模态智能与智能体AI，提出感知-记忆-工具使用-多智能体编排的系统视图，构建跨感知、认知、行动、记忆与通信层的威胁模型与攻击分类法，并归纳防御策略与开放方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G需通过多模态智能、边缘计算与自主网络控制的深度集成支持沉浸式、意图驱动、低时延关键业务；智能体AI具备异构推理、工具调用、分布式协同与动态适应能力，适用于语义通信、数字孪生、零接触网络管理等场景，但其引入的安全与隐私风险超出传统AI与网络安全模型范畴，且可在多模态数据流水线、记忆、工具、通信接口与智能体协同环路间传播。

Method: 综述性研究：提出面向6G的系统视图，覆盖感知、记忆、工具使用与多智能体编排；沿感知层、认知层、行动层、记忆层与通信层分析威胁模型与攻击面；将现有攻击整理为结构化分类法；并评述缓解策略，包括提示与记忆防御、智能体间信任机制、安全编排与隐私保护学习。

Result: 形成了一套面向6G多模态多智能体系统的跨层安全与隐私分析框架：明确各层攻击面、给出攻击分类体系，并对现有防御手段进行分类评述。

Conclusion: 面向可信多模态多智能体6G系统，提出了开放研究方向，强调需在系统层面统筹感知、记忆、工具与协同编排的安全隐私保障。

Abstract: Sixth-generation (6G) communication systems are expected to support immersive, intent-driven, and latency-critical services through tight integration of multimodal intelligence, edge computing, and autonomous network control. In this setting, agentic AI systems can reason over heterogeneous inputs, use tools and network functions, coordinate across distributed entities, and adapt to dynamic environments. These capabilities make agentic multimodal intelligence promising for 6G applications such as semantic communications, digital twins, and zero-touch network management, but they also introduce security and privacy risks that extend beyond conventional AI and network security models. Vulnerabilities can propagate across multimodal data pipelines, memory, tools, communication interfaces, and inter-agent coordination loops. We survey multimodal intelligence and agentic AI for 6G cross-layered communication systems from the perspectives of privacy and security. We present a 6G-oriented system view spanning perception, memory, tool use, and multi-agent orchestration; analyze threat models and attack surfaces across sensing, cognition, action, memory, and communication layers; organize existing attacks into a structured taxonomy; and review mitigation strategies including prompt and memory defenses, inter-agent trust mechanisms, secure orchestration, and privacy-preserving learning. We conclude by outlining open research directions for trustworthy multimodal multi-agent 6G systems.

</details>


### [129] [SpecMAGIC: Specification-Guided Multi-Task Generalization in Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30443)
*Zijian Guo,İlker Işık,H. M. Sabbir Ahmad,Wenchao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SpecMAGIC分层框架，用带时序逻辑的任务规约语言描述多智能体协调需求，借助自动机结构分解子任务，实现对新规约任务的零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务泛化在多智能体强化学习中研究不足，而现实应用要求多智能体系统在具有多样协调需求、时间跨度长的复杂任务间泛化。

Method: 定义结合时序逻辑任务规约与协调需求算子的规约语言；构建分层框架，利用任务规约的自动机结构进行子任务分解：集中式高层规划器识别并为各智能体分配兼容的子任务序列，去中心化底层策略执行单个子任务；通过按新规约组合可复用子任务策略实现免重训练的零样本泛化。并证明高层规划器的可靠性与概率完备性。

Result: 多智能体导航任务实验中，SpecMAGIC在未见过的任务规约上显著优于现有方法，且在智能体数量增加时保持强性能。

Conclusion: 以规约驱动的子任务分解与策略复用是实现MARL多任务零样本泛化的有效途径，并具备理论保证与良好可扩展性。

Abstract: Multi-task generalization is an important yet underexplored problem in multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL). Many real-world applications require multi-agent systems to generalize across complex, temporally extended tasks with diverse inter-agent coordination requirements. We introduce a specification language that combines temporal-logic task specifications with operators for expressing such requirements. We propose SpecMAGIC, a hierarchical framework that leverages the automaton structure of task specifications for subtask decomposition. A centralized high-level planner identifies and allocates compatible subtask sequences across agents, while decentralized low-level policies execute individual subtasks. This enables zero-shot generalization to new tasks by composing reusable subtask policies according to new specifications, without retraining. We establish soundness and probabilistic completeness of the high-level planner. Experiments on multi-agent navigation tasks show that SpecMAGIC substantially outperforms existing approaches on unseen task specifications and maintains strong performance as the number of agents increases.

</details>


### [130] [A Three-Pillar Governance Framework for Safe and Trustworthy AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30445)
*Edward C. Cheng,Jeshua Cheng,Alice Siu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出三支柱（3P）模型与框架，通过透明性、问责性和可信性支撑安全可信AI智能体的开发，并以渐进式验证、结构化人类监督和可审计运行控制构建自主性递增的演化路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具备自主决策能力的AI智能体在安全性、对齐、问责与人类监督方面带来显著挑战；现有HITL与负责任AI方法虽有重要基础，但在系统设计、治理与运行部署层面仍呈现碎片化，缺乏整合监督原则与运行机制的统一框架。

Method: 提出三支柱（3P）模型与框架，以透明性、问责性、可信性为支柱；设计一条通过渐进式验证、结构化人类监督与可审计运行控制来逐步提升智能体自主性的演化路径；并阐述模型如何支持智能体生命周期监控、决策可追溯、升级管理与人类—智能体协作。

Result: 框架将监督原则与运行机制相结合，覆盖生命周期监控、决策可追溯性、升级管理和人机协作等能力，为在企业级智能体环境中部署保持与人类意图对齐的AI智能体提供可操作基础。

Conclusion: 通过整合监督原则与运行控制机制，3P框架为在保持人类意图对齐的前提下、可控地推进更高自主性提供了实践基础；论文主要为概念性框架贡献，未报告实证评估结果。

Abstract: AI agents capable of autonomous decision-making introduce significant challenges related to safety, alignment, accountability, and human oversight. Existing Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) and responsible AI approaches provide important foundations, but many remain fragmented across system design, governance, and operational deployment. This paper presents a Three-Pillar (3P) model and framework for developing safe and trustworthy AI agents through transparency, accountability, and trustworthiness. The framework defines an evolutionary path in which increasing agent autonomy is introduced through progressive validation, structured human oversight, and auditable operational controls. We further describe how the model supports agent lifecycle monitoring, decision traceability, escalation management, and human-agent collaboration within enterprise agentic environments. By integrating oversight principles with operational mechanisms, the proposed framework provides a practical foundation for deploying AI agents that remain aligned with human intent while enabling controlled progression toward higher autonomy.

</details>


### [131] [Same Beliefs, Different Policies: Learning Reusable Representations for Early Stopping in LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30450)
*Jingwei Ni,Dmitry Abulkhanov,Vladislav Smirnov,Markus Leippold,Elliott Ash,Timothy Baldwin,Artem Shelmanov,Mrinmaya Sachan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BELT将"何时停止推理"拆分为结果预测与停止控制两步：从冻结LLM隐状态学习关于当前/未来答案的信念，再在冻结特征上拟合线性停止策略，从而以单一探针支持多价格精度-成本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理LLM常在得到正确答案后继续推理，浪费算力甚至把正确答案改错；何时停止属于元推理问题。现有直接效用训练在特定token价格下学习停止表征，支持新价格需重新训练控制器。

Method: BELT（BELief-based Termination）解耦结果预测与停止控制：在冻结LLM隐状态上学习对当前及未来答案的信念预测，预测目标与token价格无关；随后在冻结特征上拟合线性停止策略，通过CPU端策略重拟合或阈值调整适配不同精度-成本权衡。

Result: 在5个OOD数学基准上平均，Qwen3-1.7B减少41%、OLMo-3-7B-Think减少51%生成token且无精度损失；数学训练的BELT零样本迁移到代码与智能体任务，三领域权衡均不劣于端到端控制器；OLMo3上探针参数量比最强基线OS-Pruner单检查点少21.2倍，训练GPU时数比其5个价格专用检查点少55倍。

Conclusion: 将结果预测与停止控制解耦可在保持精度的同时显著节省计算，并以极低参数与训练成本实现跨价格、跨领域的通用早停。

Abstract: Reasoning LLMs often continue reasoning after reaching a correct answer, wasting computation and sometimes replacing it with an incorrect one. Deciding when to stop is a metareasoning problem: the model must weigh what further reasoning could achieve against its cost, given what it can already answer. Direct utility training learns a stopping representation at a chosen token price; supporting new prices can require additional controller training. We introduce BELT (BELief-based Termination), which separates outcome prediction from stopping control. It learns beliefs about current and future answers from a frozen LLM's hidden states, then fits a linear stopping policy on the frozen features. The prediction targets are independent of token price, so one probe supports different accuracy-cost trade-offs through CPU policy refitting or threshold adjustment. Averaged over five out-of-distribution (OOD) math benchmarks, BELT reduces generated tokens by 41% on Qwen3-1.7B and 51% on OLMo-3-7B-Think without loss of accuracy. The same math-trained BELT transfers to coding and agentic tasks without training on code or agentic data. In all three domains, its accuracy-cost trade-offs are competitive with or better than those of end-to-end controllers. On OLMo3, its probe has 21.2x fewer parameters than one checkpoint of the strongest baseline, OS-Pruner, and needs 55x fewer training GPU-hours than OS-Pruner's five price-specific checkpoints.

</details>


### [132] [Why Bother Me Again? Benchmarking Personal Agent Memory for Interaction Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30485)
*Jialin Li,Zhenhao Chen,Hanjun Luo,Hanan Salam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MemPABench——一个用于在策略评估个人智能体“交互偏好记忆”的交互式环境，发现现有记忆系统在偏好选择上收益有限且难以跨情境、随偏好变化灵活调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人智能体承担日益复杂的日常任务，但重复提问、多余确认与不必要打扰会形成“交互负担”。降低负担需智能体记住用户的协助偏好，而现有记忆基准多聚焦事实回忆与个性化问答，很少测试智能体如何学习并依据交互偏好行动。

Method: 构建MemPABench交互环境：(1) 设长程任务，要求智能体决定何时澄清、确认或自主行动；(2) 4个具身模拟用户，在工作与个人情境下共持有14项偏好；(3) 反馈跨4个月、99个会话，每人含5次事件驱动的偏好变化以测试记忆更新；(4) 评估维度包括偏好选择、实际交付行为及对情境与偏好变化的敏感性；(5) 在4种LLM骨干上评测4种记忆系统。

Result: 现有记忆在偏好选择上增益有限：基于检索的系统（Mem0、MemOS、简单RAG）未优于无记忆基线；基于文件的记忆提升准确率，但会把偏好应用到无关情境之外。分析表明当前记忆难以检索交互偏好并适应偏好变化，即便正确偏好可得，覆盖智能体默认交互习惯仍具挑战。

Conclusion: 交互偏好记忆是个人智能体的关键且未解难题，检索式记忆不足以支撑；“记得偏好”与“按偏好改变默认行为”之间存在鸿沟，需专门设计面向交互偏好学习与更新的记忆机制。

Abstract: Personal intelligence systems are taking on increasingly complex everyday tasks, yet repeated questions, unnecessary confirmations, and unwanted outreach can turn capable assistance into interaction burden. Reducing this burden requires agents to remember how users want to be assisted. However, existing memory benchmarks focus on factual recall and personalized answers and rarely test how agents learn and act on interaction preferences. We introduce MemPABench, an interactive environment for on-policy evaluation of memory in personal agents. MemPABench poses long-horizon tasks requiring agents to decide when to clarify, confirm, or act autonomously. Four grounded simulated users hold 14 preferences across work and personal contexts. Their feedback spans 99 sessions over four months, with five event-driven changes per user testing memory updates. We evaluate preference selection, delivered behavior, and sensitivity to context and preference change. Evaluating four memory systems across four LLM backbones, we find that current memory brings limited gains in preference selection. Retrieval-based systems (i.e. Mem0, MemOS, simple RAG) do not outperform No Memory, while File-based memory improves accuracy but applies preferences beyond relevant contexts. Our analysis shows that current memory struggles to retrieve interaction preferences and adapt to preference changes. Even with the correct preference available, overriding the agent's default interaction habits remains challenging. The environment and dataset are available at https://mempabench.com.

</details>


### [133] [Better Reasoning, Not Just Bigger Models: A Structure-Grounded Framework for Efficient and Reliable Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30611)
*Amirhossein Aghaei,Liangyan Li,Shiyi Yang,Shaohua Wang,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架 SGUR，通过结构化假设空间上的主动不确定性约减，弥补模型规模不足，在医学序贯诊断基准上以更少 token 显著优于 Graph-RAG 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮推理中智能体易产生语义合理但结构上缺乏依据的推理，尤其在观测不完整时；可靠自治智能体需要的不仅是语言流畅性，还需结构化的不确定性处理能力。

Method: SGUR 将不完整观测表示为查询图，与候选假设图通过先验加权部分图匹配对齐（数据库经验先验对决策相关关系赋权，而非等权处理所有边），并用主动信息增益策略迭代选择下一个最具信息量的节点进行查询，系统性地剪枝候选空间；全程免训练。

Result: 在 PrimeKG 衍生的序贯医学诊断基准上，仅从两个观测症状出发即达 92.5% Hit@3，较最强 Graph-RAG 基线高 9.4 个百分点，token 消耗约少 5 倍；小骨干模型可匹配或超越规模大至 10 倍的模型。

Conclusion: 结构化接地可部分替代模型规模，是比单纯扩大模型更稳健、更高效的智能体性能扩展范式。

Abstract: Scaling large language models has substantially improved linguistic fluency, yet reliable autonomous agents require more than fluent generation. In multi-turn inference, current agentic systems often produce semantically plausible but structurally ungrounded reasoning, especially under incomplete observations. We propose Structure-Grounded Uncertainty Reduction (SGUR), a training-free framework that complements token-level generation with active uncertainty reduction over structured hypothesis spaces. SGUR represents incomplete observations as a query graph and aligns them with candidate hypothesis graphs through a prior-weighted partial graph matching mechanism, where database-derived empirical priors assign greater importance to decision-relevant relations rather than treating all graph edges uniformly. The resulting inference state is iteratively refined by an active information-gain strategy that selects the next most informative node to query, systematically pruning the candidate space. We instantiate SGUR on a challenging PrimeKG-derived sequential medical diagnosis benchmark, demonstrating that structural grounding can partially substitute for model scale: starting from only two observed symptoms, SGUR achieves 92.5% Hit@3, outperforming the strongest Graph-RAG baseline by 9.4 percentage points while using approximately 5x fewer tokens. Notably, SGUR enables smaller backbone models to match or exceed the performance of models up to 10x larger, suggesting that structural grounding provides a more robust and efficient paradigm for scaling agent performance than relying exclusively on increasing model size.

</details>


### [134] [From Single-Actor Environments to Multi-Actor Event Worlds: Can LLMs Be Event World Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30488)
*Juntong Ni,Zewen Liu,Bohan Wang,Lige Zhang,Wei Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"事件世界"(event worlds)形式化框架,基于Diplomacy人类对局与Polymarket真实交易构建基准,系统评估大语言模型能否依据事件日志和智能体拟执行动作预测未来观测,并考察其他行动者行为的建模难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实系统多为多主体、可自主演化、仅通过事件日志被部分观测的场景;现有世界模型研究多假设单智能体、全观测与静态环境,缺乏对"他者自主行动"这一核心难点的评估。

Method: 形式化"事件世界";构建Diplomacy与Polymarket互补基准;采用层级化评测框架:先在给定全部行动者动作与否两种条件下比较预测,再做多步rollout、未见动作预测,并检验对智能体决策的实用性;配合微调与数据规模实验。

Result: 给定全部行动者记录动作时,微调与更多数据可显著提升预测;仅给智能体自身动作时,若他者影响强则模型预测显著变差,增大模型规模或延长历史收益有限;该差距进一步限制多步预测与决策,模型系统性高估计划成功率;但想象经验与世界模型初始化仍可改善策略学习。

Conclusion: 对其他行动者行为的预期是事件世界建模的核心瓶颈;世界模型可作为策略学习的辅助信号,但尚不能可靠替代对他者行为的多步推演。

Abstract: World models help agents assess the consequences of their actions before acting. Many real systems, however, involve multiple actors who make their own decisions, can evolve even when the agent takes no action, and are only partially observed through event logs. We formalize these systems as event worlds and ask whether large language models can predict future observations from the log and the agent's proposed actions. We build complementary benchmarks from human Diplomacy games and real Polymarket trades. Our hierarchical framework compares prediction with and without other actors' actions, then tests multi-step rollouts, predictions for actions never taken, and usefulness to agents. Fine-tuning and more data improve prediction when all actors' recorded actions are given. With only the agent's action, models struggle when other actors strongly affect the outcome, and larger models and longer histories help little. This gap also limits multi-step prediction and agent decisions, with models overestimating plan success. Yet imagined experience and initialization from world models can still improve policy learning. Anticipating other actors' actions remains a central challenge for event world modeling.

</details>


### [135] [What the Trace Shows and What the Label Says: Auditing Behavioral Detectors in Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30491)
*Idunnuoluwa A. Adeniji,Shilpika,Michael E. Papka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文审计了LLM agent轨迹级检测器标签的可靠性，发现最终正确性、可见重验证与故障传播等标签会系统性不一致，并揭示"静默工具输出覆盖"这一失效模式，进而提出测量透明度原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM agent评估将执行轨迹压缩为最终答案正确性、工具验证、故障传播等标签，但这些标签由解析器、行为检测器与评测规则生成，可能无法真实反映轨迹内容。作者希望量化这种标签与轨迹实际行为之间的偏差。

Method: 基于GSM8K构建工具调用场景，令agent调用一个输出偶发被污染的计算器，从而获得"工具返回什么、模型后续使用了什么、检测器报告了什么"的清晰真值。在6个生产模型、每个模型30道题上审计三类轨迹级检测器，并进行90条轨迹的人工复核，以及步数与token预算的消融实验。

Result: 最终正确性、可见重验证与故障传播标签存在系统性分歧。关键失效模式为"静默工具输出覆盖"：计算器返回污染值，但模型未再次调用工具却改用另一个（常为正确）值，导致坏值不再传播，而验证检测器记录为"无重检查"。人工复核与自动标签在最终正确性(67/67)、故障影响(19/19)、故障相关性(18/18)上完全一致，但在验证上仅67/81一致，14处分歧归为四类反复出现的模式。执行预算也影响结果：消融中一个模型正确数随步数与token预算在21/30至27/30间波动，另一模型稳定在27/30至29/30。

Conclusion: 自动化标签应附带其背后的轨迹证据，包括检测器所问问题是否适用于该轨迹、运行是否因执行上限被截断，以及人工修正记录。作者通过轨迹查看器使这些信息与报告结果一同可检查，倡导测量透明度。

Abstract: LLM agents are often evaluated by reducing execution traces to labels such as final-answer correctness, tool verification, and fault propagation. These labels are measurements produced by parsers, behavioral detectors, and harness rules, and they may not fully capture what the trace shows. We study this in a tool-use setting built on GSM8K, where agents call a calculator whose outputs are occasionally corrupted, giving clear ground truth for what the tool returned, what the model later used, and what the detectors reported. We audit three trace-level detectors across six production models and 30 problems per model. Final correctness, visible re-verification, and fault propagation can disagree in systematic ways. A key failure mode is silent tool-output override: the calculator returns a corrupted value, but the model later uses a different, often correct, value without calling the tool again, so the bad value stops propagating while a verification detector records no re-check. Human review of 90 traces agrees perfectly with the automated labels for final correctness (67/67), fault impact (19/19), and fault relevance (18/18), but only 67/81 for verification; all 14 disagreements fall into four recurring patterns. Execution limits also change the reported result: in an ablation across two models, one ranges from 21/30 to 27/30 correct as step and token budgets change, while the other stays between 27/30 and 29/30. These findings motivate measurement transparency: an automated label should come with the trace evidence behind it, whether the question the detector asks applied to that trace, whether the run was cut off by an execution limit, and any human corrections. Our trace viewer makes this information inspectable alongside the reported results.

</details>


### [136] [From Prose to Properties: Testing Research Claims with ARATest for Agent-Native Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30495)
*Yifeng He,Jiachen Liu,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 ARATest——一个基于属性的测试库，将论文中的经验性断言抽象为"属性骨架"（可复用的参数化关系），以可执行测试的形式检验断言，并支持三值判定与可重放的违例证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI agent 已能自动做实验并报告结论，使经验性断言产生的速度快于人工核查速度。每条断言都隐含某个需在其案例上成立的性质，但仅以自然语言表述，缺乏显式性质便无法确定何种证据能证伪它，导致验证跟不上断言产出。

Method: 通过抽象"属性骨架"（跨断言复现的参数化关系）近似断言语义。ARATest 以带类型的声明将断言绑定到骨架，固定被比较量、案例、作用域与数值策略；共享案例类型保证各生成器按断言的统计单元提供案例；采用三值语义（成立/违背/不确定）；默认生成器读取 ARA 制品中与断言关联的证据，auto-research 生成器派 agent 运行发布的代码以获取新案例；DissClaimer 插件将上述生成器作为持续测试运行。

Result: 在 33 份 ARA 制品的 243 条断言中，54 条获得绑定证据的忠实声明：32 条成立、8 条被违背、14 条不确定；其中 2 条违例揭示已发表论文的错误并经作者确认。ARATest 在 19 个有效对照上无误报地检测出全部 553 个植入故障，优于两个 LLM 评审器；新鲜运行还暴露了报告证据无法呈现的行为。

Conclusion: 固定的一套测试可将显式断言的属性同时对照"已报告证据"与"新鲜证据"进行核查，提示基于属性测试的自动化断言验证具备可行性。

Abstract: AI agents now run experiments and report findings, so research produces claims faster than researchers can check them. Each empirical claim implies a property that must hold over its cases, but it states that property only in prose. Without an explicit property, no test can decide which evidence would falsify a claim, so validation cannot keep pace with the claims agents produce. To make claims testable, we approximate a claim's semantics by abstracting its property skeleton, a parameterized relation that recurs across claims. We present ARATest, a property-based testing library that checks these skeletons as executable tests. A typed declaration binds a claim to a skeleton and fixes the claim's compared quantities, cases, scope, and numerical policy. A shared case type makes every generator supply cases in the claim's statistical unit. A three-valued semantics assigns each property one of three verdicts: holds, violated, or inconclusive. Every violation carries a replayable witness. A default generator reads the evidence that agent-native research artifacts (ARA) link to each claim, and auto-research generators dispatch an agent to run released code for fresh cases. Our DissClaimer plugin supplies that agent and runs these generators as continuous testing.
  Of 243 claims in 33 ARA artifacts, 54 received faithful declarations bound to evidence: 32 held, eight were violated, and 14 remained inconclusive. Two violations exposed errors in published papers that their authors confirmed. ARATest detected all 553 seeded faults without a false alarm on 19 valid controls, outperforming two LLM judges. Fresh runs exposed behaviors that reported evidence could not show. These results suggest that one fixed test can check explicit claim properties against both reported and fresh evidence.

</details>


### [137] [Can LLMs Improve Their Own Harness? A Controlled Study on ARC-AGI-3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30499)
*Jens Lehmann,Sahar Vahdati*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文考察前沿语言模型能否自主构建并迭代改进用于ARC-AGI-3的符号化harness（编排模型调用的程序），在27条谱系、两次代码修订协议下评估其留出集表现与推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用智能体应能高效获取新技能而无需人为重设计软件脚手架；而语言模型很少单独行动，依赖harness处理感知、记忆、工具调用、恢复与推理分配。已有结果表明人类指导的系统在ARC-AGI-3公开游戏上已达上限分数，说明有效设计存在，但尚不清楚前沿模型能否自主构建并改进此类harness。

Method: 采用固定编码引导（fixed coding bootstrap）：每个模型构建完整任务harness，在该harness下于5个开发游戏上行动，并对整个代码库进行两次修订；每个版本在20个从不参与适应的游戏上评估。以Relative Human Action Efficiency（RHAE）衡量任务表现（结合关卡完成度与相对人类基线的动作效率），并记录以美元计价的推理成本作为实用资源指标；共27条谱系。

Result: harness表现从零到接近上限，跨度极大；两次修订仅使留出集平均RHAE提升2.80分（95%区间[-1.44, 7.35]）；开发集增益高估留出集改进；推理成本下降可能以任务表现为代价；人类指导系统仍显示出更大提升空间。

Conclusion: 在两次修订协议下，自主harness构建与可靠适应之间存在差距：即便任务表现强劲，也不能保证后续修订可泛化或保留既有能力。

Abstract: A generally intelligent system acquires new skills efficiently without requiring humans to redesign its software scaffold for every new task. Yet language models rarely act alone. A harness is a symbolic program that orchestrates calls to the model, handling perception, memory, tool use, recovery, and inference allocation on the model's behalf. ARC-AGI-3 is an interactive benchmark in which agents must explore unfamiliar environments, figure out their goals on the fly, and keep learning as they go. On it, human-steered systems already report ceiling scores across the public games, so we know effective designs exist. We ask whether frontier models can build and improve such a harness on their own. Through a fixed coding bootstrap, each model constructs a complete task harness, acts through that harness on five development games, and revises the entire codebase twice. Every version is evaluated on twenty games that never influence adaptation. We measure task performance with Relative Human Action Efficiency (RHAE), which combines level completion with action efficiency relative to human baselines, and record dollar-denominated inference cost as a practical resource measure. Across 27 lineages, harness performance ranges from zero to near-ceiling scores. Two revisions raise mean held-out RHAE by only 2.80 points (95% interval [-1.44,7.35]). Development gains overstate held-out improvement, and reductions in inference cost can come at the expense of task performance. Human-steered systems demonstrate further headroom.
