TL;DR: 将元智能体架构搜索形式化为带切换成本与风险约束的顺序替换问题，提出CMAS：用配对影子回放+干预式组件信用打分，经校准的方差惩罚同时显著性检验+边距门槛晋升，并配套模拟金丝雀与预设回滚；同时发布MetaInvest-Bench（制度切换模拟器+五类负对照）。96条流上把归一化动态oracle遗憾从0.116降至0.104、留出效用0.44→0.48，无增益时收缩至近单智能体团队；部署推理成本降约6%但含搜索的总token成本最高；每次更新误晋升率3.4%（标称5%），封闭重跑4.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化智能体系统设计多在静态任务集上以即时标量奖励评估，而真实部署面临持续分布漂移、延迟且相互依赖的反馈、组件失效相关、以及昂贵的架构变更代价。回测无法证明元层能力，缺少可计算最优架构、可控泄漏与对抗性治理评测的基准。

Method: CMAS：检索归档架构并生成类型化图编辑；对每个候选与现任架构在公共随机数下做配对影子回放（paired shadow replay），结合干预式组件信用归因打分；晋升需通过经验校准、方差惩罚的同时（simultaneous）显著性检验并超过预设边距，随后进入模拟金丝雀阶段与预设回滚。MetaInvest-Bench：制度切换模拟器，指定工人能力使代理最优架构可计算，含泄漏控制回放、对抗性治理赛道与五个负对照；以金融流作为风险敏感测试床（不主张alpha）。

Result: 96条流上，归一化均值代理动态oracle遗憾由0.116降至0.104，留出效用由0.44升至0.48（对比最强自适应基线）；当额外工人无增益时收缩至近单智能体团队；部署推理成本约低6%，但含搜索的token成本在对比方法中最高；每次更新误晋升率3.4%（目标5%），封闭重跑为4.8%。

Conclusion: 校准是经验性的：仅针对作者设计基准上的更新级回放统计量，不保证整体效用提升、不保证模拟金丝雀阶段无害、也不提供序列级错误控制。贡献在于把元架构搜索的部署风险（切换成本、延迟依赖反馈、相关失效）显式建模，并提供可验证的评测协议与诚实的负对照框架。

Abstract: Automated agentic-system design is typically evaluated on static task suites with immediate scalar rewards. Deployed systems face persistent shift, delayed dependent feedback, correlated component failures, and costly architecture changes. We formalize meta-agent architecture search as sequential incumbent replacement with switching costs and hard risk constraints and introduce Conservative Meta-Agent Search (CMAS). CMAS retrieves archived architectures and proposes typed graph edits, then scores each against the incumbent by paired shadow replay under common random numbers, with interventional component credit. Promotion requires an empirically calibrated, variance-penalized simultaneous score above a pre-specified margin, and is followed by a simulated canary stage and pre-specified rollback. Since backtests cannot certify meta-level skill, we introduce MetaInvest-Bench, a regime-switching simulator whose specified worker competence makes a surrogate-optimal architecture computable, with leakage-controlled replay, adversarial governance tracks, and five negative controls. Financial streams serve as a risk-sensitive testbed for meta-level generalization; we claim no alpha. Across 96 streams, CMAS cuts normalized mean surrogate dynamic-oracle regret from 0.116 to 0.104 and raises held-out utility from 0.44 to 0.48 over the strongest adaptive baseline; when extra workers add no value, it contracts to near-single-agent teams. Its deployment inference cost is ~6% lower, though its search-inclusive token cost is the highest of the methods compared. The observed per-update false-promotion rate is 3.4% against a nominal 5% target, and 4.8% in a sealed rerun. The calibration is empirical: it concerns an update-level replay statistic on an author-designed benchmark, and guarantees neither full-utility improvement, nor absence of simulated canary-stage harm, nor sequence-level error control.

</details>


### [209] [DIAL: Steerable Language Models with Distilled Instruction-Adherence Levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31438)
*Thanh Q. Tran,John Russell Himawan,Arun Verma,Kiwan Wong,Wei Xiao,Daniela Rus,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 DIAL：用冻结 LLM 的无分类器引导（CFG）生成训练信号，经 on-policy 蒸馏训练轻量、文本条件化的 steering adapter，实现对文本指令遵循程度的细粒度数值控制，兼具效率与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户与应用的指令遵循需求强度不同，而提示词无法可靠地提供细粒度控制。现有 steering 方法无法同时满足细粒度控制、效率与跨多样指令的可扩展性。

Method: (1) 细粒度控制：对冻结 LLM 使用 CFG 产生不同遵循水平的训练信号，进行 on-policy 蒸馏；(2) 效率：设计轻量 steering adapter，用作用于隐状态的分类器计算闭式 steering 编辑，并按目标遵循水平、以 token 为单位自适应调节编辑幅度，同时考虑隐状态几何以保持连贯性；(3) 可扩展性：分类器以文本为条件，共享 adapter 可支持新指令而无需逐指令拟合，训练仅需指令与请求文本。

Result: 在多个 LLM 系列上，DIAL 在概念表达与流畅性之间取得有竞争力的平衡，概念得分随遵循水平单调上升；构建 SteerIF 数据集（98,625 条概念、人设、可验证与描述性指令）；在 SteerIF 上训练后可细粒度控制人设，与提示结合时提升可验证指令遵循。

Conclusion: DIAL 首次同时实现细粒度、高效、可扩展的指令遵循可控性，并验证了蒸馏式 CFG 信号 + 隐状态分类器 steering 的有效性；SteerIF 为相关研究提供基准。

Abstract: When a user says "Keep it short", how short should the LLM's response be? More generally, users and applications require different levels of instruction adherence, but prompting does not reliably provide fine-grained control over them. We therefore study "steerable language models" that provide fine-grained numerical control over the degree to which models follow general instructions specified in text. However, existing approaches do not jointly offer fine-grained control, efficiency, and scalability across diverse instructions. To address this gap, we introduce DIAL (Distilled Instruction-Adherence Levels) to achieve all three properties. For fine-grained control, we perform classifier-free guidance (CFG) with a frozen LLM to provide training signals for on-policy distillation of instruction adherence at different levels. For efficiency, we design a lightweight steering adapter that uses a classifier over the LLM's hidden states to compute a closed-form steering edit. The classifier adaptively sets the steering magnitude at each token based on the target adherence level, while accounting for the geometry of the LLM's hidden states to preserve response coherence. For scalability, the classifier is text-conditioned, allowing a shared adapter to support new instructions without per-instruction fitting, and training requires only instruction and request text. Evaluation across multiple LLM families shows that DIAL achieves a competitive balance between concept expression and response fluency, with concept scores increasing monotonically across adherence levels. We introduce SteerIF, an instruction-only dataset of 98, 625 concept, persona, verifiable, and descriptive instructions. When trained on SteerIF, DIAL provides fine-grained control over personas and, when combined with prompting, improves verifiable instruction following.

</details>


### [210] [FairFRESH: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Fair and Explainable Household Food Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31506)
*Kodur Sai Vinay Sathvik,Kakoli Bora,Tejaswini R. M*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将家庭食物推荐建模为受约束的公平分配问题，提出神经符号框架FairFRESH，学习成员效用、按显式福利目标分配食物、用确定性过滤器保证饮食规则合规，并用Shapley/Banzhaf归因解释推荐；理论证明了阈值附近无有限惩罚可保证合规、贪心分配近似保证及Nash福利的尺度不变性，实验验证了公平性、合规性、可解释性与共享餐食的效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推荐系统面向单个用户，但家庭成员共享有限食物储备且各有不同饮食规则，独立推荐易导致资源冲突与饮食违规，因此需将家庭食物推荐重构为受约束的公平分配问题。

Method: 提出FairFRESH神经符号框架：学习各成员效用，在显式福利目标（功利主义与平滑Nash）下分配食物，通过确定性过滤器强制饮食规则；用Shapley与Banzhaf归因提供解释；理论分析包括惩罚项在规则阈值附近的失效、贪心分配近似保证、Nash福利尺度不变性，以及用极小极大定理分析轮换共享餐食。

Result: 在NHANES衍生数据上，学习效用模型Recall@20为0.293，优于五个学习型推荐器（最高0.244），但不及前一日重复启发式（0.321）；四人家庭留出集上，Nash分配较冲突消解使Gini差异降低42%，Recall@20从0.180升至0.197，距平滑Nash最优上界2%以内，单成员效用重缩放仅改变至多1.5%的分配；推荐全部满足饮食规则，归因分数与组件移除后效用损失相关（r=0.76）；模拟中共享餐食使食物储备使用减少约三分之一，周轮换使最差成员效用较最佳固定餐食提升38%。

Conclusion: FairFRESH在保证饮食规则合规与公平性的同时取得有竞争力的推荐性能，并提供可解释性与理论保证；共享餐食与轮换机制可显著降低资源消耗并改善成员间福利均衡。

Abstract: Recommender systems typically serve one user at a time. However, in households, members share a finite pantry and follow different dietary rules, making independent recommendations prone to resource conflicts and dietary violations. We formulate household food recommendation as constrained fair division and present FairFRESH, a neuro-symbolic framework that learns per-member utilities, allocates foods under explicit welfare objectives, enforces dietary rules through a deterministic filter, and explains recommendations using Shapley and Banzhaf attributions. Theoretically, we show that no finite penalty guarantees compliance near a dietary-rule threshold, establish approximation guarantees for greedy allocation under utilitarian and smoothed Nash objectives in the stock-free model before filtering, and characterize the scale invariance of Nash welfare. We also use the minimax theorem to analyze rotating shared meals. On NHANES-derived data, the learned utility model achieves Recall@20 of 0.293, outperforming five learned recommenders (at most 0.244) but not a previous-day repetition heuristic (0.321). On held-out four-person households, Nash allocation reduces Gini disparity by 42% relative to conflict resolution while improving Recall@20 from 0.180 to 0.197. It comes within 2% of an upper bound on the smoothed Nash optimum, and rescaling one member's utilities changes at most 1.5% of assignments. All recommendations satisfy the encoded dietary rules, while attribution scores correlate with utility loss upon component removal (r = 0.76). In simulation, shared meals reduce pantry use by about one-third, and weekly rotation increases the worst-off member's utility by 38% over the best fixed meal.

</details>


### [211] [DuoCook: An Overcooked-Inspired Benchmark for Visual Planning and Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31511)
*Ziyi Jiao,Heng Zhang,Drishti Regmi,Xiaokang Ye,Lianhui Qin,Peter Yichen Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个基于Unreal Engine、由编码智能体构建的Overcooked风格基准，用单一视觉语言模型同时控制两名玩家，在20张地图上评估集中式协调能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 烹饪类协作任务要求视觉语言模型跟踪动态场景状态、排序配方步骤并在时间预算下分配共享资源，现有环境缺乏对上述能力的系统评测。

Method: 在Unreal Engine中构建20张地图（5个类别），覆盖设备争用、地形变化与交接依赖；单一VLM从俯视视觉观测同时控制两名玩家，仅选择高层动作、由引擎执行导航；提供道具图例、锅具食材计数指示及网格化目标点以支持视觉与空间锚定；评估4个VLM的任务得分、订单完成率、交付精度、动作接受率与API开销，并做共享自写笔记本记忆的消融实验（两种条件均保留六轮动作历史）。

Result: 最强模型gpt-6-astra平均订单完成率为26.2%；笔记本记忆效果因模型而异，Claude与Qwen得分平均提升，GPT与Gemini为负向差异。

Conclusion: 该基准可系统性揭示VLM在集中式多智能体协调中的能力短板，且记忆机制收益并不稳健，需按模型定制。

Abstract: Coordinating two players in a cooking task requires a vision-language agent to track changing scene states, sequence recipe steps, and allocate shared resources under a time budget. We introduce an Overcooked-inspired benchmark for evaluating these capabilities in Unreal Engine environments constructed using coding agents. A single vision-language model controls both players from top-down visual observations, selecting high-level actions while the engine executes navigation. The benchmark contains 20 maps in five categories. These categories support evaluation of centralized coordination under equipment contention, changing terrain, and handoff dependencies. A prop legend and pot ingredient-count indicators provide explicit visual cues, while grid-based destinations support spatial grounding. The evaluation compares four VLMs on all 20 maps, measuring task score, order completion, delivery precision, action acceptance rate, and model API expenditure. A memory ablation on the same 20 maps examines the effect of a shared, self-authored notebook while retaining six-response action history in both conditions. The strongest evaluated model, \texttt{gpt-6-astra}, achieves 26.2\% mean order completion. Notebook memory has mixed score effects across models, with positive mean differences for Claude and Qwen and negative differences for GPT and Gemini.

</details>


### [212] [From Prompt Rules to Golden Data Model: Production-Scale Generation of Personalized, Compliant Pharmaceutical Claims](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31519)
*Suyash Mishra,Qiang Li,Subhabrata Adak,Upender Phogat,Srikanth Patil,Satyanarayan Pati*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将合规与个性化信号从提示词级规则迁移到检索时的结构化上下文（Golden Data Model, GDM），可显著提升药企宣传声明生成的目标适配性、质量与复用率，并降低API成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已广泛用于制药等受监管行业的商业与医学内容生成，但合规性、事实准确性与受众个性化保障仍是主要挑战；作者希望验证“结构化数据架构”是否比“扩写提示词规则”更可扩展。

Method: 生产案例研究：构建基于Golden Data Model（GDM）的Composer内部工具。GDM覆盖4个产品、1,867条药品声明，整合声明元数据、参考文献、受众标签、物料类型、使用历史、Match Text Variant（MTV）链接及六维质量评分（6C）；在检索阶段进行结构化上下文过滤，并在6个“产品—受众”细分上评估。

Result: 目标适应症+29%、目标内容类型+62%、目标受众+67%，6C质量评分+86%，声明复用+34%；部署期生产日志显示每周API成本下降29.5%。

Conclusion: 该系统与数据架构贡献表明：在受监管高风险领域中，为检索时过滤重构声明数据比扩张提示词规则更具可扩展性；论文同时记录了数据清洗、图谱链接与质量评分的工程路径，并将结果定位为描述性部署证据。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate domain-specific commercial & medical content (e.g., claims, statements, and promotional phrases) in regulated industries such as pharmaceuticals. However, ensuring its regulatory compliance, factual accuracy, and audience-specific personalization remains a major challenge. We report a production case study asking whether moving compliance and personalization signal from prompt-level rules into retrieval time structured context improves pharmaceutical claims generation, using Composer, a deployed internal tool built on a Golden Data Model (GDM) of 1,867 pharmaceutical claims spanning four products. The GDM integrates claim metadata, references, audience labels, material type, usage history, Match Text Variant (MTV) links, and a six dimension quality score (6C). Across six product-audience segments, the context pipeline improves intended indication (+29%), intended content type (+62%) and intended target audience (+67%), 6C quality score (+86%), and claim reuse (+34%). Production log analysis over the deployment period shows a 29.5% reduction in weekly API cost. We report this as descriptive deployment evidence. This work is a systems and data architecture contribution: it shows that in a regulated, high-stakes domain, restructuring claim data for retrieval-time filtering is a more scalable lever than expanding prompt-level rules, and documents the engineering path data cleaning, graph linking, and quality scoring.

</details>


### [213] [Jev-DLC: Decision-Level Continual Learning from Typed Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31521)
*Yangyang Feng,Jianle Gan,Zhuoyan Feng,Chengwei Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Jev-DLC：将 Jev 风格的类型化用户反馈（接受/撤销并指明所替代的决策）作为跨请求的决策级持续学习信号，用冻结打分器上方的有界互补通道存储并融合动作身份证据与偏好—被拒的定向关系，无需原始交互历史、不增加打分器调用、不做运行时语言模型反向传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个体偏好往往只在部署后才显现——用户接受某决策或撤销它以选择另一个。既有持续个性化缺乏对这类显式、带类型反馈的建模，且需要区分"纠正对后续决策的实际影响"，因此作者希望把显式类型化纠正转化为紧凑的因果状态，并配套能按时间顺序评估纠正后续效应的基准。

Method: 1) 将每次纠正同时视为关于重复出现动作身份的证据，以及偏好项与被拒项之间的定向关系；2) 在冻结打分器之上的有界互补通道中保存这两类信号，并融合其得分以影响下一次决策；3) 约束：不保留原始交互历史、不额外调用打分器、运行时不进行语言模型反向传播；4) 构建 TCPA-Bench 时序评测套件，仅通过纠正对后续决策的影响来计入其贡献。

Result: 在 TCPA-Repeat 上，Jev-DLC 总体用户宏平均准确率为 44.83%，比 Recent-4 历史回放高 2.43 个百分点；在至少两次早期反馈事件之后达到 56.07%，相对该深度下最强报告基线领先 7.54 个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果表明，显式的类型化纠正可被压缩为用于持续个性化的因果状态；仅保留纠正的定向与身份信号即可在低开销、无历史存储条件下持续提升决策质量，且纠正的价值应以其对后续决策的影响来衡量。

Abstract: Individual preferences often become visible only after deployment, when a user accepts one decision or revokes it in favor of another. Jev-style typed interfaces make this feedback explicit by identifying the selected action and, under revocation, the decision it replaces. We study how these consequences persist across requests as Decision-Level Continual Learning from Typed Feedback (Jev-DLC). Jev-DLC treats each correction as both evidence about a recurring action identity and a directed relation between the preferred and rejected choices. It preserves these signals in complementary bounded channels above a frozen scorer and combines their scores to shape the next decision. The method retains no raw interaction history, adds no scorer call, and performs no runtime language-model backward pass. We also introduce TCPA-Bench, a chronological suite that credits a correction only through its effect on later decisions. On TCPA-Repeat, Jev-DLC reaches 44.83% user-macro accuracy overall, 2.43 points above Recent-4 history replay. After at least two earlier feedback events, it reaches 56.07%, a 7.54-point margin over the strongest reported comparison at this depth. These results show how explicit typed corrections can become compact causal state for continual personalization.

</details>


### [214] [Beyond Causal Masks: Auditing and Localizing Hidden Future-Token Leakage in Adaptive Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31522)
*Huiting Liu,Jinsong Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 隐藏的未来token泄漏可造成虚假架构增益：一种自适应残差路由解码器在teacher-forced评估下loss从3.5958降至1.4259，但前缀不变性审计失败、自由生成困惑度显著升高；泄漏源于经动态路由查询路径广播的全序列摘要。两个独立训练的12层因果修复消除泄漏后，loss与困惑度回到对照水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归模型可能在teacher-forced评估下表现优异，却在自由运行生成时退化。作者怀疑这类"虚假架构增益"源于隐藏的未来token泄漏，并在一具24层自适应残差路由解码器上系统调查该失效模式，强调仅凭teacher-forced loss判断架构进展的风险。

Method: 采用前缀不变性契约、最大前缀输出差异（LeakScore）、互补行为指标与逐层剖析作为诊断工具；通过逐层诊断与实现检查定位泄漏路径；训练两个独立的12层因果修复版本；辅以六组合成实现配对（跨五个模块族）、干净预训练对照、受控故障注入及跨数值精度的缓存一致性实验。

Result: 异常24层检查点验证损失1.4259（基线3.5958）但未通过前缀不变性审计，生成期参考模型困惑度显著更高；泄漏定位为全序列摘要经动态路由查询路径广播，尽管因果注意力掩码本身正确。两个12层因果修复消除可检测泄漏，验证损失与参考困惑度恢复至对照尺度，虚假优势消失。

Conclusion: 隐藏的未来token访问会产生误导性的架构进展结论；更低的teacher-forced损失应结合全模型因果检查与自由运行评估共同解读，前缀不变性审计是识别此类泄漏的必要手段。

Abstract: Hidden future-token leakage can create false architectural gains: an autoregressive model may appear substantially better under teacher-forced evaluation while its free-running generation deteriorates. We investigate this failure in an adaptive residual-routing decoder whose sequence-level routing summary exposes prefix positions to future tokens despite a correct causal attention mask. An anomalous 24-layer checkpoint reduces validation loss from the baseline's 3.5958 to 1.4259, yet it fails a prefix-invariance audit and has substantially higher reference-model perplexity during generation. Layer-wise diagnostics and implementation inspection trace the violation to a full-sequence summary broadcast through the dynamic routing-query path. Two independently trained 12-layer causal repairs eliminate detectable leakage and return validation loss and reference perplexity to the control scale, removing the large apparent advantage. As diagnostic tools, we use the established prefix-invariance contract, maximum prefix-output discrepancy (LeakScore), complementary behavioral metrics, and layer-wise profiles. Supporting experiments cover six synthetic implementation pairs spanning five module families, clean pretrained controls, controlled fault injection, and cache consistency across numerical precisions. These results illustrate how hidden future-token access can produce misleading claims of architectural progress and why lower teacher-forced loss should be interpreted together with whole-model causal checks and free-running evaluation.

</details>


### [215] [Contestable Agents for Data Governance: Recording Human Stewardship of AI-Generated Metadata Across Structured and Unstructured Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31553)
*Humza Akhtar,Brian Freeman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"可争辩治理"(contestable governance)框架：由LLM专家智能体为存在争议的元数据决策生成带评分与引用的论证，数据管理员可接受、拒绝、修改或补充论证后再提交，治理记录保留证据、干预与结果；配合版本化领域词表、分级triage路径与经验沉淀门控，并以两个HVAC用例（基于Brick Schema的点位分类、保修失效模式判定）说明该模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业组织中运行知识分散于结构化记录（遥测、点位表、事务记录）与非结构化来源（手册、服务记录、通信、巡检报告），但治理项目通常分而治之，分类、关联与文档化高度依赖少数资深领域专家。LLM智能体虽可提出元数据、引用文档并标记质量问题，但未经审查的建议会把错误扩散至下游系统，而流于形式的"一键批准"无法留下审查痕迹，缺乏问责与可追溯性。

Method: 构建"可争辩治理"工作流：1) 专家智能体针对争议性元数据决策产出带评分、带引用的论证；2) 管理员在提交前可接受、拒绝、修改或新增论证；3) 治理记录留存证据、干预与最终结果；4) 版本化领域词表向智能体提供模型特定术语与缩写定义；5) 设计triage路径，将完整论证机制仅用于不确定度高的提案，同时在提交前保留人工审查；6) 定义评分模型与记录模型，并设置门控机制决定哪些已核实决策可成为先例。

Result: 以两个HVAC用例验证该模式：其一为采用Brick Schema类别标签的点位分类，并提供开源参考实现；其二为保修失效模式判定示例，将索赔字段与遥测数据关联至服务记录和带版本公告。

Conclusion: 将LLM智能体定位为"可争辩的建议者"而非自动决策者，通过论证、人工干预记录与先例门控，可在保持提交前人工审查的前提下实现元数据治理的可追溯与可问责，并具备向其他工业领域迁移的潜力。

Abstract: Industrial organizations hold operational knowledge in structured records, such as telemetry, point lists, and transactions, and in unstructured sources, such as manuals, service notes, communications, and inspection reports. Governance programs often manage these sources separately. Classifying, linking, and documenting them depends on a small group of employees with deep domain knowledge. Agents built on large language models can propose metadata, cite documents, and flag quality issues, but unreviewed proposals can spread errors into downstream systems. A perfunctory approval leaves no record of what a reviewer checked. We present contestable governance: specialist agents produce scored, cited arguments for disputed metadata decisions; a steward can accept, reject, modify, or add arguments before commit; and a governance record retains the evidence, interventions, and outcome. A versioned domain glossary supplies model-specific terms and acronym definitions to the agents. We describe the workflow, a triage path that reserves full argumentation for uncertain proposals while preserving review before commit, the scoring and record models, and a gate that controls which verified decisions become precedent. Two HVAC use cases illustrate the pattern: point classification using Brick Schema class labels, supported by an open reference implementation, and an illustrative warranty failure-mode decision linking claim fields and telemetry to service notes and revisioned bulletins.

</details>


### [216] [What Makes a Good Unmasking Policy? A Controlled Study of Learned Unmasking in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31576)
*Jinya Sakurai,Xun Xu,Jaehong Yoon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将监督式解掩码策略视为对一种解码过程的蒸馏，系统拆解教师标签、模仿状态、策略输入与NFE预算四个设计维度，提出"轨迹收缩"目标训练856K参数策略，在LLaDA-8B与Dream-7B上取得更优精度—NFE前沿与吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散语言模型并行生成，效率取决于哪些预测被提交；已有学习式解掩码策略在监督信号、输入与容量上各不相同，难以辨析真正起作用的因素。

Method: 以"策略蒸馏解码过程"为框架，独立消融四个因素：(1)教师标注何种预测为可提交；(2)策略在哪些状态上模仿；(3)策略可观测的信息（置信度/隐状态）；(4)需降低多少NFE以抵消策略开销。据此提出轨迹收缩目标——仅接受仍停留在参考轨迹上的提交，输入结合置信度与隐状态。

Result: "匹配最终输出"的标注在低NFE下过于宽松；隐状态单独较弱但可补充置信度。约10%的NFE削减即可抵消最高7.5M参数策略的开销。856K参数策略在GSM8K上取得最佳精度—NFE前沿与最高吞吐，在MATH-500、HumanEval中等目标下具竞争力，并较大程度迁移至Dream-7B-Instruct。

Conclusion: 解码策略的关键在于标签定义与状态选择而非容量；轨迹收缩标注配合置信度+隐状态可在小参数下实现高效高精的解掩码。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models generate tokens in parallel, but their efficiency depends on which predictions are committed. Learned unmasking policies improve the speed--accuracy trade-off, yet differ in supervision, inputs, and capacity, obscuring which choices matter. We view supervised policies as distilling a decoding procedure through a teacher and examine independently what the teacher labels as committable, on which states the policy imitates it, what the policy observes, and how much the number of function evaluations (NFE) must decrease to offset policy overhead. We find that labeling predictions matching the final output is overly permissive at low NFE, and hidden states are weak alone but can complement confidence. About a 10% NFE reduction suffices to offset policy overhead for policies up to 7.5M parameters across tested settings. We therefore train a policy on trajectory-contraction targets, which accept only commitments staying on a reference trajectory, using confidence and hidden states. On LLaDA-8B-Instruct, the resulting 856K-parameter policy achieves the best accuracy--NFE frontier and highest throughput on GSM8K, is competitive at moderate targets on MATH-500 and HumanEval, and largely transfers to Dream-7B-Instruct.

</details>


### [217] [Who Is Sycophantic to Whom? Confident LLMs, Human Deference, and the Collapse of Trust Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31579)
*Deepthika S,Pawni Yadav,B. Sankar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文反向探究"谄媚"：当LLM自信、前后一致但事实错误时，用户是否会向其妥协。32名被试与同一模型（GPT-5-mini）构建的5个助手学习科学主题，助手事实被替换为可信谬误的比例分别为0/25/50/75/100%，且均以完全自信口吻陈述。104次测验、1040个回答显示：成绩从7.4降至2.9（满分10），被替换项被选中率60%（正确教学项仅11%，OR=12.5）；助手每陈述一次谬误，约65%被采纳且各替换水平一致，被吸收的谬误以高置信（8.4/10）持有，置信度对正确性的区分力（AUROC 0.70）降至随机水平，而事后信任评分几乎不变。作者提出基线校正的"人类顺从指数"（Human Deference Index），主张可信性是"人-AI配对"的属性、谄媚评测应双向测量、学习界面需逐条呈现可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有谄媚（sycophancy）研究多关注模型是否迎合用户偏好，却忽视反向问题：用户是否会迎合自信但错误的模型。该方向对AI辅助学习场景的可信性与界面设计具有直接意义，且缺乏对"人类侧谄媚"的量化基线。

Method: 被试内实验设计：32名参与者通过聊天学习最多5个科学主题，助手均由GPT-5-mini构建，其简报中事实被可信谬误替换的比例分别设为0%、25%、50%、75%、100%，所有助手均以完全自信语气陈述。每主题后进行突袭测验，选项包含正确事实、植入谬误及两个干扰项，并对每个答案作置信度评分。辅以对话记录编码（transcript coding）分析谬误陈述与采纳的对应关系、回访行为及把测验题贴回对话的比例，并提出基线校正的人类顺从指数。

Result: 104次测验、1,040个回答中，得分由7.4降至2.9（满分10）；植入谬误项被选率60%，正确教学项仅11%（OR=12.5）。对话编码显示助手每陈述一次谬误，约65%情形被采纳，且该比例在各替换水平下基本恒定。被吸收的谬误以高置信持有（8.4/10）；正确答案相对错误答案的置信度优势消失，置信度对正确性的区分力由AUROC 0.70降至随机水平；任务后信任评分几乎无变化。助手越差，被试回访聊天越多，但回访伴随更多谬误吸收而非更少；全部对话轮次中30%是将测验题贴回被检验来源。助手本身几乎从不谄媚，在质疑下也从不退让。

Conclusion: 人类侧存在显著的"顺从型谄媚"：自信语气使谬误被高置信吸收并摧毁置信度的校准价值，而主观信任评分对此不敏感。作者据此提出基线校正的人类顺从指数，主张可信性应视为人-AI配对的属性、谄媚评测须双向测量，学习类界面应逐条使可靠性可见（reliability legible claim by claim）。

Abstract: Research on sycophancy asks whether large language models (LLMs) bend towards what their users want to hear. We ask the reverse question: when an LLM is confident, consistent, and wrong, do users bend towards it? In a within-subjects experiment, 32 participants learned up to five science topics by chatting with five assistants built on the same model (GPT-5-mini). Unknown to participants, each assistant's briefing had 0, 25, 50, 75, or 100% of its facts replaced by plausible falsehoods, and every assistant spoke with full confidence. A surprise quiz after each topic offered the correct fact, the planted falsehood, and two distractors, with a confidence rating for every answer. Across 104 quizzes and 1,040 answers, scores fell from 7.4 to 2.9 out of 10, and the planted option was chosen on 60% of falsified items against 11% of correctly taught ones (odds ratio 12.5). Transcript coding showed that whenever the assistant stated a falsehood, it was adopted about 65% of the time at every falsification level. Absorbed falsehoods were held with high confidence (8.4/10), the modeled confidence advantage of right over wrong answers vanished, and the discrimination of confidence for correctness fell from an AUROC of 0.70 to chance, while post-task trust ratings barely moved. Participants tended to return to the chat more when the assistant was worse, but returning went with more absorption, not less, and 30% of all turns pasted quiz questions back to the source being checked. The assistant itself was almost never sycophantic and never conceded under pushback. We introduce a baseline-corrected Human Deference Index to quantify this human-side sycophancy and argue that trustworthiness is a property of the human-AI pair, that sycophancy evaluations should measure both directions, and that learning interfaces must make reliability legible claim by claim.

</details>


### [218] [Simulated Control Evals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31593)
*Jeffrey Olmo,David Zhang,Tyler Tracy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"模拟控制评估"(SCEs)：用语言模型模拟工具响应替代真实环境，使控制评估可在任意部署环境中进行。在LinuxArena上，SCE预测安全性的RMSE为3.6个百分点，显著优于BashArena的10.1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统控制评估依赖专门构建的控制设置，并将其结果外推到部署环境；但部署环境中环境与任务特征会影响防护措施的有效性，这些特征在控制设置中往往无法体现，因此需要一种能在更广泛部署环境中直接开展控制评估的方法。

Method: 引入模拟控制评估（SCEs），用语言模型模拟工具响应以替代真实执行环境。在LinuxArena上进行验证，以真实执行为ground truth，并与另一独立控制设置BashArena的预测误差以及LinuxArena内部指标变异进行比较，同时分析攻击策略分布、攻击者推理、监控者推理的KL散度及样本效率。

Result: 对独立采样的评估与部署任务集，SCEs在5%审计预算、五种监控者下预测安全性的RMSE为3.6个百分点，BashArena为10.1；SCEs能复现攻击策略、攻击者推理与监控者推理的分布，其与真实执行的KL散度比BashArena低十倍以上；SCEs可解释78–85%的环境间攻击成功率、监控分数与安全性方差；样本效率高，用不到部署任务集5%的任务对抽样即优于BashArena；模拟发现的策略还能提升监控者在留出LinuxArena攻击上的表现，效果与执行得到的策略改进大致相当。

Conclusion: 面向具体部署环境的模拟控制评估比在独立控制设置中执行的控制评估更具预测性，可在缺乏现成控制设置的部署环境中高效、准确地开展控制评估。

Abstract: Control evaluations measure whether safeguards can prevent an AI model from causing undesired outcomes even when it is intentionally trying to do so. These evaluations typically use purpose-built control settings and extrapolate their results to a model's deployment. However, the effectiveness of safeguards in deployment can depend on features of environments and tasks that control settings do not represent. To broaden where control evaluations can be run, we introduce simulated control evaluations (SCEs), which use a language model to simulate tool responses in place of an environment. We evaluate SCEs on LinuxArena, where execution provides ground-truth outcomes. We compare SCE prediction errors with those from a separate control setting, BashArena, and with variation in control metrics within LinuxArena. For independently sampled evaluation and deployment task sets, SCEs predict safety with a root mean squared error of 3.6 percentage points across five monitors at a 5% audit budget, compared with 10.1 for BashArena. SCEs closely reproduce distributions of attack strategies, attacker reasoning, and monitor reasoning, with over tenfold lower KL divergence from execution than BashArena. SCEs explain 78-85% of the between-environment variance in attack success, monitor scores, and safety. This accuracy is sample efficient: SCEs predict safety with less error than BashArena using fewer than 5% as many task-pair draws as the deployment task set. Strategies discovered through simulation also improve monitor performance on held-out LinuxArena attacks, outperforming BashArena and approximately matching improvement from executed strategies. These results suggest that deployment-specific SCEs can be more predictive than control evaluations executed in separate control settings.

</details>


### [219] [EpiSEM-Bench: Evaluating Safety and Evidence Recovery in LLM Agents under Missing Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31650)
*Qijin Zhang,Hanzhen Wang,Yuehan Zhao,Haoyu Dai,Yao Shu,Zhongxiang Dai,Shuang Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 \bench{}，一个可执行基准，用于评估 LLM 智能体在关键证据缺失条件下的不安全执行、安全边界与证据恢复能力，包含六个领域 300 对匹配的不安全/安全任务，并配以独立审计工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具调用型语言模型智能体依赖环境事实判断动作是否被允许。当工具响应遗漏安全相关事实时，即使用户请求无害、返回陈述均真实，智能体仍可能执行被禁止的动作，现有基准缺乏对此类"证据缺失导致的不安全执行"的系统评估。

Method: 构建 \bench{} 可执行基准，含 300 对匹配的不安全/安全任务，覆盖六个领域；通过系统性抑制关键事实，测量关键证据遗漏下的不安全执行，并确定最小观测失败基数（包括证据完备下的失败及穷尽测试后无观测失败的案例）；使用独立审计工具检验智能体是否主动寻求缺失事实、在完成允许任务的同时扣留被禁止动作；在十个模型上进行评测。

Result: 十个模型在遗漏鲁棒性、证据获取与恰当行动上存在显著差异；对缺失证据的抵抗力并不必然转化为有用的恢复能力；提供恢复访问可降低全部十个模型的不安全执行率，但各智能体在是否主动获取缺失事实并将其用于两种情形下的恰当行动上表现不同。

Conclusion: 证据缺失是智能体安全评估中被忽视的关键维度；抵抗缺失证据与有效恢复是两种可分离的能力，需分别度量；\bench{} 为安全边界与证据恢复研究提供可执行、可审计的评测基础。

Abstract: Tool-using language model agents rely on environmental facts to decide whether an action is permitted. When a tool response omits a safety-relevant fact, an agent may carry out a prohibited action even though the user request is benign and every returned statement is true. We introduce \bench{}, an executable benchmark for evaluating unsafe execution, safety boundaries, and evidence recovery in LLM agents under missing evidence. It contains 300 matched unsafe/safe task pairs across six domains. By systematically suppressing critical facts, the benchmark measures unsafe execution under critical evidence omission and determines the minimum observed failure cardinality, including failures with full evidence and cases with no observed failure after exhaustive testing. An independent audit tool tests whether agents seek missing facts and then withhold prohibited actions while completing permissible tasks. The benchmark reveals substantial variation across the ten models in omission robustness, evidence acquisition, and appropriate action. Resistance to missing evidence does not necessarily translate into useful recovery. Recovery access reduces unsafe execution for all ten models, but agents differ in whether they actively obtain the missing facts and use them to act appropriately in both worlds.

</details>


### [220] [Direct Pass@$k$ Optimization for Thought Pruning: Balancing Quality and Diversity in Test-Time Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31667)
*Mahdi Salmani,Adel Javanmard,Devansh Gupta,Meisam Razaviyayn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种以优化 pass@k 为目标的候选池剪枝方法，使质量与多样性通过目标函数本身自然平衡，在八个开源推理模型上优于现有质量型与多样性型剪枝策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时扩展需生成大量候选解并跑到完成，计算开销大，因此在生成短前缀后剪枝。现有剪枝分两类：质量型（打分并丢弃预测错误的部分解）与多样性型（保留不同推理轨迹以覆盖不同最终解）。二者实为探索-利用的权衡，但既有工作几乎未显式暴露并优化这一权衡。

Method: 引入一种直接针对该权衡的剪枝流程：以选中解池上的 pass@k 为优化目标，选择使"至少有一个部分解续写至完成后得到正确答案"概率最大的子集。该目标同时奖励高质量部分解、惩罚冗余，从而由准则本身而非分离的启发式设计自然平衡质量与多样性。

Result: 在八个开源权重推理模型上，方法优于现有的质量型与多样性型剪枝策略；当保留中等数量的部分解继续生成时，增益尤为显著。

Conclusion: 直接以 pass@k 为目标进行剪枝，可将质量-多样性权衡显式纳入优化，优于分离式启发设计，说明剪枝阶段直接平衡二者具有实际价值。

Abstract: Test-time scaling has emerged as a key inference strategy for reasoning models, where a model generates a large number of candidate solutions in the hope of finding at least one correct solution. Running all candidate solutions to completion can be computationally demanding, so recent work prunes the candidate pool after generating only a short prefix, committing further computation only to the surviving candidates. Existing pruning strategies divide broadly into two categories: Quality-based methods assign a score to each partial solution and discard those predicted to ultimately lead to an incorrect final solution, while diversity-based methods select partial solutions that follow different reasoning trajectories and are therefore likely to cover different final solutions. The tension between these two objectives is an instance of exploration versus exploitation, yet little to no prior work on pruning exposes this trade-off directly, let alone optimizes it. In this paper, we introduce a pruning procedure that addresses this trade-off directly by optimizing pass@$k$ over the selected solution pool. In particular, it chooses the pool to maximize the probability that at least one of its partial solutions, when continued to completion, yields a correct answer. Because this objective simultaneously rewards high-quality partial solutions and penalizes redundancy among them, quality and diversity are naturally balanced by the criterion itself rather than through separate ad-hoc design choices. On eight open-weight reasoning models, our method outperforms existing quality-based and diversity-based pruning strategies. The gains are particularly pronounced when the pruning procedure keeps a moderate number of partial solutions for continued generation, highlighting the value of directly balancing quality and diversity during pruning.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [221] [Agentic Verified Optimization of Real-World Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30188)
*Zhe Ye,Udaya Ghai,Zhizhen Qin,Dawn Song,Soonho Kong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出"智能体验证优化"(agentic verified optimization)：智能体在加速程序的同时给出机器可检验的等价性证明，并构建Aqueduct框架将C/C++/Go/Rust程序迁移到Lean 4进行验证优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有等价性检验多依赖测试，智能体可通过迎合测试而不保持预期行为（作弊/奖励黑客）。而验证代码生成面临规格书写瓶颈：需要额外编写形式化规格。以既有实现作为规格可绕开该瓶颈，且定理证明的近期进展使证明变得可行。

Method: 在Lean 4中于函数级与仓库级形式化该任务（程序可执行亦可推理）。因现实应用鲜有用Lean编写，开发Aqueduct智能体框架，将C、C++、Go、Rust程序迁移为原生Lean代码，并通过与原程序的差分测试验证每个移植版本的正确性。

Result: 在Verina的189个参考实现上，智能体为156个生成了更快且带证明的重写，含指数递归改动态规划等算法级改动；在17个迁移应用上，92个带证明重写使51个应用场景中的47个获得加速。应用级收益小于函数级收益。

Conclusion: 当前智能体已能在独立函数与迁移自真实程序的应用上执行验证优化；应用级增益较小，使"优化哪些函数"的选择成为重要问题。文中讨论了剩余挑战及走向生产级验证优化的步骤。

Abstract: Program optimization aims to improve performance while preserving behavior. Coding agents can already make programs faster by changing algorithms, data structures, and execution strategies. However, existing equivalence checks mostly rely on tests, which agents have been shown to exploit, passing the tests without preserving the intended behavior. We propose agentic verified optimization, in which an agent produces a faster implementation together with a machine-checked proof of equivalence to the existing one. Because the existing implementation serves as the specification, the task avoids the specification-writing bottleneck of verified code generation, and recent advances in theorem proving make the proofs attainable. We formulate the task at the function and repository levels in Lean 4, where programs can be both executed and reasoned about. Few real-world applications are written in Lean, so we develop Aqueduct, an agentic framework that migrates C, C++, Go, and Rust programs into native Lean, validates each port by differential testing against its original. On the 189 reference implementations in Verina, agents produce faster proved rewrites for 156, including algorithmic changes such as replacing exponential recursion with dynamic programming. On 17 migrated applications, their 92 proved rewrites speed up 47 of 51 application scenarios. These application-level gains are smaller than the function-level ones, making the choice of functions to optimize an important problem. Current agents can thus already carry out verified optimization on both standalone functions and applications migrated from real programs. We discuss the remaining challenges and the steps toward verified optimization in production.

</details>


### [222] [Detection Is Not Enough: Reasoning Traces as Testable Program Hypotheses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30200)
*Vaidehi Hegde*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究在无参考解、无评判模型、无权重更新条件下单条推理轨迹可被验证的程度：以冻结的LLaMA-2-7B-chat在GSM8K上实验，发现瓶颈不在检测而在修复——自一致性检测AUROC仅0.741且存在结构性上限，候选衍生的蜕变探针虽能暴露部分错误，但修复率过低使任何策略均无显著精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型部署时无参考解，推理轨迹是判断答案的唯一证据。现有无参考替代方案各有缺陷：自评估会损害其所保护的答案；采样一致性衡量的是稳定性而非正确性；扰动研究从人工模板读取期望答案，评的是模型而非轨迹。因此需要探究单条轨迹在无参考、无评判模型、无参数更新下的可验证上限。

Method: 在冻结的LLaMA-2-7B-chat与GSM8K上：(1) 以轨迹自洽性构建检测器并测其AUROC；(2) 分析错误轨迹的类型分布以定位检测上限的结构性来源；(3) 提出候选衍生的蜕变探针——将轨迹抽象为关于题目量的程序，扰动其中一个量、执行程序并将改写题再问模型一次，使每条轨迹自带测试；(4) 给出重试决策的判据：仅当检测精度超过 D/(R+D)（R、D分别为重试修复错误轨迹与损害正确轨迹的比率）时flag才值得重试。

Result: 自一致性检测AUROC为0.741并因结构性原因停滞：三分之二的错误轨迹不含错误方程（正确执行了错误计划），且在错误计划类失败上检测器标记错误轨迹的频率低于正确轨迹。蜕变探针在1,319道测试题中937题（71.0%）存在有效扰动；在有可解析首条轨迹的847题中，被支持轨迹的正确率为60.6%，基线为31.6%，但探针同时反驳了45.5%的正确轨迹。所有测试策略均未带来统计显著的精度提升。按修复率估算，即使检测器完美，同等重试预算下仅能挽回约66/1,319题。

Conclusion: 检测并非该流程的瓶颈，修复才是开放问题。轨迹自洽性检测受错误计划类失败的结构性限制，蜕变探针可提供轨迹自身测试但误否决率高、修复增益有限；低修复率使再强的检测器也难以转化为精度提升。未来应聚焦于修复机制而非检测器改进。

Abstract: When a language model is deployed, no reference solution exists, so the reasoning trace it writes is the only evidence for judging its answer. The reference-free substitutes each fail differently: self-assessment degrades the answer it was meant to protect, sampling agreement measures stability rather than correctness, and perturbation studies read the expected answer off a human-written template, grading a model rather than a trace. We ask how far a single trace can be verified with no reference solution, no judge model and no weight update, and find, for a frozen LLaMA-2-7B-chat on GSM8K, that detection is not what limits such a pipeline. Checking a trace against itself reaches an AUROC of 0.741 and stops there for a structural reason: two thirds of wrong traces state no false equation, having executed a mistaken plan correctly, and on wrong-plan failures the detector flags a wrong trace less often than a right one. A candidate-derived metamorphic probe exposes many of these failures: we abstract the trace into a program over the question's quantities, perturb one quantity, execute the program, and ask the model the rewritten question once, so every trace supplies its own test. Valid perturbations exist for 937 of 1,319 test questions (71.0%); among the 847 that also have a parseable first trace, supported traces are correct 60.6% of the time against a 31.6% base rate, although the probe also refutes 45.5% of correct traces. No tested policy yields a statistically significant improvement in accuracy. A flag earns its retry only when detector precision exceeds D/(R+D), where R and D are the rates at which a retry repairs a wrong trace and damages a correct one; our repair rate is so low that, at the same retry budget, even a perfect detector would recover an estimated 66 of 1,319 questions. Repair, not detection, is the open problem.

</details>


### [223] [A Consolidated Deployment Edition of a Microservice Core-Banking Platform: Composing Services by Reference into Three Hosts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30212)
*Tomisin Fagbola*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文以某多租户 .NET 核心银行平台的“第二部署版本”为对象，把五个 API 服务合并为单一 API 主机，并与原有九任务 ECS Fargate 版本对比：合并版暴露全部 844 个操作，47 个金融负载用例结果与账本一致，读吞吐为原版 2.3 倍、内存不足一半，服务任务计算成本估算降低 11–24%，但故障域扩大；16 个注入故障中检查检出 10 个、行为测试另检出 5 个，1 个逃逸。结论限于单平台与模拟部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务虽带来独立部署与扩缩容优势，但每个服务都需预留算力并增加运维负担。作者希望在大体保留原有服务代码与构建流程的前提下，考察“按项目引用合并为少数宿主”这一替代部署形态是否能降低资源开销，并量化其在功能一致性、性能与成本、故障可检测性方面的权衡。

Method: 在同一多租户 .NET 核心银行平台上构建第二部署版本：原版为九个 AWS ECS Fargate 任务；合并版通过项目引用将五个 API 服务组合进一个 API 主机，并另设消费者主机与后台主机。服务代码仍留在原仓库，两个版本由相同提交构建。论文描述了组合机制、模块清单、启动检查、请求处理与配置优先级，并在模拟 AWS 部署（镜像其拓扑、任务规格与配置）中进行对比：覆盖全部声明操作、47 个金融负载用例、五类主体的读探针与跨租户/写授权用例，以及 16 个注入故障的检出分析。

Result: 合并版暴露全部 844 个声明操作；47 个金融负载用例中两版本观察结果相同、账本一致；五类主体读探针的 231 个端点结果中 230 个一致，唯一差异与模拟负载均衡器相关；11 个跨租户与写授权用例两版本均符合预期。模拟环境中合并版读吞吐为原版 2.3 倍，内存占用不足一半；按目录价估算服务任务计算成本降低 11–24%（不含共享网关、负载均衡器、数据存储等运营成本）。代价是故障域扩大：一次 API 主机崩溃会使五个模块全部离线。16 个注入故障中，启动/健康检查类检出 10 个，行为测试额外检出 5 个，仍有 1 个逃逸。

Conclusion: 将五个 API 服务按项目引用合并到单一宿主，可在保持操作覆盖率、业务结果与授权行为一致的前提下，显著提升读吞吐、降低内存与计算成本，代价是故障域扩大与部分故障需依赖行为测试而非结构性检查。作者强调结论仅来自单个平台及其拓扑、任务规格与配置的模拟部署，并非生产 AWS 实测，外推需谨慎。

Abstract: Microservices give teams independent deployment and scaling, but each deployed service also reserves compute and adds operational work. This paper examines a second deployment edition of a multi-tenant .NET core-banking platform. The existing edition runs as nine AWS ECS Fargate tasks; the alternative composes the five API services by project reference into one API host, alongside a consumer host and a background host. The service code remains in its original repositories, and both editions continue to be built from the same commits. We describe the composition mechanism, module manifest, start-up checks, request handling and configuration precedence, then compare the editions in an emulation of the AWS deployment. The composed edition exposes all 844 declared operations. Across 47 financial workload cases, both editions produced the same observed outcomes and matching ledgers. In read probes across five principal types, 230 of 231 endpoint results matched; the remaining difference was associated with the emulated load balancer. Eleven cross-tenant and write-authorisation cases also produced the expected outcomes in both editions. In the emulation, the composed edition delivered 2.3 times the read throughput and used less than half the memory. Estimated service-task compute at list prices was 11-24% lower, excluding the shared gateway, load balancer, data stores and other operating costs. The consolidation also enlarges the failure domain: one API-host crash takes all five modules offline. Of 16 injected faults, the edition's checks detected 10, behavioural tests detected five additional faults, and one fault escaped both. These findings concern one platform and an emulated deployment that mirrors its topology, task sizes and configuration; they are not production-AWS measurements.

</details>


### [224] [JANUS: Agentic Cross-Language Fuzzing Harness Generation for Android Native Libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30218)
*Xiangyu Zhang,Yucheng Su,Lingling Fan,Yinan Li,Sen Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Modern Android apps extensively rely on native libraries, bridging Java/Kotlin and C/C++ through the Java Native Interface (JNI). This cross-language interoperation, however, exposes the memory-safe Android Runtime to the unsafe C/C++ world, creating an attack surface that has been repeatedly exploited for full device compromise. While fuzzing is a proven technique for vetting native libraries, state-of-the-art harness generation tools struggle to faithfully capture closed-source cross-language API usage: (i) precise program analysis across the JNI boundary does not scale, whereas lightweight approximations miss critical invocation patterns; and (ii) none tackles the construction of complex composite objects consumed by JNI methods.
  In this paper, we propose JANUS, the first agentic approach to synthesizing cross-language JNI-aware fuzzing harnesses for Android native libraries. JANUS iteratively resolves both explicit and implicit cross-language call dependencies to recover app-specific runtime context, and recursively assembles cross-language argument objects in a bottom-up manner. JANUS leverages an autonomous agentic workflow to reduce reliance on hand-crafted heuristics, capturing the vast landscape of hidden API usage knowledge embedded in closed-source native libraries. Evaluation on 44 popular real-world apps (such as Google and YouTube Music, covering 21,386 JNI methods total) shows that JANUS produces over 5.9 times as many valid harnesses (6,094) as state-of-the-art automatic harnessing tools. On pre-installed apps from five mainstream Android vendors (e.g., Xiaomi, Vivo, Samsung), JANUS further uncovers 6 previously unknown vulnerabilities in closed-source native libraries.

</details>


### [225] [Agentic Architecture of Information Systems. The AAM/5C Model: A Reference Model for Distributed Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30221)
*Laurent Goujon*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该专著提出AAM/5C概念参考模型（五种能力×七个逻辑层），以单一授权规则R-AUT分离提议、决策与执行者，并给出十条假设（H1–H10）的可反驳性设计与验证程序，但报告没有实证结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型组织面临“认知复杂性之墙”：当系统规模达到数百个交互组件时，维护依赖图并跟上事件响应节奏已超出人类团队能力，需要一种规范性的参考模型来组织已知机制。

Method: 概念性建模与处方性设计：将Capture、Comprehend、Choose、Coordinate、Control五种能力实例化到L1–L7七个逻辑层；用R-AUT规则约束流程——L4概率规划器提议、L6治理层在签名策略下决策并记录签名评估证明、L5安全层据此签发短时效期act token、策略执行点在目标层实施前验证二者；提供Rego授权策略与对等共识的部分TLA+规约；在测量前固定可反驳H1–H10（含H7b日志完备性、H8b执行点不可绕过）的协议；H7a、H8a在明示保证契约下按构造成立。

Result: 无实证结果。贡献为三点新构造——分布式自主循环的Validation步骤、瞬时权威面（ephemeral authority surface）、认知依赖平面（cognitive dependency plane）——并将模型与自主计算、多智能体系统、企业架构及MCP/A2A等智能体协议进行定位对比；同时提出带量化里程碑的验证计划。

Conclusion: AAM/5C是一个有待验证的处方性参考模型：其价值在于以单条授权规则统一组织既有机制并提出可检验假设体系，所有新构造尚待实证。

Abstract: Large organizations increasingly face what this book calls the cognitive complexity wall: at the scale of hundreds of interacting components, maintaining the dependency map and reacting at the pace of incidents exceeds what a human team can deliver. This conceptual volume proposes AAM/5C, in which five capabilities (Capture, Comprehend, Choose, Coordinate, Control) are instantiated across seven logical layers, L1 to L7. A single authorization rule, R-AUT, separates who proposes, who decides and who executes: a probabilistic planner (L4) proposes; a governance layer (L6) decides under signed policies and records its verdict in a signed evaluation proof; a security layer (L5) issues a short-lived act token bound to that proof; a policy enforcement point verifies both before the target layer materializes the act. Under an explicit guarantee contract, two analytical statements (H7a, H8a) hold by construction. The other statements of its ten hypotheses (H1 to H10), including log completeness (H7b) and non-bypass of the enforcement point (H8b), are refutable, and the protocol that could refute them is fixed before any measurement. The contribution of the book is a prescriptive reference model that articulates, under this single authorization rule, known mechanisms and adds three constructs, all to be validated: the Validation step of the Distributed Autonomy Loop, the ephemeral authority surface and the cognitive dependency plane. The book positions them against autonomic computing, multi-agent systems, enterprise architecture and agent protocols (MCP, A2A), gives an authorization policy in Rego and a partial TLA+ specification of peer consensus, and sets out a validation program with quantified milestones. It reports no empirical results. This English edition is a translation of the French original, produced with the assistance of AI tools and validated by the author.

</details>


### [226] [The Customer Is the Builder: How AI Coding Agents Are Rewriting the Economics of Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30307)
*Ripon Chandra Malo,Zimeng Yu,Tong Qiu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出"软件替代前沿"概念，用基准测试外推与市场指标对比，发现AI编码代理能力快速提升（约129天翻倍），32%组织受访者已因自建能力放弃至少一次采购，但市场层面尚未出现明显购买转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码代理可能改变组织"买软件还是自建功能"的决策。作者聚焦具备领域知识的组织与专业采购者，试图刻画客户能够在可接受标准下以低于购买成本自建并维护的功能边界，即软件替代前沿，并检验其与市场变化的相对节奏。

Method: 定义"软件替代前沿"为可自建且成本低于购买的功能集合；利用公开数据测量领先代理在50%成功率下可完成任务长度（以人类工作时间计）的增长速率；广泛检索开源替代品（识别575个候选，部分功能匹配需独立验证）；以Craft套件12个应用仓库、12.0天公开开发作为自建可行性案例；结合投资份额、商业安装量与组织调查自报数据做市场检验。

Result: 自2023年起，领先代理50%成功率任务长度约每129天翻倍；识别575个潜在开源替代方案；Craft套件在12.0天内产出12个应用仓库，但仍属早期版本，完整性与可靠性未验证。市场证据混合：已购软件美国投资份额上升、跟踪的商业安装量增长，但32%的组织调查受访者报告曾因可用编码代理自建功能而放弃至少一次软件采购。

Conclusion: 自建能力可能在整体采购转移显现之前先影响个体购买决策，形成"预警性缺口"。但基准外推属趋势预测而非客户切换预测，自报数据亦不能确定实际被替代的支出或自建工具的可靠性，需进一步验证。

Abstract: AI coding agents could change whether organizations buy software or build the functionality they need. We focus on organizations and professional buyers whose domain knowledge can help them specify and evaluate tools for their work. We define the software substitution frontier as the functions customers could build and maintain at an acceptable standard for less than buying them. Using public data, we compare growing capacity to build with indicators of market change. Since 2023, the length of benchmark tasks that leading agents can complete at 50 percent success, measured in human working time, has doubled about every 129 days. A broad search identified 575 potential open-source alternatives to selected commercial products, although some functional matches still require independent validation. The Craft suite produced 12 application repositories over 12.0 days of public development, but these remain early versions with unverified completeness and reliability. Market evidence is mixed: purchased software gained US investment share and tracked commercial installations grew. Yet 32 percent of respondents to an organizational survey reported forgoing at least one software purchase because their organization could build the functionality with coding agents. These self-reports do not establish the spending displaced or the reliability of the resulting tools. The gap motivates a warning: customers' capacity to build could affect individual buying decisions before a broad purchasing shift becomes visible. We examine this possibility using benchmark projections and observable market tests. The projections are an extrapolation, not a forecast of customer switching.

</details>


### [227] [TrustedCode: Evaluating Large Language Models for Developing Trusted Code through Automated Program Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30432)
*Muhammad Danial,Muhammad Taimoor Khan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TrustedCode：基于LLM与Why3的AI生成Python代码自动形式化验证框架，集成思维链提示以完成类型检查、WhyML翻译与规范验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约30%的GitHub Python代码贡献由AI生成，其可靠性不足；形式化验证可建立正确性信任，但Python支持薄弱，且规范编写与程序验证本身门槛高，阻碍实际采用。

Method: 用LLM完成三步流程：(i) Python类型检查；(ii) 经Why3框架翻译为WhyML；(iii) 借助Why3后端自动定理证明器对照形式化规范验证。通过LangGraph集成思维链提示，识别并缓解LLM在规范生成与证明中的局限。

Result: 在10个不同复杂度的Python程序上实验；错误分析显示50%源于领域知识缺口，30%为语法不准确，20%为语义与类型相关问题。

Conclusion: 结果凸显针对LLM引导的Python形式化验证流程进行定向改进的必要性；工具已开源（github.com/mtaimoorkhan/TrustedCode）并提供演示视频。

Abstract: Recent reports, such as "Who is using AI to code?", indicate that approximately 30\% of Python code contributions to GitHub are AI-generated. Given the inherent unreliability of this code, formal verification emerges as a promising approach to establish trust in software correctness. Although leveraging large language models (LLMs) for formal verification has garnered increasing attention, practical adoption remains limited due to the challenges in writing formal specifications and verifying program correctness against them. Existing tools predominantly support verification for languages such as Java and C, with minimal support for Python. We present TrustedCode, an automated framework for verifying AI-generated Python code. TrustedCode uses LLMs to (i) type-check Python code, (ii) translate it into WhyML via the Why3 framework, and (iii) verify it against formal specifications using Why3 back-end automated theorem provers. Our method integrates chain-of-thought prompting through LangGraph to identify and address LLM limitations in specification generation and proving the specified programs. To evaluate TrustedCode, we conducted experiments on ten Python programs of varying complexity. Our analysis reveals that 50\% of the observed errors stem from domain knowledge gaps, 30\% from syntactic inaccuracies, and the remaining 20\% from semantic and type-related issues. These findings underscore the importance of targeted improvements in LLM-guided formal verification workflows for Python. TrustedCode is an open source tool available at: \href{github.com/mtaimoorkhan/TrustedCode}{github.com/mtaimoorkhan/TrustedCode}. The video demonstration is available at: \href{youtu.be/Nr5I2g8ec-s}{youtu.be/Nr5I2g8ec-s}

</details>


### [228] [Stale Instruction Files Override the Code in Coding Agents: A One-Sentence Fix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30571)
*Xiaonan Xu,Wenjing Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文实证测量编码智能体在指令文件与既有代码冲突时倾向于盲从指令文件的行为，并验证在用户级指令文件中加入一句"以既有代码为准"即可显著纠正该行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体（如 Claude Code、Codex CLI）会遵循项目指令文件（CLAUDE.md、AGENTS.md），即使其内容与既有代码不一致、甚至智能体已察觉冲突。作者希望量化这一行为，并检验一句用户指令能否让智能体改以既有代码为准。

Method: 构建40个Python项目，令指令文件要求一种约定而既有代码采用另一种（如print而非logging）。让Claude Code与Codex CLI为每个项目添加功能，分设"无优先句"与"用户级指令文件含'既有代码优先'一句"两种条件，共320次运行。

Result: 无该句时，90.1%的选择遵循过时的指令文件；Claude Code在80次运行中有79次指出冲突却仍遵循文件；Codex CLI在80次中有20次改写既有代码以匹配文件，其中17次导致项目自身测试失败。加入该句后，遵循代码的比例由4.2%升至91.4%，改写行为消失，测试通过率由89.4%升至100%。

Conclusion: 作者将该行为归因于两类信息的输入通道差异：指令文件以"指令"形式进入模型，代码以"工具输出"形式进入，而模型被训练为对后者赋予更低权重。用户当前即可添加该句，智能体与模型厂商也可将这一规则内置于产品中。

Abstract: Purpose: Coding agents follow a project's instruction file, such as CLAUDE.md or AGENTS.md, even when it disagrees with the existing code and even when they notice the disagreement. We measure this behaviour and test whether one sentence in the user's own instruction file makes agents follow the code instead.
  Methods: We built 40 Python projects in which the instruction file asks for one convention while the existing code uses another, for example print instead of logging. Claude Code and Codex CLI added a feature to each project, with and without a sentence in the user-level instruction file that gives the existing code precedence, in 320 runs.
  Results: Without the sentence, 90.1% of the agents' choices followed the stale file. Claude Code pointed out the conflict in 79 of 80 runs and still followed the file. Codex CLI rewrote the existing code to match the file in 20 of 80 runs, and the project's own tests then failed in 17 of them. The sentence raised the share of choices that follow the code from 4.2% to 91.4%, stopped the rewrites and raised the test pass rate from 89.4% to 100%.
  Conclusion: We attribute the behaviour to how agents receive the two sources: the instruction file reaches the model as an instruction and the code as tool output, which models are trained to rank lower. Users can add the sentence today, and makers of agents and models can build the same rule into their products.

</details>


### [229] [From Tokens to Credits to Outcomes: Pricing AI Across Models, Applications, and Cloud Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30701)
*Pratik Gupta*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对2023–2026年AI软件定价模式进行可复现的实证研究，基于公开定价页面与文档构建数据集与分类法，发现"订阅+用量配额"是AI原生公司主导模式（55%），混合模型应视为包装锚点、配额机制与计费单元的组态而非互斥类别，并据此提出自动化计费平台的需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SaaS按席位收费源于边际服务成本近乎为零，而模型推理具有非零边际成本且客户用量跨越数个数量级，由此催生预付额度、按量计费、席位+用量混合以及按结果定价等新模式。这些定价模型尚未被系统刻画，缺乏可复现的实证基础与对计费系统设计的指导。

Method: 采用可复现的公开定价资料（定价页面与文档）研究，覆盖三个队列：Forbes AI 50（2026）全样本作为AI原生核心样本、按书面规则纳入的销售AI服务的云平台、以及冻结的9个具特色设计的定向样本。对每个产品的定价原语与额度机制进行编码并附有日期的原始出处，构建公开数据集，归纳原语分类法及队列分布，并提炼自动化可配置计费平台的需求。

Result: AI原生公司中"订阅+用量配额"是可观测的主导模式（占可观测AI原生条目的55%；在混合边界情形的敏感性分析下为45–61%）；52个AI原生条目中有23个未公开定价。混合产品更适合理解为其包装锚点、配额机制与计费单元的组态，而非互斥类别。计费平台需求包括额度账本状态、结果定价的归因流水线，以及对高定价变更速度的配置支持。

Conclusion: AI软件定价正从席位制向多样化、可组合的原语体系迁移，且相当比例厂商不公开定价。研究提供了可复现的数据集、分类法与系统需求，为理解与工程化实现AI计费提供实证基础与起点。

Abstract: Between 2023 and 2026, AI changed how software is priced. Traditional SaaS charged per seat because serving one more user cost almost nothing. Model inference has non-zero marginal cost, and usage varies by orders of magnitude across customers, producing prepaid credits, metered usage, hybrid seat-plus-usage models, and outcome pricing that supplement, and in rare cases replace, seat-based pricing. These pricing models have not been systematically characterized. This paper presents a reproducible study of public pricing artifacts - pricing pages and documentation - across three cohorts: the full Forbes AI 50 (2026) as the AI-native core sample, cloud platforms selling AI services included by a documented rule, and a frozen purposive cohort of 9 distinctive designs. It contributes (1) a dataset coding each offering's pricing primitive and credit mechanics with dated primary sources, prepared for public release with the paper, (2) a taxonomy of the primitives in use and their distribution across cohorts, and (3) empirically grounded requirements for automated, configurable billing platforms, including ledger state for credits, attribution pipelines for outcome pricing, and configuration support for high pricing-change velocity. Subscription with usage quota is the dominant observable model among AI-native companies (55% of observable AI-native rows; 45-61% under sensitivity analysis of hybrid boundary cases). 23 of the 52 AI-native rows publish no pricing. Hybrid products are better understood as compositions of packaging anchor, allowance mechanism, and billable unit than as mutually exclusive categories. All artifacts are public; my employer is excluded from the sample.

</details>


### [230] [From Natural Language to Verified C: Verification-Guided Generation with ACSL Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30725)
*Zian Wang,Qin Li,Weisong Sun,Haibin Cai*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 VeriGen-C 框架，将 C 程序与 ACSL 规范作为统一工件联合合成，显著提升可验证率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 辅助代码生成放大了对自动化形式验证的需求，生成程序不仅需可执行，还需具备机器可检验的正确性证据。现有方法要么只合成功能合理的代码，要么仅从自然语言提示生成看似合理的规范，并未把实现与规范作为面向验证的统一工件进行协同设计，导致自动验证器难以消解证明义务：缺失或隐式证明锚点、不利于自动推理的程序结构、代码级状态与规范级需求事实不一致，且即便验证通过也可能只建立了遗漏关键行为的弱规范。

Method: 提出面向形式验证的联合合成框架 VeriGen-C，从自然语言需求同时生成 C 程序与 ACSL 规范。代码生成与修复被约束为暴露与证明相关的状态、简化控制流以适配演绎验证、消除阻碍自动证明搜索的实现模式；规范生成与精炼同样受约束，以缓解验证超时并保证与用户需求对齐。

Result: 在 HumanEval-X、FM-Bench 与 LiveCodeBench Lite 上，相对接口兼容基线，已验证且需求对齐的解决方案比例分别从 5.5% 提升至 44.5%、从 44.3% 提升至 80.0%、从 6.5% 提升至 58.7%。

Conclusion: 将实现与规范作为统一工件联合合成、并对二者施加验证友好约束，可有效提升形式验证成功率和需求对齐度。

Abstract: AI-assisted code generation amplifies the demand for automated formal verification. Generated programs must not only be executable but also accompanied by machine-checkable evidence of correctness. Existing approaches can synthesize functionally plausible code or plausible-looking specifications from natural-language prompts; however, they do not explicitly co-design the implementation and specification as a unified artifact optimized for verification. Consequently, automated verifiers often fail to discharge proof obligations. Typical failure modes include missing or implicit proof anchors, program structures that are hostile to automated reasoning, and inconsistencies between code-level state and specification-level requirement facts arising from the independent generation of code and specifications. Even when verification succeeds, it may only establish weak specifications that omit requirement-critical behavior. This paper introduces VeriGen-C, a formal-verification-friendly framework for the joint synthesis of C programs and ACSL specifications from natural-language requirements. Code generation and repair are constrained to expose proof-relevant state, to streamline control flow for deductive verification, and to eliminate implementation patterns that hinder automated proof search. Specification generation and refinement are similarly constrained to mitigate verification timeouts and to ensure alignment with the user requirements. Empirical evaluations on HumanEval-X, FM-Bench, and LiveCodeBench Lite demonstrate that VeriGen-C substantially improves the rate of verified and requirement-aligned solutions relative to an interface-compatible baseline, from 5.5% to 44.5%, from 44.3% to 80.0%, and from 6.5% to 58.7%, respectively.

</details>


### [231] [VLTest: Finding Joint Image-Text Failures in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30794)
*Bin Duan,Jintao Lin,Guowei Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: VLTest 是一种黑盒视觉语言模型测试方法，通过过滤保持预期答案的变体、并对每个单模态变异先单独测试再做四格组合测试，从而准确识别和归因失败，在确认失败数量与联合失败归因上显著优于 SINVAD 和 TextFooler。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 已被广泛嵌入软件系统，但测试存在两个难点：(1) 变异图像或文本任一输入都可能改变预期答案，因此输出变化本身不代表失败；(2) 部分失败仅在图文同时变异时出现，若要将其归因于变异组合，需验证每个变异单独作用的效果。

Method: 提出 VLTest 黑盒测试方法：图像变体通过编辑 VQGAN 隐码生成，文本变体通过 rewrite-expand-prune 流水线生成；过滤可能改变预期答案的变体；先测试每个单独变异，再测试组合，使每个联合失败都有四个匹配的测试单元（四格确认）；由 VLM 判官在确认输入仍保持预期答案后才判定失败。

Result: 在三个任务、八个 VLM（含两个商用 API 模型）上：判官确认 81.7% 的候选，对人工标注的精确率为 88.8%–94.6%；四格确认拒绝 22.5% 的联合候选。相同预算下，VLTest 生成器发现的确认失败数为 SINVAD 与 TextFooler 的 1.8–2.5 倍；每 1000 次联合调用发现 91–197 个可归因联合失败，为基线变体同种配对方式的 1.3 倍。

Conclusion: VLTest 通过保持答案的变体过滤与四格联合确认机制，有效缓解了输出变化不等于失败、以及联合失败归因困难两大问题，在确认失败检出效率与联合失败归因能力上优于现有基线，适用于真实 VLM 系统的黑盒测试。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly embedded in software systems. Testing them raises two problems. Mutating either input can change the expected answer, so an output change is not by itself a failure. Some failures appear only when both inputs are mutated; attributing them to the combination requires testing each mutation alone. We present VLTest, a black-box VLM testing approach. It generates image variants by editing VQGAN latent codes and text variants with a rewrite-expand-prune pipeline, filters variants likely to change the expected answer, and tests each variant alone before testing combinations, so every joint failure has four matched cells. A VLM judge confirms a failure only after checking that the input keeps the expected answer. On three tasks with eight VLMs, including two commercial API models, the oracle confirms 81.7% of the candidates, with 88.8% to 94.6% precision against human labels, and four-cell confirmation rejects 22.5% of joint candidates. Under the same budget, VLTest's generators find 1.8 to 2.5 times as many confirmed failures as SINVAD and TextFooler, and its joint tests find 91 to 197 attributed joint failures per 1,000 joint calls, 1.3 times as many as the same pairing applied to the baselines' variants.

</details>


### [232] [What Ethereum Developers Ask For, and Whether They Get It: Ten Years of Ethereum Stack Exchange](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30829)
*Tamer Abdelaziz*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文分析2016—2025年Ethereum Stack Exchange全部54,637个提问，用提示式主题流水线（8个以太坊专属种子话题，共22类）打标签，并以三模型委员会筛查、人工裁决争议样本（准确率87.9%–93.5%）。研究发现：需求集中于合约编写、部署与调试，工具链为第二大且持续增长的话题；回答供给整体走弱——获得任一回答的比例下降9.5–18.6个百分点，获得采纳答案的比例下降16.2–24.5个百分点（后者在已获回答样本中）。主题构成变化仅能解释0.08个百分点，而提问者身份（声誉）解释力更强：采纳率从<10声誉的19.8%到≥10k声誉的66.8%，跨度47个百分点，新用户占比上升可解释最多59%的采纳率下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有以太坊开发者痛点研究依赖问卷与访谈，只能记录开发者被问及特定主题时的"所述"；ESE则记录了开发者自发"所求"以及是否被解决。作者希望用完整记录回答三个问题：开发者求助什么、该渠道是否仍在解决问题、若不能解决则变化来自问题本身还是解决方案。

Method: (1) 语料为2016年1月至2025年12月ESE全部54,637个提问；(2) 构建基于提示的主题流水线，以8个以太坊专属话题为种子并允许涌现新话题，最终22类；(3) 由三家提供商的语言模型组成委员会筛查标签，对全部争议样本人工裁决，标签准确率87.9%–93.5%；(4) 因语料为全量，比率不含抽样误差；(5) 使用五种估计量刻画十年趋势，并通过按早期主题构成重加权来分解采纳率下降，比较主题与提问者属性（声誉等7个可控属性）的解释力。

Result: 需求高度集中于合约编写、部署与调试；工具链为第二大话题且持续增长，安全、共识与扩容类问题稀少。供给下降且不均衡：仍有回答到达，但闭环（获得采纳答案）下降更快。十年间获得任一回答的比例下降9.5–18.6个百分点，已获回答者中达到采纳答案的比例下降16.2–24.5个百分点。以早期主题构成重建后期采纳率，降幅仅从9.40变为9.32个百分点，主题构成无法解释下降。提问者身份解释力远超问题内容：采纳率从<10声誉的19.8%升至≥10k声誉的66.8%，跨47个百分点且各年份均有梯度，而跨主题仅17个百分点；提问者趋于新用户可解释最多59%的采纳率下降；提问者可控制的7个属性中最大者仅对应约5个百分点。

Conclusion: 以太坊开发者支持缺口集中在工具链领域与新进开发者群体。ESE虽仍产出回答，但问题闭环能力显著弱化，且弱化主要源于提问者构成（新人比例上升）而非话题构成；因此改进应聚焦工具链文档/工具成熟度，并针对新手的提问与答疑机制。

Abstract: The Ethereum community learns where developers struggle through surveys and interviews, which record what developers say when asked about a chosen theme. Ethereum Stack Exchange (ESE) records what they ask for unprompted, and whether anyone resolves it. We study every question posted to ESE from Jan 2016 to Dec 2025, 54,637 in all, and label each with a prompt-based topic pipeline seeded with eight Ethereum-specific topics and open to new ones (22 topics in all). A three-provider committee of language models screens the labels, and manual adjudication of every disputed case puts their accuracy at 87.9 to 93.5%. Because the corpus is the complete record, no rate carries sampling error. We ask what developers ask for help with, whether the venue still resolves it, and, where it does not, whether the questions changed or their resolution did.
  Demand is concentrated on writing, deploying, and debugging contracts; the toolchain is the second-largest topic and growing, while security, consensus, and scaling are thin. Supply has weakened, and unevenly: answers still arrive, closure falls further. Across five estimators of the decade, the share of questions drawing any answer fell by 9.5 to 18.6 percentage points, but among answered questions the share reaching an accepted answer fell by 16.2 to 24.5. Rebuilding the later acceptance rate at the earlier topic mix moves the fall only from 9.40 to 9.32 points, so the topic mix does not explain it. Who asks separates outcomes far more sharply than what is asked: acceptance runs from 19.8% for askers below 10 reputation to 66.8% at 10k and above, a 47- point spread with a gradient in every year, against 17 across topics, and a shift toward newer askers accounts for up to 59% of the acceptance fall. The largest of seven properties an asker controls accompanies 5 points. The record places the support gap in the toolchain and among newcomers.

</details>


### [233] [Separate Field Scoring Protects Unused Tools When Agents Pool Tool-Use Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30867)
*Abhijit Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该文提出SYNAPSE方法，将工具使用示例作为独立字段纳入检索评分，并通过一个为有使用记录的工具保留槽位的路由器，在提升有使用记录工具命中率的同时保护无使用记录的held-out工具的检索效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体共享同一工具目录时，可以汇集"哪个工具服务哪类请求"的使用经验。但直接把使用示例作为额外条目加入索引会挤占held-out工具（无任何agent使用过）的检索空间，因此需要一种既能利用使用信号、又不损害未见过工具检索的方法。

Method: 对比两种做法：(1) 将使用示例作为额外索引条目；(2) 提出SYNAPSE——将使用情况作为独立字段单独评分。并设置消融：为有使用记录的工具加入等量、等长的其他工具文本条目；用其他工具文本替换该工具自身的使用记录；对有无使用记录的工具施加常数分数偏移。在StableToolBench及另外两个目录（含TOUCAN、ToolRet查询集）上，配合两种嵌入器评估Hit@5，并与为使用记录预留槽位的路由器比较。

Result: 在三个目录中的两个、两种嵌入器下，加入使用示例作为额外条目显著提升有使用记录工具的Hit@5，但大幅降低held-out工具的Hit@5；填充其他文本的消融也带来几乎同等的held-out损失，却几乎没有增益。StableToolBench上SYNAPSE的held-out Hit@5为0.785，高于预留槽位路由器的0.683，且在三个目录、两种嵌入器上均保持领先；该保护特性在其他文本替换和常数偏移设置下依然存在，而有使用记录工具的增益则必须依赖"正确的工具"的使用信息。在TOUCAN（两种嵌入器）和ToolRet（一种嵌入器）上，路由器在有标注查询上保住了更多增益；将使用记录分配给十个模拟智能体，其汇集效果与单智能体持有同样记录完全一致。

Conclusion: 把使用情况作为独立评分字段（SYNAPSE）可在获取使用增益的同时保护held-out工具检索，优于将示例直接注入索引；但增益最大化的方案取决于工作负载，需在使用增益与held-out保护之间权衡。智能体间汇集使用经验无额外损耗，可与单智能体同等获益。

Abstract: Agents that call tools from a shared catalog can pool what they learn about which tool serves which request. We measure what pooled usage examples do to tool retrieval, reporting separately the held-out tools, for which no agent has usage, and all other tools. Adding usage examples to the index as extra entries for used tools raises Hit@5 (whether a gold tool is in the top five) for those tools and lowers it for held-out tools, by a large margin on two of three catalogs and with both embedders we tested. Giving used tools the same number of equally long entries filled with other tools' text lowers held-out Hit@5 about as much, but gives little or none of the gain. We call our method of scoring usage as its own field SYNAPSE. On StableToolBench, the held-out gold tool appears in its top five more often than with a router that reserves slots for usage (0.785 against 0.683), and this held-out lead holds on all three catalogs with both embedders. This protection persisted when we replaced each tool's usage with other tools' text, and a constant score offset for tools with usage gives similar held-out Hit@5 in most settings. The gain for tools with usage, in contrast, needs usage about the right tool. The router keeps more of the gain on labeled queries on TOUCAN with both embedders and on ToolRet with one, so the better choice depends on the workload. Splitting the usage among ten simulated agents shows that pooling helps exactly as much as giving one agent the same records.

</details>


### [234] [ATLAS: A Multi-Agent Framework for Lifting Binary to Canonical Compiler IR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30877)
*Xiaoao Zhu,Ni Zhang,Jie Ren,Zhiqiang Li,Jie Zheng,Zhanyong Tang,Zheng Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ATLAS：多智能体框架，从优化后的二进制反汇编重建形式有效、规范的 LLVM IR，在 HumanEval-Decompile 与 MBPP 上达到 91.8% 可重执行率与 89.4% CFG 相似度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脱离源码将剥离、高度优化的二进制提升为规范的优化前 LLVM IR，对下游安全分析与遗留软件迁移至关重要；但激进编译优化会重写控制/数据流、删除结构元数据、引入类型擦除的低层惯用法，导致现有 lifter 生成畸形或语义偏离的 IR。

Method: 提出 ATLAS 多智能体框架，包含两大机制：(i) 结构感知的区域级检索，利用伪探针归因将优化指令对齐到规范 IR 样例；(ii) 协同精化循环：Lift 智能体基于检索样例生成初始 IR 草稿，Advisor 智能体解读 LLVM verifier/编译诊断与静态检查并提出局部修复动作，Validation 智能体执行编辑、重跑工具链验证（可行时含运行时检查）并将诊断回馈给 Advisor，迭代至 IR 通过验证且行为一致或达到预算上限。

Result: 在 HumanEval-Decompile 与 MBPP 上，跨 ARM、x86-64、RISC-V 二进制，ATLAS 在 LLVM IR 提升任务上优于七个基线，实现 91.8% 可重执行率与 89.4% CFG 相似度；相比 Binary→C→IR 流水线，在指针流精度与下游可分析性上亦更优。

Conclusion: 通过恢复良构、以机器为中心的表征，ATLAS 支撑实用的跨 ISA 重编译与可验证的纯二进制迁移工作流。

Abstract: Lifting stripped, heavily optimized binaries to a canonical, pre-optimization LLVM intermediate representation (IR) is essential for downstream security analysis and legacy software migration when source code is unavailable. However, aggressive compiler optimizations rewrite control and data flow, remove structural metadata, and introduce type-erased low-level idioms, causing current lifters to produce malformed or semantically divergent IR. We present ATLAS, a multi-agent framework designed to reconstruct formally valid and canonical LLVM IR from optimized disassembly.ATLAS combines (i) Structure-aware, region-level retrieval, which utilizes pseudo-probe attribution to align optimized instructions with canonical IR exemplars, and (ii) a collaborative refinement loop that closes the gap between probabilistic generation and deterministic compiler constraints. Concretely, a Lift agent generates an initial IR draft conditioned on retrieved exemplars, an Advisor agent interprets LLVM verifier/compilation diagnostics and static checks to propose localized repair actions, and a Validation agent applies the edits, re-runs toolchain verification and (when available) runtime checks, and feeds the resulting diagnostics back to the Advisor. The agents iterate until the IR is verifier-clean and behaviorally consistent, or a budget limit is reached. On HumanEval-Decompile and MBPP, ATLAS outperforms seven baselines on lifting LLVM IR, achieving 91.8% re-executability and 89.4% CFG similarity across ARM, x86-64, and RISC-V binaries. Moreover, our evaluation demonstrates that ATLAS also outperforms the Binary to C to IR pipelines in pointer-flow precision and downstream analyzability. By recovering a well-formed, machine-centric representation, ATLAS enables practical cross-ISA recompilation and verifiable binary-only migration workflows.

</details>


### [235] [When Code Gets Cheap, Evaluation Gets Scarce: An Experience Report on AI-Assisted Agile Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30916)
*Barbara Weber,Olaf Zimmermann,Marco Franceschetti*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 单开发者不手写代码，三天构建并部署合规关键的人机协同考试评分系统；作者主张AI的核心影响不是加速编程，而是改变稀缺资源——代码变充裕，可靠评估成为迭代的瓶颈约束，工程判断仍是第二稀缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有关于编码助手的研究多止于"加快代码产出"，缺乏对端到端构建与部署全过程的考察；作者希望澄清AI对软件工程实践的真实影响，特别是瓶颈从何处转移。

Method: 单案例深度研究：由一位同时是开发者、最终用户与领域专家的参与者，在不手写代码的情况下构建合规关键、人在回路（human-in-the-loop）的考试评分系统，采用从利益相关者画像到行为审计的带门控（gated）可追溯流程；对比测试套件、真实部署与多步AI代理行为中的缺陷。辅以第二个项目的类似模式及一项独立的单专家案例作为佐证。

Result: 初始系统在三个工作日建成并可对真实群体评分。多个缺陷通过了测试套件：位于被mock的边界（浏览器、语言模型API），或仅在真实使用中出现；部署暴露了本地测试遗漏的假设；多步AI助手绕过流程检查点，直至检查点被改造为可强制执行的关卡。即便在评估成本最低的"三重合一"条件下，评估仍构成进展约束。

Conclusion: AI把写代码变为充裕资源，可靠评估成为每次迭代的必要且约束性环节，工程判断为第二稀缺。提出五条经验：运行带门控、可追溯的构建流程；区分可知与需在运行原型中发现的部分；在真实环境验证；不仅门控产出还要门控助手；记录决策的假设及助手会遗忘的流程状态。该结论在评估需额外人员或专业知识时可能减弱，但正因如此该案例是评估稀缺性的严苛检验。

Abstract: Coding assistants are widely reported to speed up code production; most accounts stop there. We report the end-to-end construction and deployment of a compliance-critical, human-in-the-loop exam-grading system, built by a single developer who wrote no code by hand, protected by a gated process from stakeholder persona to audited behavior. The initial system was built in three working days and could grade a real cohort. We draw one claim from this experience: AI changes software engineering less by making programming faster than by changing what is scarce. Writing code becomes abundant. Reliable evaluation-knowing whether a result is correct and meets the actual need-remains necessary at every iteration and becomes the binding constraint. Engineering judgment remains a second scarcity. Several defects passed the test suite: they sat at the boundaries the tests had mocked, the browser and the language-model API, or appeared only in real use. Deployment exposed assumptions that local tests had missed. Moreover, an AI assistant acting across many steps bypassed process checkpoints until we made them enforceable gates. All of this was observed in a triple union: the developer was also the end user and domain expert, making expert judgment immediately available and evaluation as cheap as it can be. This favors the iteration gains, which may shrink where evaluation requires additional people or expertise, but makes the case a hard test for evaluation scarcity: evaluation still constrained progress. We draw five lessons: run a gated, traceable construction process; specify what is knowable and discover the rest against a running prototype; validate in the real environment; gate the assistant, not only the work; record decisions with their assumptions and the process state the assistant forgets. A second project showed similar patterns; an independent single-expert case corroborates the shift.

</details>


### [236] [REGATTA: A Cross-Domain Measurement Framework and Fault-Injection Harness for Enterprise AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30924)
*Luong Tuan Thanh,Abhijit Sanyal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 REGATTA 框架，用三项测量工具（125 项有源引用的监管检查表、25 个故障语料、设计/执行两阶段套件级故障检测 harness）评估企业 AI-agent 测试套件能否暴露指定故障及其参考规则是否可信，并发现检查表存在大量引用缺陷、两阶段判定一致性低于随机水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业 AI-agent 评测需要证据来判断两件事：测试套件是否能暴露指定故障、其参考规则是否可信；现有工作缺乏对这一测量过程本身的系统性诊断。

Method: REGATTA 含三项工具：(1) 125 项源引用检查表，覆盖美国与越南的 5 个"行业×司法辖区"单元；(2) 25 个故障语料，分 5 类；(3) harness 在设计阶段与执行阶段给套件级故障检测打分。在 3 个 LLM 生成器的 180 个套件（5,400 场景）上做四项测量诊断。

Result: 溯源审计：24 项错误陈述现行规则，另 46 项引用缺陷，31 项仅有非约束性/不可识别/不可核实来源，24 项核实无误；仅被实质标记项经作者复核，24 项无误项仅经 AI 复核。条目覆盖率不均，执行阶段故障画像跨生成器稳定，历史阶段在 66–69% 的故障—套件对上一致。现行模型美国保险重测中，阶段分歧超过重复执行波动，但阶段一致率 .536 低于合并机会水平 .549，未通过 V3 下界不等式。单专家盲审支持设计阶段判定协议（κ=.72, 95% 区间 [.52,.88]）；40 个现行模型执行探针得 κ=.75。

Conclusion: 结果仅刻画工具"按文本所述"的表现，不等同于法律合规性；设计阶段判定协议获得初步信度支持，但阶段间一致率未达预设判据，工具的效度仍需进一步验证。工具、审计与可复用代码已开源。

Abstract: Enterprise AI-agent evaluation needs evidence about whether test suites expose specified faults and whether their reference rules are trustworthy. We introduce REGATTA (Regulatory Evaluation of Agents via Two-stage Testing and Assurance), a measurement framework with three instruments. A 125-item source-cited checklist spans five industry-by-jurisdiction cells across the United States and Vietnam. A 25-fault corpus covers five categories, and a harness scores suite-level fault detection at the design and execution stages. We examine four measurement diagnostics on 180 generated suites (5,400 scenarios) from three LLM generators. A source audit finds 24 items misstating the rule in force, 46 additional citation defects, 31 items with only non-binding, unidentifiable, or unverifiable sources, and 24 verified clean. Only initially substance-flagged items received author review; all 24 clean rows received AI review only. Results therefore describe the instruments as written, not legal compliance. Item coverage varies, execution-stage fault profiles are stable across generators, and the historical stages agree on $66$--$69\%$ of fault--suite pairs. A current-model US-insurance retest finds stage disagreement above repeated-execution variation, but stage agreement $.536$ falls below pooled chance $.549$, failing V3's lower inequality. Blind adjudication by one expert supports the design-stage judging protocol ($κ=.72$, 95% interval $[.52,.88]$); 40 current-model execution probes give $κ=.75$. The instruments, audit, and reusable code are released.

</details>


### [237] [Tracing Hallucinations in LLM Reasoning for UML Class Diagram Generation: An Empirical Taxonomy and Mitigation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30946)
*Mojdeh Rahmanian,Foutse Khomh,Ashkan Sami,Yanchao Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个面向LLM从需求生成UML类图过程的幻觉分类体系（3个维度、11个子类型），基于DeepSeek-R1的推理轨迹标注并检验于DeepSeek-V4-Pro，进一步验证基于提示词的针对性缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM可依据自然语言需求生成UML类图，但幻觉会损害图表的正确性与完整性；现有评测多只评估最终图，未考察可揭示幻觉来源的中间推理轨迹。

Method: 从60条人工标注的DeepSeek-R1轨迹中归纳出三维度（Prompt Faithfulness、Code Faithfulness、Reasoning Quality）11子类型的经验性分类法；通过双LLM标注流水线扩展至另120条R1轨迹，并在20条DeepSeek-V4-Pro轨迹上检验；分析轨迹长度与需求复杂度对幻觉频率的影响；在13个Golden UML ModelSet案例、3个Qwen3模型上进行提示词约束与事后审查的缓解实验。

Result: DeepSeek-R1在两个数据集中Code Faithfulness维度最常见，而DeepSeek-V4-Pro以Prompt Faithfulness为主，呈模型依赖的失效模式；人工标注中轨迹长度与幻觉频率无显著单调关联，需求复杂度与之负相关（尤以Prompt Faithfulness误差为甚）；加入Prompt Faithfulness与Reasoning Quality约束使合并平均F1提升4.0个百分点，事后Code Faithfulness审查无一致增益；关系建模始终为得分最低要素。

Conclusion: 在生成过程内部定位幻觉可为针对性的提示词缓解提供依据；结论向DeepSeek与Qwen3之外模型族的泛化性尚待验证。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) can generate UML class diagrams from natural language requirements, but hallucinations may compromise the correctness and completeness of the resulting diagrams. Existing evaluations mostly assess the final diagram, without examining the intermediate traces of reasoning-capable LLMs, which can reveal where these hallucinations arise. We develop an empirical taxonomy of eleven hallucination sub-types organised into three dimensions: Prompt Faithfulness (deviations between requirements and reasoning), Code Faithfulness (discrepancies between reasoning and generated PlantUML code), and Reasoning Quality (problems within the reasoning). The taxonomy was derived from 60 manually annotated DeepSeek-R1 traces, applied to 120 further DeepSeek-R1 traces through a dual-LLM annotation pipeline, and examined on 20 DeepSeek-V4-Pro traces. Code Faithfulness was the most frequent dimension for DeepSeek-R1 in both datasets, whereas Prompt Faithfulness dominated for DeepSeek-V4-Pro, suggesting model-dependent failure patterns. In the manually annotated traces, reasoning-trace length showed no significant monotonic association with hallucination frequency, whereas requirement complexity was negatively associated with it, particularly for Prompt Faithfulness errors. In a mitigation experiment on 13 Golden UML ModelSet cases with three Qwen3-based models, adding Prompt Faithfulness and Reasoning Quality constraints to the generation prompt raised the pooled mean F1 score by 4.0 percentage points, whereas a post-hoc Code Faithfulness review provided no consistent further benefit. Relationship modelling remained the lowest-scoring element throughout. These results suggest that localising hallucinations within the generation process can inform targeted, prompt-based mitigation. Whether these findings generalise beyond the DeepSeek and Qwen3 model families remains to be tested.

</details>


### [238] [ProductWebBench: Evaluating Website Continuity Beyond Task Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31118)
*Jiajun Wu,Jian Yang,Yaxin Du,Shukai Liu,Linzheng Chai,Tuney Zheng,Wei Zhang,Haitao Yang,Yan Bai,Yihang Lou,Xianglong Liu,Bryan Dai*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出 ProductWebBench 基准，在真实前端代码库上同时评估"目标功能是否建成"与"网站连续性是否保持"，揭示现有前端编码代理的隐藏缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前端编码代理仅以"请求目标是否被构建"作为评分标准，忽略了真实工程变更的第二重义务——站点其余部分必须继续正常工作（website continuity）。
