Method: 构建 ProductWebBench：含 320 个基于已有代码的 Change 任务与 80 个长周期 Build 任务。每个任务在评估前冻结仓库快照、分阶段需求、浏览器证据与确定性检查，且仅在参考解通过全部检查、似是而非的错误解失败时才纳入。模拟用户逐阶段揭示需求，早期通过的检查在后续运行中持续生效。

Result: 13 个模型共 8,737 次运行显示两类义务相互脱节：通过全部需求检查的 Change 运行中 28.2% 仍触发连续性检查失败（空白路由、横向溢出、控制台报错），10.8% 将已通过的浏览器状态回退为失败。在 586 个同时存在两种读数的运行中，18.3% 被判定破坏了已验证状态，回归具精确溯源。交互层面，静态 DOM 已通过的运行中仍有 29.5% 失败。

Conclusion: ProductWebBench 主张以补丁所落入的"活系统"而非单一目标为评分对象，将网站连续性纳入前端编码代理的标准评估。

Abstract: Frontend coding agents are usually graded on one question: did the requested target get built. Real engineering changes carry a second obligation, because the rest of the site has to keep working. We call that second obligation website continuity, and introduce ProductWebBench, a benchmark that scores both on living frontend repositories: 320 Change tasks over existing code and 80 long-horizon Build tasks. Every task freezes a repository snapshot, staged requirements, browser evidence and deterministic checks before evaluation, and enters only once a reference passes every check and plausible-wrong ones fail. A simulated user reveals the requirements one stage at a time, and every check cleared earlier stays armed for the rest of the run. Across 8,737 runs from 13 models the two come apart: of the Change runs passing every requested-work check, 28.2% still fail a continuity check -- a blank route, horizontal overflow, console errors -- and 10.8% push a browser state they had already passed back to failing. Because the same checks also run on the untouched repository, each regression carries exact provenance, not a post-hoc label: on the 586 runs where both readings exist, 18.3% are caught breaking a verified state. Interaction is a further blind spot, failing in 29.5% of runs whose static DOM already passes. ProductWebBench therefore scores a patch against the living system it lands in, not the target alone.

</details>


### [239] [Beyond Accuracy: Disentangling Decomposition from Typed-State Quality Assurance in Multi-Agent LLM Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31280)
*Maria Gabriela Alves Zuppardo,Vinicius Cardoso Garcia*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究在需求工程任务上做归因分析：多智能体LLM流水线的F1提升主要来自分解带来的专用提示词，而非智能体间的类型化契约校验；类型化状态主要改善边界错误处理与跨模型一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM流水线常被认为在软件工程任务中带来收益，但收益究竟来自智能体间的类型化契约（typed contract），还是来自任务分解所促成的专用提示词，缺乏清晰归因。作者因此聚焦需求工程中的这一归因问题。

Method: 任务为将需求分类为功能/非功能需求，并按MoSCoW优先级排序。比较三种架构：单次调用基线；两次调用变体（复用流水线的专用提示词，但传递未验证的中间结果）；带Pydantic校验类型化状态的流水线。实验覆盖18种条件：PROMISE NFR+数据集（625条需求）、3个本地部署的小语言模型、2种语言。

Result: 相对基线的F1提升与两次调用、专用提示词变体相关（最高+0.075）；类型化状态仅带来-0.003至+0.013的小差异，且在六组比较中仅一组显著。类型化流水线能在智能体边界截获越界输出，而未验证变体在更后阶段失败并丢弃该需求；类型化流水线还表现出跨模型MoSCoW一致性的小幅可复现提升，但其成因无法由该设计分离。

Conclusion: 该研究属关联性研究：对比中混杂了提示词、调用次数与分阶段上下文等因素，结果仅基于3个7–8B模型和单一数据集，因果结论受限。实现与复现包已存档于Zenodo。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM pipelines are often credited with gains in software engineering tasks, but it is rarely clear whether a gain stems from the typed contract between agents or from the specialized prompts that decomposition enables. We study this attribution problem in Requirements Engineering, comparing three architectures for classifying requirements as functional or non-functional and then prioritizing them with MoSCoW: a single-call baseline; a two-call variant that reuses the pipeline's specialized prompts but passes an unvalidated intermediate; and a pipeline with a Pydantic-validated typed state. The 18 conditions cover PROMISE NFR+ (625 requirements), three locally hosted small language models, and two languages. The F1 gain over the baseline is associated with the two-call, specialized-prompt variant (up to +0.075), whereas the typed state adds a small difference (-0.003 to +0.013) that is significant in one of six comparisons. Beyond accuracy, the typed pipeline contains out-of-range model outputs at the agent boundary, while the unvalidated variant fails later and drops the requirement, and it shows a small, reproducible increase in inter-model MoSCoW consistency whose cause our design cannot isolate. The study is associative: the contrasts bundle prompts, call count, and staged context, and the results come from three 7--8B models on one dataset. The implementation and replication package are archived on Zenodo (https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.23380444).

</details>


### [240] [Superintelligent Software Engineering: Governance That Scales with Capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31306)
*Mohammad Mehdi Gholinejad*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出"超级智能软件工程"学科定义：当智能体能力超越人类工程师时，治理（门禁、契约、决策、知识衰减控制）必须随能力同步扩展；仅靠能力提升无法改善软件质量。基于三项论点与一个简单模型说明治理的必要性，给出GitHub仓库的探索性证据，并提出方法论框架OSTEON，但明确声明未测量其实际效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究集中于训练能力超越人类工程师的软件智能体，但作者认为"能力"本身不等于"更好的软件工程"。核心隐忧是：一旦智能体产出速度超过评审者，人类阅读所能捕获的、建立在过时知识上的变更比例趋近于零，因此需要一种随能力扩展的治理机制，而非单纯追求能力提升。

Method: 论证性/立场性论文，方法由四部分组成：(1) 三项命题式论点——正确性依赖代码未承载的决策与契约；知识会衰减；智能体超越评审者后阅读捕获率下降；(2) 一个简单分析模型，刻画固定评审容量阈值之后的失控变更增长；(3) 探索性实证快照：对452个star最高的公开GitHub仓库进行统计，检查根目录或.github下的智能体上下文文件存在率，并测量被引用路径的失效比例（给出95%置信区间与不同解读口径下的区间）；(4) 提出OSTEON方法论框架：为每个项目建立受治理的决策与契约知识库，每次变更对照知识库做机械校验，会话开始时检查知识衰减，规划设门禁，会话记录学习而不直接写入规则。

Result: 模型显示：超过固定评审容量后，逃逸变更（escaping changes）随吞吐量线性增长，而阅读捕获的份额趋近于零；治理可以降低该增长斜率，能力本身不能。实证快照：48.67%（95% CI 44.1–53.3）的仓库存在根级或.github智能体上下文文件；在命名了仓库路径的中位文件中，9.5%–22%的路径无法解析（取决于引用解读方式）。作者明确报告未测量OSTEON的实际效果，将关于其机制的断言标注为观点、将作者实践启发式标注为设计理据，并列出了可证伪该论题的问题。

Conclusion: 应把"能力扩展"与"治理扩展"视为并行需求，将超级智能软件工程确立为一门以治理随能力扩展为核心的学科。现有仓库的智能体上下文文件已相当普遍但质量堪忧（相当比例路径已失效），印证知识衰减问题。OSTEON作为该范式下的可检验处方被提出，其价值有待后续按作者列出的可证伪问题加以实证检验。

Abstract: Software agents are being trained to exceed human engineers; we argue that capability alone does not produce better software engineering. We define superintelligent software engineering as the discipline of building and evolving software with agents whose capability exceeds that of human engineers, in which governance (gates, contracts, decisions and decay control) scales with capability. The argument rests on three claims. First, correctness in a project rests on decisions and contracts that the code does not carry. Second, knowledge decays. Third, once an agent outpaces its reviewers, reading catches a shrinking share of the changes built on stale knowledge. A simple model shows that, past a fixed review capacity, escaping changes grow linearly with throughput while the share that reading catches falls toward zero; governance can lower the slope, capability alone cannot. An exploratory snapshot of 452 of the most-starred public GitHub repositories finds a root-level or .github agent context file in 48.67% (95% confidence interval 44.1 to 53.3); in the median file that names repository paths, 9.5% to 22% of those paths do not resolve, depending on how a reference is read. We then present OSTEON, a discipline for the regime, released with a DOI (10.5281/zenodo.23436020): each project gets a governed knowledge repository of decisions and contracts, every change is checked mechanically against it, and the knowledge itself is checked for decay at every session start; planning is gated, and sessions record what they learn without writing rules. We report no measured effect of OSTEON, mark claims about its mechanisms as opinion and the author's practice heuristics as design rationale, and state the questions that would falsify the thesis.

</details>


### [241] [Typed Template Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31342)
*Lu Maltsis,Dominik Winterer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 Typed Template Fuzzing (TTF)，用 LLM 仅在预处理阶段将种子转换为类型化模板，从而在无需语言特定解析器、类型检查器和额外 LLM 查询的情况下实现语义感知的变异模糊测试，实现工具 Typos。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂输入软件的模糊测试长期困难：传统方法要么需手工编码语言约束、工程量大，要么依赖用户声明文法；近期 LLM-based fuzzer 又有缺陷——直接生成测试会牺牲吞吐量且 LLM 出错时产生无效输入，或依赖语言特定解析器、静态分析和 AST 操作库。

Method: TTF 在预处理步骤中用 LLM 把种子变换为类型化模板；这些模板使变异式模糊测试具备语义感知能力，且模糊测试过程中无需语言特定解析器、类型检查器或进一步的 LLM 查询。利用种子与模板的对应关系，TTF 还能修复 LLM 的错误。工具实现为 Typos。

Result: 在 21 种输入语言（含 C、C++、Go、Rust、SystemVerilog、Scala、Java）及 PyTorch、Qiskit 等库上评估；为期六个月的模糊测试活动中报告 135 个 bug，其中 128 个为先前未知；平均 96% 的变体有效，接近领域专用 fuzzer 的有效率；并优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过将 LLM 限定在预处理阶段并借助类型化模板，TTF/Typos 兼顾了语义正确性与变异式模糊测试的吞吐量，实现了跨多语言的通用、低开销且高有效率的问题发现。

Abstract: Fuzzing software with complex inputs has been a longstanding challenge. Traditionally, fuzzers either programmatically encode language constraints, requiring significant engineering effort, or rely on users to declare grammars and constraints. Recent LLM-based fuzzers address these drawbacks but introduce new issues. Some use LLMs for test generation, sacrificing throughput and producing invalid inputs when the LLM makes mistakes. Others rely on language-specific parsers, static analysis, and AST manipulation libraries. We introduce Typed Template Fuzzing (TTF), a lightweight LLM-based fuzzing methodology. The key idea is to use LLMs in a preprocessing step to transform seeds into typed templates. The typed templates then enable semantically aware, mutation-based fuzzing without language-specific parsers, type checkers, or further LLM queries during fuzzing. By exploiting the correspondence between seeds and templates, TTF can moreover repair LLM mistakes. We have implemented TTF in our tool Typos, which we evaluate across 21 input languages, including C, C++, Go, Rust, SystemVerilog, Scala, and Java, as well as libraries such as PyTorch and Qiskit. In a six-month fuzzing campaign, we reported 135 bugs, 128 of which were previously unknown. Perhaps most notably, on average 96% of Typos's mutants are valid, coming close to the validity rates of domain-specific fuzzers. Our evaluation further shows Typos's benefits over the state of the art.

</details>


### [242] [Automating Feature Flag Cleanup at Scale with Multi-Agent LLM Systems: An Industrial Experience at DoorDash](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31351)
*Atharv Chandratre,Jai Datar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DoorDash提出端到端基于LLM的Feature Flag自动化清理系统，集成MCP查询线上旗标投放数据、Jira工单处理与自动创建PR；在50个陈旧旗标上为45个（90%）生成了工程师认可的删除PR，平均成本$4.79、耗时13.8分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运行时配置中的Feature Flag在功能全量上线后成为技术债，可能误启用休眠代码路径带来运维风险。DoorDash有56,000+旗标、623个仓库，截至2026年5月有1,659个陈旧旗标，人工清理需1–2小时/个；现有基于规则的清理工具在其代码库中效果不足。

Method: 构建端到端多智能体LLM清理系统：通过MCP查询实时旗标投放数据，处理Jira工单，并端到端生成Pull Request（完成旗标及其门控代码的移除）。

Result: 在50个陈旧旗标评测中，45个（90%）产出了工程师批准的删除PR；平均每次清理成本4.79美元、耗时13.8分钟。基于成功率、单次成本与时间指标评估了系统的质量与可靠性边界。

Conclusion: 系统证明了大规模场景下的运营可行性；论文进一步总结了架构设计中的经验教训，为开发类似多智能体系统提供实践指导。

Abstract: Feature flags are runtime configuration mechanisms used for gradual feature rollouts, experimentation, and kill switches. Once a feature is fully rolled out, the flag and its gated code become technical debt and can introduce operational risk through inadvertently enabling dormant code paths. At DoorDash, over 56,000 feature flags span 623 repositories, with 1,659 currently stale as of May 2026, with internal estimates of one to two hours per manual cleanup. Existing rule-based cleanup tools proved insufficient in DoorDash's codebases. Our contribution is an end-to-end automated LLM-based feature flag cleanup system that integrates querying live feature flag rollout data using MCP, Jira ticket processing, and end-to-end pull request creation. Our system successfully produced engineer-approved flag removal PRs for 45 (90%) of the 50 stale flags it was evaluated on, proving the system's operational viability at scale with each cleanup averaging only $4.79 and 13.8 minutes. We evaluated the system's quality and reliability boundary based on success rates, cost per cleanup and time metrics. Furthermore, we discuss our lessons learned when architecting this system, to provide practical guidance for developing similar multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [243] [Procrustes-Bering-Sheaft: Automated Model Discovery and Virtual Crash Tests for Microservices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31444)
*Anatoly A. Krasnovsky*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 Procrustes-Bering-Sheaft 工具链，自动化微服务的轻量模型发现与虚拟崩溃测试，基于蒙特卡洛采样估计操作可用性，并集成强制调用契约、容错扫描、基线对比、组件重要性分析与 CI/CD 门禁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务部署中操作可用性难以在真实故障下评估，现有基于图的建模方法缺乏强制调用契约、容错扫描、基线比较与流水线集成手段；同时可达性与实际成功条件之间存在偏差，需要显式刻画操作成功谓词。

Method: 三组件解耦：Procrustes 校验部署与追踪证据；Bering 发现依赖并纳入副本声明；Sheaft 评估声明的操作成功条件。每个测试通过蒙特卡洛采样服务与通信故障估计可用性；各组件以独立 CLI 交换可复用、带版本的制品。

Result: 单一 HTTP 操作演示：6 个受控执行状态下，可达性与实测成功出现 2 处不一致；显式 required-call 谓词与全部 6 个状态吻合。两个单副本新增建模给出不同决策：recommendation 仍违反可用性目标，catalog 恢复基线估计 0.979900 及网格容差 0.12。解析 oracle、受控测试与存档重放验证了实现与数值连续性。

Conclusion: 该工具链将可用性评估形式化为可验证的操作成功谓词加蒙特卡洛采样，强调副本声明与强制调用的必要性；案例表明仅靠可达性会误判可用性，单副本改动的影响具组件依赖性，需以 CI/CD 门禁支撑决策。

Abstract: Procrustes-Bering-Sheaft automates lightweight model discovery and virtual crash tests for microservices. Each test estimates operation availability by Monte Carlo sampling of service and communication failures. Procrustes checks deployment and tracing evidence; Bering discovers dependencies and incorporates replica declarations; Sheaft evaluates declared operation-success conditions. Independent command-line interfaces exchange reusable, versioned artifacts. The toolchain extends an earlier graph-based approach with mandatory-call contracts, failure-tolerance sweeps, baseline comparison, component-importance analysis, and CI/CD gates. We demonstrate the workflow on one HTTP operation. Six controlled execution states exposed two mismatches between reachability and observed success; an explicit required-call predicate matched all six. Two modelled one-replica additions produced different decisions: recommendation retained an availability-target violation, whereas catalog restored the baseline estimate of 0.979900 and grid tolerance of 0.12. Analytical oracles and controlled tests verify the implementation; archived replay checks numerical continuity with two earlier studies.

</details>


### [244] [CAMGen: A Core Change-Aware Multi-View Framework for Commit Message Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31552)
*Bo Xiong,Weisheng Chen,Zongen Ren,Yifeng Zhu,Xutian Li,Runbang Yan,Chong Wang,Peng Liang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 CAMGen，一个核心变更感知的多视图提交信息生成框架，通过功能语义视图、结构依赖视图和历史范例视图为 LLM 提供补充上下文，生成更完整、能解释变更影响与动机的提交信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于仓库上下文的 LLM 提交信息生成方法难以刻画局部编辑如何影响由多个代码实体共同实现的功能，而理解这一功能层面影响对于说明主要变更及其后果至关重要。

Method: CAMGen 先识别代表主要变更意图的核心实体；再通过仓库级语义聚类构建候选功能单元，选取包含核心实体的单元形成"功能语义视图"；同时引入"结构依赖视图"和"历史范例视图"；三视图上下文与代码 diff 一并输入 LLM 指导生成。

Result: 在三种编程语言上均优于代表性 CMG 基线；人工评估显示生成的提交信息更完整，对变更动机与影响的解释更清晰。

Conclusion: 功能上下文与互补的仓库信息对提交信息生成具有显著价值，多视图融合能有效提升生成质量。

Abstract: Commit messages help developers understand code changes and support software maintenance. Recent approaches use repository context to improve commit message generation (CMG) with large language models (LLMs). However, existing CMG methods provide limited support for understanding how local edits affect functionality jointly implemented by multiple code entities. This functional understanding is essential for generating commit messages that explain the main change and its impact. To address this problem, we propose CAMGen, a core change-aware multi-view commit message generation framework that incorporates functional context into generation. CAMGen identifies core entities that represent the primary change intent. It then forms candidate functional units through repository-wide semantic clustering and uses those containing the core entities to construct a Functional Semantic View. This view supplies semantic context for interpreting the affected functionality. CAMGen also uses context provided by the Structural Dependency View and the Historical Exemplar View. The context from all three views is then provided to the LLM together with the code diff to guide CMG. The experimental results show that CAMGen outperforms representative CMG baselines across three programming languages. Human evaluation further shows that CAMGen generates more complete commit messages with clearer explanations of change rationale and impact. These results demonstrate the value of functional context and complementary repository information for CMG.

</details>


### [245] [VERA: A Responsible AI Dashboard for Assessing LLM Deployments in Light of the EU AI Act](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31588)
*Robin Quériaux,Solveig Bachellery,Léa Déléris,Saurabhsingh Rajput,Mariam Barry,Tushar Sharma,Jérôme Lebecq*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: VERA 是一个开源的、可自托管的仪表盘工具，用于帮助非工程背景的审批人员依据 COMPL-AI 框架对大型语言模型进行负责任 AI（RAI）评估。它可将 12 项可由现有基准测量的技术要求自动打分，并记录每次评估的能耗，以提供环境影响证据。在大型欧洲银行的实际部署中，VERA 将评估耗时从原始文件的平均 56 秒降至 30 秒，组织内问卷的实用性评分为 4.36/5；工具与研究材料均已公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受监管机构在部署大模型前通常需要负责任 AI 实践的技术证据。COMPL-AI 已提供与欧盟《人工智能法案》对应的技术解读，将证据组织为六个原则组、十八项技术要求，其中十二项可由现有基准测量。然而，产生这些证据的基准套件多以命令行方式运行，而负责审批部署的人员是非工程师，存在工具与使用者之间的可用性鸿沟。

Method: 作者提出并实现 VERA——一个开源、可自托管的仪表盘，并部署于一家大型欧洲银行。评审者选择模型并在其上运行公开基准；VERA 对十二项可测量要求进行打分，并测量每次评估的能耗，增加运行级环境影响证据。作者将四个以服务方式提供的开放权重模型跑通需求流水线，并通过被试内（within-subject）研究比较两种条件下参与者阅读基准结果的表现：一是直接阅读原始输出文件，二是通过 VERA 仪表盘。

Result: 仪表盘能为每项可测量要求显示得分；在被试内研究中，使用仪表盘将负责任 AI 评估所需时间从阅读原始文件时的平均 56 秒缩短至 30 秒。组织内问卷显示其有用性评分较高（4.36/5）。四个开放权重模型均成功通过需求流水线并得到各项得分。

Conclusion: VERA 表明，将命令行基准评估封装为面向非技术审批者的可视化仪表盘，可显著提升 RAI 评估效率并保持较高实用性，同时通过能耗计量补充环境影响维度的证据。仪表盘与研究材料均已公开，以促进在不同组织情境下的可重复性与可复现性。

Abstract: Institutions that deploy large language models in regulated settings often need technical evidence of responsible AI practices before approving a deployment. COMPL-AI provides a technical interpretation of the EU AI Act that organises this evidence under six principle groups and eighteen technical requirements, twelve of which current benchmarks can measure. The benchmark suites that produce this evidence are typically operated from a command line, while the staff who approve deployments are non-engineers. We present VERA, an open-source self-hosted dashboard deployed to facilitate responsible AI processes within a large European bank. A reviewer selects a model and runs public benchmarks against it. VERA scores the twelve requirements that current benchmarks can measure and instruments the energy consumed by each evaluation, adding run-level evidence for environmental impact. We run four served open-weight models through the requirement pipeline, and the dashboard displays a score for each measurable requirement. A within-subject study then compares how participants read these benchmark results: from the raw output files in one condition, and through the VERA dashboard in the other. The dashboard reduced the time needed to perform the responsible AI assessment, reducing the time required from 56 seconds using raw files to 30 seconds using our dashboard, on average. Our dashboard scored highly (4.36 out of 5) on usefulness in the survey conducted within the organization. The dashboard and the study material are released publicly to promote replicability and reproducibility in different organisational contexts.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [246] [IRAH: Hierarchical Agentic Tree Search for Retrieval Pipeline Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30167)
*Maria Ana Cardei,Aniket Singh,Amir Moghadam,Srividhya Baikadi,Rashmi Shetty*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出IRAH（Information Retrieval Agentic Harness），将面向信息检索的语言智能体树搜索（LATS-IR）与离线元学习初始化相结合，在有限评估预算下联合优化RAG检索管道的结构组件与数值超参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为RAG优化信息检索管道，需要同时选择结构组件和数值超参数，但在有限评估预算下进行联合搜索十分困难；现有方法（如TPE）在此场景下效率与效果不足。

Method: 提出IRAH框架：LATS-IR采用UCT引导的分层搜索，先探索宏观结构层面的管道选择，再细化数值参数，并借助自然语言反思引导后续变异、提供可检查的搜索轨迹；同时结合离线元学习初始化（warm-start）。

Result: 在三个同分布基准和一个分布外基准上评估。在相同元学习初始化下，LATS-IR在三个同分布基准中的两个优于TPE，提升最高达6.4%，在分布外基准上保持竞争力；四个基准整体上，warm-start IRAH取得最高平均留出nDCG@10（0.4816），并优于cold-start版本。

Conclusion: IRAH是在受限评估预算下具有竞争力的检索管道优化方法，验证了智能体树搜索与元学习初始化结合的有效性。

Abstract: Optimizing Information Retrieval (IR) pipelines for Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) requires jointly selecting structural components and numerical hyperparameters under limited evaluation budgets. We introduce the Information Retrieval Agentic Harness (IRAH), which combines Language Agent Tree Search for IR (LATS-IR) with offline meta-learned initialization. LATS-IR performs UCT-guided hierarchical search, first exploring macro-structural pipeline choices and then refining numerical parameters, with natural-language reflections to guide subsequent mutations and provide inspectable search traces. We evaluate IRAH on three in-distribution benchmarks and one out of-distribution benchmark to assess generalization under distribution shift. Under the same meta-learned initialization, LATS-IR outperforms TPE on two of three in-distribution benchmarks, with gains of up to 6.4%, and remains competitive on the out-of-distribution benchmark. Across all four benchmarks, warm-started IRAH achieves the highest average held-out nDCG@10 (0.4816) and improves over its cold-start counterpart. These results establish IRAH as a competitive approach to retrieval pipeline optimization under constrained evaluation budgets.

</details>


### [247] [FormBench: A Benchmark for Fine-Grained Retrieval in Formulation Patents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30175)
*Vineeth Venugopal,Elsa Olivetti,Jonathan Welch*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: FormBench 是一个面向"从技术文档中检索精确化学信息"的检索基准，含 55,352 条查询与两个约 100 万段落的语料变体（随机干扰项 C0 / 结构近邻干扰项 C1），并配有锚点、难负例、软负例的三元组评分信号；评测显示商业 API 编码器表现极差、TAPT 微调可显著提升、BM25 仍具竞争力，且 API 失败源于域内几何塌缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索评测多聚焦通用文本，缺乏针对技术文档中精确化学信息检索的专用基准，且缺少能区分模型判别力的分级负例信号与结构近邻干扰设置。作者希望系统考察商业 API、开源编码器及其任务自适应微调在强干扰环境下的真实能力边界。

Method: 构建 FormBench：55,352 条查询，两个约 1M 段落语料——C0（随机采样干扰项）与 C1（结构近邻干扰项）；每条查询配锚点段落、难负例与软负例，负例由材料本体经实体重叠打分生成，形成分级判别信号。评测 10 种模型配置：商业 API（OpenAI、Voyage）、开源编码器（Nomic、GTE-large、Qwen3-Embed）及其 TAPT 变体；并做语料规模消融、几何分析与分类/聚类对比实验。

Result: (1) 商业 API 在所有配置下得分很低（OpenAI-3-large：C1 0.009、C0 0.213）；(2) 在 FormBench 三元组上做 TAPT，将 Qwen3-Embed 提升至 C1 0.4085、C0 0.4835；(3) BM25 仍有竞争力（C1 0.375、C0 0.467），仅被 TAPT-Qwen3 超越；(4) 语料规模消融显示语料大小显著影响检索行为；(5) API 失效可追溯至域内几何塌缩（within-domain geometric collapse）；(6) 在分类与聚类任务上，API 反超所有 TAPT 变体。

Conclusion: 检索能力与语义任务能力存在解耦：强商业嵌入在精确化学信息检索上因域内表示塌缩而严重失效，而任务自适应微调与词法检索（BM25）在结构近邻干扰下更稳健。FormBench 以 BEIR 兼容格式、CC-BY-4.0 许可发布于 huggingface.co/datasets/vinven7/FormBench，可作为技术文档化学信息检索的可复现评测平台。

Abstract: We introduce FormBench, a platform for testing the ability of models to retrieve precise chemical information from technical documents. FormBench contains 55,352 queries and two retrieval corpus variants each with ~1M passages: FormBench-Random (C0), with randomly sampled distractors, and FormBench-Structured (C1), with structurally proximate distractors. Each query is paired with an anchor passage, a hard negative, and a soft negative - derived from a materials ontology through entity overlap scoring, providing a deeper graded signal on a model's discriminatory power. We evaluate 10 model configurations spanning commercial API encoders (OpenAI, Voyage), open-source encoders (Nomic, GTE-large, Qwen3-Embed), and their task-adaptive fine-tuned (TAPT) variants. We find: (1) commercial APIs score very poorly across all configurations (OpenAI-3-large: 0.009 on C1, 0.213 on C0); (2) TAPT on FormBench triplets lifts Qwen3-Embed to 0.4085 on C1, 0.4835 on C0; (3) BM25 remains competitive (0.375 on C1, 0.467 on C0), exceeded only by TAPT-Qwen3; (4) corpus size has a significant impact on retrieval behavior, as demonstrated through a corpus-size ablation; (5) the API failure traces to within-domain geometric collapse; (6) APIs outperform even the TAPT variants on classification and clustering tasks. FormBench is released in BEIR-compatible format at huggingface.co/datasets/vinven7/FormBench under CC-BY-4.0.

</details>


### [248] [FlexVDR: Learning to Allocate Matryoshka Representation Budgets for Visual Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30177)
*Haowen Xiang,Yibo Yan,Xin Zou,Shuliang Liu,Yi Cao,Mingdong Ou,Xuming Hu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: FlexVDR：面向查询自适应的多预算视觉文档检索框架，通过联合路由族与预算分配器，在单模型内按查询动态选择编码深度、嵌入宽度与token数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉文档检索依赖多向量表示以捕捉页面细粒度证据，但编码与匹配开销大。现有压缩与弹性表示虽提供更小的配置，却未解决"查询该用哪种配置"的问题：分配决策需在付出完整计算前估计各备选表示的检索价值，并适配不同资源偏好。

Method: 构建覆盖编码器深度、嵌入宽度与token数的联合路由族；用检索监督与蒸馏训练紧凑路由，并通过原生旁路保留全预算参考；由Budget Allocator从早期查询特征预测各路由的检索效用，并结合可配置的资源偏好选择路由。

Result: 在ViDoRe V1、V2、V3公开集与五个骨干网络上，FlexVDR在为单个查询选择紧凑路由的同时，保留了大部分原生检索质量；可配置资源偏好使同一检索器无需重训练或切换模型即可调整计算量。

Conclusion: 将预算分配视为查询级决策而非固定配置，可在单一检索器内实现质量与计算的可调权衡。

Abstract: Visual document retrieval relies on rich multi-vector representations to capture fine-grained evidence in document pages, but encoding and matching these representations can be computationally expensive. Existing compression and elastic representation methods expose smaller configurations, yet making multiple budgets available does not determine which configuration a query should use. The allocation decision must estimate the retrieval value of alternative representations before incurring their full computation, while accommodating different resource preferences. We introduce FlexVDR, a framework for query-adaptive budget allocation within a single retriever. We construct a joint family of routes over encoder depth, embedding width, and token count. Retrieval supervision and distillation train the compact routes, while a native bypass preserves the full-budget reference. A Budget Allocator predicts route-specific retrieval utility from early query features and combines these predictions with a configurable resource preference to select a route. Experiments across five backbones on ViDoRe V1, V2, and V3 public show that FlexVDR retains much of the native retrieval quality while selecting compact routes for individual queries. A configurable resource preference allows the same retriever to adjust its computation without retraining or switching models.

</details>


### [249] [Association between Player Perception and AI Disclosure on Steam: An Exploratory Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30178)
*Fabian Wiesinger,Johanna Pirker,Denis Helic*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该文分析 Steam 评论，考察披露使用生成式 AI 的游戏是否与不同的评论模式相关，发现披露 AI 的游戏其负面评论中 AI 相关批评更多。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏开发中生成式 AI 应用快速增长，玩家反应是争论核心，但缺乏对 AI 披露与玩家评价关系的实证考察。

Method: 构建跨多个披露组的数据集，对 Steam 评论进行语言学分析，包括关键词分析、词频分析与特征词分析，探究 AI 相关讨论。

Result: 披露 AI 的游戏在负面评论中伴随更高水平的 AI 相关批评。

Conclusion: 作为探索性第一步，为理解玩家如何看待 AI 披露提供初步依据。

Abstract: The use of generative AI in game development is growing rapidly and is the subject of intense debate in which player reactions play a central role. This paper contributes to this discussion by analyzing Steam reviews to examine whether games with AI disclosure are associated with different review patterns. Using a dataset across multiple disclosure groups, we apply linguistic analysis including keyword-based, word frequency, and characteristic word analysis to investigate the AI-related discussion on Steam. Our results show that AI-disclosed games are associated with higher levels of AI-related criticism in negative reviews. Our findings provide a first exploratory step towards understanding how players perceive AI disclosure.

</details>


### [250] [QProtFuse: Query-Guided Layer Fusion for Retrieval-Augmented Protein Function Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30180)
*Mianzhi Pan,Peishuo Liu,Yawen Ouyang,Botian Wang,Yiming Rong,Hao Zhou,Jianbing Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出QProtFuse，用查询自适应融合PLM多层特征并联合学习蛋白与注释检索，提升蛋白功能预测的RAG效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从氨基酸序列预测蛋白功能对理解生物过程至关重要。LLM可灵活生成自然语言功能描述但蛋白理解有限，故需RAG。现有蛋白RAG方法主要扩展外部检索信号，忽视单一蛋白语言模型内部蕴含的丰富知识；且检索性能随PLM层和生物任务变化，传统仅用最后一层并非最优。

Method: QProtFuse：以查询自适应地融合PLM多层蛋白特征，并通过模态内与跨模态对比目标联合学习蛋白检索和注释检索。

Result: 在Prot-Inst-OOD上取得最佳蛋白与注释检索分数，注释重排后Recall@1达16.4%；下游RAG功能描述质量提升，LLM-as-a-judge评分Qwen3-8B为6.22/10、DeepSeek-V4-Pro为6.68/10，均为最高。

Conclusion: 消融与权重分析表明，查询引导的多层融合与互补训练目标能有效挖掘PLM内部知识；检索应关注层间与任务差异而非固定末层。

Abstract: Predicting protein functions from amino acid sequences is essential to understanding biological processes. Large language models (LLMs) offer flexible function descriptions to natural-language queries, yet their limited protein understanding motivates retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). Existing protein RAG methods primarily expand external retrieval signals while overlooking the rich knowledge encoded within a single protein language model (PLM). We show that retrieval performance varies across PLM layers and biological tasks, making conventional final-layer selection suboptimal. To this end, we propose QProtFuse, which uses queries to adaptively fuse multi-layer protein features and jointly learns protein and annotation retrieval through intra-modal and cross-modal contrastive objectives. On Prot-Inst-OOD, QProtFuse achieves the best protein and annotation retrieval scores, reaching 16.4\% Recall@1 with annotation reranking. These gains improve downstream RAG-based function descriptions, yielding the highest LLM-as-a-judge scores for both Qwen3-8B (6.22/10) and DeepSeek-V4-Pro (6.68/10). Ablations and weight analyses demonstrate the value of query-guided fusion and complementary training objectives for exploiting a PLM's internal knowledge.

</details>


### [251] [Deep Search in Real-World Web Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30181)
*Xiaomin Yu,Hongliang Chen,Zhe Huang,Shilin Yan,Shao-Rong Su,Xuqiao Ma,Feiyang Wang,Jiayu Chen,Jiwei Fang,Yu Yan,Yini Huang,Chen Chen,Ruoxi Cheng,Yuzhi He,Yuhui Zhang,Xiaoxing Hu,Chen Liu,Jianbo Lin,Yijiang Li,Xiaobin Hu,Menglong Li,Zhuoran Lu,Weishi Wang,Zhongqi Yue,Chengwei Qin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出OpenDS任务与OpenDS-World基准，系统评测Deep Search智能体在真实开放Web上端到端多步深度搜索与证据整合能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Deep Search智能体的评测多基于静态检索环境，而真实Web信息分散于多源、多模态、异构格式，且需通过交互式访问获取，智能体自主搜索、访问与整合证据的端到端能力缺乏充分评估。

Method: 定义OpenDS任务：智能体需自主探索真实Web，通过网页搜索、浏览器交互与证据处理，从多个独立来源发现并串联证据，得出唯一事实性答案。构建OpenDS-World基准，含200个高难度任务，覆盖多模态信息、异构文件格式与交互式访问需求，并经人工独立校验以保证证据有效性与答案唯一性。在统一工具环境与执行协议下评测12个前沿模型。

Result: 最佳模型准确率39.0%；1000次独立人工尝试的准确率为21.1%。对工具使用、搜索开销、终止行为与失败恢复的分析揭示模型间研究策略差异显著，工具协同与长程搜索管理仍是持续挑战。

Conclusion: OpenDS-World为系统评估与推进智能体在真实开放Web环境下的自主深度搜索能力提供了统一评测平台。

Abstract: Despite substantial progress in Deep Search agents, their ability to conduct end-to-end research in real-world open Web environments remains insufficiently evaluated. Unlike static retrieval settings, information on the real-world Web is distributed across diverse sources, modalities, and heterogeneous formats, often involving complex interaction mechanisms that require agents to autonomously search for, access, and integrate relevant evidence during execution. To address this gap, we introduce the OpenDS task, designed to evaluate agents' ability to perform multi-step deep search in the live, open Web. This task requires agents to autonomously explore the real Web, discover and connect relevant evidence from multiple independent sources through Web search, browser interaction, and evidence processing, and ultimately determine a unique factual answer. Building on this task formulation, we construct OpenDS-World, a benchmark comprising 200 challenging tasks spanning diverse information modalities, heterogeneous file formats, and interactive access requirements, with independent human verification to ensure evidence validity and answer uniqueness. We evaluate 12 frontier models under a unified tool environment and execution protocol. The best-performing model achieves 39.0% accuracy, while human evaluation across 1,000 independent attempts yields 21.1% accuracy. Further analyses of tool usage, search cost, termination behavior, and failure recovery reveal substantial differences in research strategies across models, as well as persistent challenges in tool coordination and long-horizon search management. OpenDS-World provides a unified evaluation platform for systematically assessing and advancing agents' autonomous deep search capabilities in real-world open Web environments.

</details>


### [252] [FAIRGate: Agentic Runtime Policy Selection and Verified Repair for Fairer Recommendation Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30359)
*Navkar Jain,Enock Ayiku,Kenneth Fletcher*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出FAIRGate：位于基排序器与最终top-K之间的模型无关的智能体式策略选择层，从预定义公平策略库中选择策略，由确定性执行器施加约束修复、验证器把关，最终仅由语言模型对已固定的决策生成可核验的审计叙述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平感知推荐系统通常在训练中编码单一公平目标，或在打分后采用固定再排序规则；但实际部署中，候选池、物品元数据、曝光债务、用户偏好历史与效用预算均随请求动态变化，合适的公平动作应因请求而异。

Method: FAIRGate为模型无关的投递控制器。智能体不自行定义公平目标，而是从含长尾曝光、物品组曝光均衡、集中度控制、校准保护与弃权在内的预定义策略库中选择策略；确定性执行器通过约束修复施加所选策略；验证器仅在相关性、可行性、元数据、校准与审计约束均满足时接受结果；语言模型组件仅接收已验证的审计轨迹，在排序决策固定后生成带证据链接的叙述。

Result: 摘要未报告定量实验结果；定性而言，该设计使智能体承担运行时职责，同时保持公平执行的可度量、有界与可重放。

Conclusion: 将智能体职责限制在策略选择、由确定性组件负责执行与验证、语言模型仅做事后可追溯叙述，可在动态部署条件下实现可度量、可审计、可复现的公平推荐。

Abstract: Fairness-aware recommender systems often encode one fairness objective during training or apply a fixed re-ranking rule after scoring. In deployment, however, the appropriate fairness action can vary across requests because the candidate pool, item metadata, exposure debt, user preference history, and utility budget all change at runtime. We propose FAIRGate, a model-agnostic delivery controller that places an agentic policy-selection layer between a base ranker and the final top-K list. The agent does not define fairness objectives from scratch. It chooses from a predefined policy library covering long-tail exposure, item-group exposure balance, concentration control, calibration protection, and abstention. A deterministic executor then applies the selected policy through constrained repair, and a validator accepts the result only when relevance, feasibility, metadata, calibration, and audit constraints are satisfied. The final language-model component receives only the verified audit trace and produces evidence-linked narration after the ranking decision is fixed. This design gives the agent runtime responsibility while keeping fairness enforcement measurable, bounded, and replayable.

</details>


### [253] [When Agentic Retrieval Fails Silently: Framework and Production Evidence from a Creative AI Assistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30423)
*Yan,Liang,Anuj Agarwal,Aditya Reddy Cheruku*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出"归因鸿沟"问题并给出三层评估框架LARA，在Adobe Express上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CTR、MRR、行为NDCG等传统指标假设"用户直接看到排序结果并据此行动"。在智能体助手（agentic assistant）中，生成层先消费检索结果，该假设在三方面失效：阅读者是谁（agent先于用户读结果）、作用时间（用户行为可能发生在检索后多轮或多会话）、因果归属（最终结果混合了检索内容与模型自身知识）。作者将此称为归因鸿沟（attribution gap）。

Method: 提出分层智能体检索评估框架LARA（Layered Agentic Retrieval Assessment），将检索质量、参与度与结果、产品成功作为三个独立层级，并用四字段血缘（lineage）schema串联，无需新增建模。以Adobe Express为案例：同一检索基础设施同时服务图库搜索界面与智能体助手，后者约占同一用户群体会话量的八分之一。

Result: 聊天用户改写查询的比例高于图库用户（44.4% vs. 33.1%），因为聊天中的改写用于精炼生成输出。LARA为检索恢复了归因能力。

Conclusion: LARA通过分层解耦与血缘schema，在无需新模型的前提下弥合智能体检索的归因鸿沟，为检索系统在生成式场景中的评估提供可行路径。

Abstract: CTR, MRR, and behavioral NDCG assume the user sees ranked results and acts on them. In an agentic assistant, a generation layer consumes retrieval output first, and that assumption breaks in three ways. The agent reads the results before the user does (who). The user's action can come several turns or sessions after retrieval (when). Outcomes blend retrieved content with the model's own knowledge (what caused it). We call this the attribution gap. We propose Layered Agentic Retrieval Assessment (LARA), which measures retrieval quality, engagement and outcome, and product success as three independent layers joined by a four-field lineage schema that requires no new modeling. We examine the motivation on Adobe Express, where one retrieval infrastructure serves a gallery search surface and an agentic assistant at roughly one-eighth its session volume for the same user population. Chat users reformulate more than gallery users (44.4% vs. 33.1%) because chat reformulation refines generated output. LARA restores attribution for the retrieval.

</details>


### [254] [Getting a GRIP on Graph-Based Search for Learned Sparse Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30425)
*Vihan Lakshman,Franco Maria Nardini,Cosimo Rulli,Rossano Venturini*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文挑战"倒排索引优于图索引用于学习稀疏检索(LSR)"的普遍认知，提出将倒排索引的优势融入图搜索的GRIP系统，在MS MARCO v2(138M段落, SPLADE)上于95% recall@10时比SOTA倒排索引Seismic快2.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期工作表明，针对学习稀疏嵌入改造的倒排索引在LSR的召回-延迟权衡上达到SOTA，并在固定召回下大幅优于图索引，社区因此普遍认为图基ANN搜索对LSR劣于倒排索引。作者质疑这一结论，认为标准图索引在稀疏向量上开箱即用表现不佳并非本质缺陷，而是可被克服的实现问题。

Method: 提出三项加速图基LSR的技术：(i) 基于词项的入口点播种(term-based entry point seeding)；(ii) 通过稀疏草图(sparse sketching)的近似路由；(iii) 基于稀疏划分的图构建(sparse partition-based graph construction)。三者整合为新的图基ANN系统GRIP，实质是将倒排索引的强项注入图搜索流程。

Result: 在MS MARCO v2(138M段落)基准上，使用SPLADE嵌入，GRIP在95% recall@10时相较面向LSR定制的SOTA倒排索引Seismic取得2.5倍延迟改进。

Conclusion: 图基ANN索引经针对性改造后可超越倒排索引在LSR上的表现，研究结果对"部署LSR默认选择倒排索引"这一惯例提出质疑，提示应重新审视LSR的索引选型。

Abstract: Learned sparse retrieval (LSR) has recently gained traction in the information retrieval community for the ability to combine the generalization capabilities of neural representations with the interpretability of keyword-based search. A number of recent works have demonstrated that inverted indexes, when appropriately modified for learned sparse embeddings, achieve state-of-the-art recall-latency tradeoffs for LSR, often outperforming graph-based indexes by substantial margins in latency at a fixed recall target. As a result, the community has generally concluded that graph-based approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) search is inferior to inverted indexes for LSR. This paper challenges this conventional wisdom. Our key insight is that, while applying standard graph-based ANN indexes out-of-the-box to sparse vectors leads to uncompetitive performance, we can overcome these shortcomings by infusing the strengths of inverted indexes into graph-based search. In particular, we introduce three techniques to accelerate graph-based LSR: (i) term-based entry point seeding, (ii) approximate routing via sparse sketching, and (iii) sparse partition-based graph construction. We combine these techniques into a new graph-based ANN system for LSR we call GRIP. On the MS MARCO v2 benchmark (138M passages) with SPLADE embeddings, GRIP achieves a $2.5\times$ latency improvement over Seismic, a state-of-the-art inverted index tailored for LSR, at 95\% recall@10. Ultimately, our results call into question the default choice of inverted indexes for deploying learned sparse retrieval.

</details>


### [255] [The Fusion Tax: A Diagnostic Analysis of When Hybrid Retrieval Hurts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30612)
*Aaditya Chauhan,Arun Agarwal*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出"融合税"(fusion tax)概念,量化混合检索在单条查询上相对更优组件的排名损失,并据此给出以稀疏-稠密平衡为依据的融合决策指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合检索(稀疏BM25 + 稠密编码器,常用RRF)因聚合指标提升而被广泛推荐,但聚合收益掩盖了单个查询上的损失;作者主张把"不融合"作为一等公民结果来审视,追问融合在个体查询层面究竟付出什么代价。

Method: 在混合检索的真实部署配置下系统评测:标准Anserini BM25、5个稠密编码器(22M–596M参数、跨三代模型)、学习型稀疏SPLADE替代方案,以及cross-encoder二阶段重排;覆盖7个BEIR数据集、3种融合函数(含RRF与稠密加权分数插值),并以留一语料验证选权重、损失厌恶的风险敏感评估、跨语料可迁移性分析。

Result: 即使最优方法(稠密加权分数插值)也在13–48%查询上把相关文档排到其两个组件中较优者之下,在8–21%查询上低于可部署的纯稠密检索器(留一验证权重下为14–48%与11–25%),而聚合上仍全面胜过纯稠密;在7个语料中的6个上,风险敏感评估显示下行损失显著。该损失以排序效应为主、聚合召回基本不变,最易出现在仅被单一检索器覆盖的查询上,并跨编码器梯队与SPLADE替代稳定存在。当稠密检索器明显更强时RRF税更高,仅在两者均衡时才优于纯稠密。融合失败的预测在语料内较弱、跨语料迁移不可靠,因预测特征符号随稀疏-稠密平衡而改变;cross-encoder仅在自身可靠时挽回部分损失。

Conclusion: 混合检索的聚合优势与单查询损失并存,应显式建模融合代价;论文将发现凝练为以目标语料实测稀疏-稠密平衡为键的融合决策指南。

Abstract: Hybrid retrieval, which fuses a sparse lexical retriever with a dense neural one, is widely recommended in the software documentation surveyed in September 2026, usually with reciprocal rank fusion (RRF), on the strength of aggregate gains. We ask what fusion costs on individual queries, treating "do not fuse" as a first-class outcome, in the setting in which hybrid retrieval is deployed: a standard Anserini BM25, five dense encoders spanning 22M-596M parameters and three model generations, a learned-sparse (SPLADE) alternative, and a cross-encoder second stage, on seven BEIR datasets and three fusion functions. Even the best method, dense-weighted score interpolation, ranks relevant documents below the better of its two components on 13-48% of queries and below the deployable dense-only retriever on 8-21% (14-48% and 11-25% at a weight selected under leave-one-corpus-out validation), while beating dense-only in aggregate on every corpus with the primary encoder; the downside is significant under loss-averse risk-sensitive evaluation on six of the seven corpora. We call this loss the fusion tax. It is mostly a ranking effect that preserves recall in aggregate, it is most likely on the queries that only one retriever covers, and it persists across the encoder ladder and the SPLADE substitution. RRF carries the higher tax wherever the dense retriever is clearly stronger and only beats dense-only where the two components are balanced. Fusion failure is weakly predictable within a corpus but transfers unreliably across corpora, because the features that predict it change sign with the sparse-dense balance. A cross-encoder recovers part of the tax only where it is itself reliable. We distill these findings into guidance that keys the fusion decision to the measured sparse-dense balance of the target corpus.

</details>


### [256] [OneLatent: Latent Reasoning for Efficient Foundation Recommendation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30675)
*Hao Jiang,Peiru Du,Pengfei Yao,Mengting Li,Siyuan Lou,Kuo Cai,Sheng Yu,Qiang Luo,Jian Liang,Ruiming Tang,Fei Pan,Peng Jiang,Wenwu Ou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出OneLatent隐式推理框架，将显式CoT压缩为可学习latent token，实现高效推荐并线上收益提升9.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有以LLM为骨干的基础推荐模型（FRM）依赖显式CoT推理，需生成冗长推理轨迹，带来巨大推理开销；且依赖人工设计模板，难以刻画多样、动态的用户兴趣。

Method: 1) Multi-View Adaptive CoT（MV-ACoT）：从多视角探索用户兴趣并自适应调整推理复杂度，生成多样高质量教师监督；2) 基于预训练FRM的三阶段latent token对齐范式，将CoT轨迹渐进内化为可学习latent token；3) 多阶段课程式后训练策略，激活latent-token推理以适配下游推荐任务；并构建生产级实时服务系统。

Result: 在工业级快手数据集与公开Kuaishou LLM-Rec基准上稳定优于显式CoT方法与传统基线；相比Think与No-Think变体，SID@64分别提升17.44%与9.33%，在线推理吞吐提升17倍以上。

Conclusion: 隐式latent推理可在无显式推理轨迹的情况下保持/超越CoT效果；快手本地服务广告场景在线A/B测试显示相对OneRec、OneReason等强基线实现约9.6%的收益提升，验证其工业可部署性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong reasoning capabilities, motivating their use as the backbone of foundation recommendation models (FRMs). Existing methods enhance recommendations through explicit Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning under a Think-then-Answer paradigm. However, explicit CoT incurs substantial inference overhead by generating lengthy reasoning traces and relies on manually designed templates that struggle to capture diverse, dynamic user interests. We propose OneLatent, an efficient latent reasoning framework that compresses explicit reasoning traces into several learnable latent tokens, enabling Latent-Reason-then-Answer inference without generating verbose traces. OneLatent first introduces Multi-View Adaptive CoT (MV-ACoT), which creates diverse, high-quality teacher-generated supervision by exploring user interests from multiple perspectives and automatically adapting reasoning complexity to each instance. Building on pretrained FRMs, it then uses a three-stage latent-token alignment paradigm to progressively internalize CoT traces into learnable latent tokens. Finally, a multistage curriculum-based post-training strategy activates latent-token reasoning for downstream recommendation tasks. Experiments on an industrial-scale Kuaishou dataset and the public Kuaishou LLM-Rec benchmark show that OneLatent consistently outperforms explicit CoT-based methods and traditional baselines. Compared with the Think and No-Think variants of FRMs, OneLatent improves SID@64 by 17.44% and 9.33%, respectively, while achieving over 17x higher online inference throughput. We further develop a production serving system for scalable, real-time FRM inference. An online A/B test in Kuaishou's local-services advertising scenario shows that deploying OneLatent with this system yields an estimated 9.6% revenue lift over strong online baselines, including OneRec and OneReason.

</details>


### [257] [Lychee: Certified Screening of Inverted Lists for High-Recall Vector Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30697)
*Dongfang Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Lychee：一种GPU倒排索引近似最近邻搜索，用张量核心对探测列表做认证下界筛选，只对少数存活向量计算精确距离，在几乎不读满精度向量的前提下返回与全精度IVF完全相同的k近邻，在1M–1B向量上比IVF-Flat快9.7–117倍并可达到0.9999召回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高召回（0.999–0.9999）近邻搜索在面部比对、欺诈检测、医学图像检索等场景中不可或缺，漏检即漏警。然而现有方案存在根本矛盾：倒排索引（IVF）靠增加探测列表提升召回，但需以全精度读取并打分每个被探测向量，吞吐随探测列表数成反比下降；图索引（CAGRA、HNSW）在高召回区饱和、难以达标；量化列表又必须用完整向量对候选重排。核心痛点在于近似索引无法告知哪些查询丢失了真实近邻，因此需要一种"不读向量即可判定其不可能进入top-k"的机制。

Method: 作者提出"认证排除"（certified exclusion）思想：利用精确搜索的认证下界，对绝大多数被探测向量在不读取其内容的情况下判定其不可能进入所探测列表的top-k。具体实现为Lychee——基于GPU的倒排文件检索系统：以同列表锚点（anchors）导出的阈值，在张量核心上对被探测列表进行批量筛选，仅对少量"存活者"计算精确距离。理论上证明Lychee返回的正是所探测列表的精确k近邻（即全精度IVF的结果），且每查询仅读取数百个完整向量，与探测列表数量无关。

Result: 在1M至1B规模的11个数据集上、对比10个GPU与CPU基线：在≤100M数据集、200个探测列表、等召回条件下，比IVF-Flat快9.7–117倍；达到CAGRA与HNSW无法企及的召回水平；在>10M向量时比基于认证排除的精确搜索快5–18倍；在4块GPU上以0.9999召回检索1B向量，在BIGANN-1B上比IVF-Flat快44.5倍。

Conclusion: Lychee通过将"认证排除"下界筛选与GPU张量核心计算结合，打破了IVF中"召回—吞吐"的固有权衡：既保持与全精度IVF完全一致的精确结果保证，又将每查询的全精度向量读取量压至常数级别（数百个），从而在十亿级数据上实现0.9999高召回下的显著加速。其意义在于为对漏检零容忍的检索型应用提供了一条可认证、可扩展的高召回ANN路径。

Abstract: Nearest-neighbor search over embedding vectors matches faces against watchlists, transactions against known fraud patterns, and medical images against confirmed cases, where a missed neighbor is a missed threat, fraud, or diagnosis. Because an approximate index cannot report which queries lost a true neighbor, these applications require recall of 0.999 to 0.9999, at a cost far below that of exact search over billions of vectors. Inverted-file indexes reach high recall by probing more lists, but they read and score every probed vector at full precision, so that throughput falls in inverse proportion to the number of probed lists. Graph indexes saturate below these targets, whereas quantized lists must rerank their candidates with the full vectors. Our insight is to decide for almost every probed vector that it cannot enter the top k of the probed lists without reading the vector, through the certified lower bound of exact search by certified exclusion. This paper turns this insight into Lychee, a GPU inverted-file search that screens the probed lists on tensor cores under a threshold from anchors of the same lists and computes exact distances only for the few survivors. We prove that Lychee returns exactly the k nearest vectors of the probed lists, the answer of an inverted-file search at full precision, while it reads a few hundred full vectors per query regardless of the number of probed lists. On eleven datasets from one million to one billion vectors and against ten GPU and CPU baselines, Lychee is 9.7 to 117 times faster than IVF-Flat at equal recall with 200 probed lists on datasets of up to 100 million vectors. It also reaches recall levels that CAGRA and HNSW do not reach and outruns exact search by certified exclusion by 5 to 18 times above ten million vectors. On four GPUs, Lychee searches one billion vectors at recall 0.9999, 44.5 times faster than IVF-Flat on BIGANN-1B.

</details>


### [258] [Automated Enrichment Analysis with GraphRAG: Efficient Multi-Database ORA Extraction and Biological Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30710)
*Guangcan Li,Shuyuan Zheng,Jiayao Liu,Shigeto Seno,Makoto Onizuka*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出GBOR-RAG框架，将异源基因集数据库统一为术语-基因知识图，用图式ORA评分并检索紧凑连通证据子图作为LLM条件，实现可溯源、跨库整合的富集结果解读，并引入claim级忠实度指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ORA输出常跨多个基因集数据库碎片化，并以扁平术语列表呈现，掩盖了相关富集信号之间的基因层面联系，缺乏整合性与可追溯性。

Method: 将异构基因集数据库表示为统一的term–gene知识图（共享基因连接跨库术语）；在图上做ORA打分后检索保留跨库关系与基因级支持的紧凑连通证据子图；以该子图作为LLM生成生物学解释的条件（RAG）；并提出claim级忠实度指标，评估生成论断是否有显式证据支持。另叠加PubMed文献层。

Result: 图式ORA与常规ORA高度一致（平均Pearson r=0.997），查询时间中位数降低37倍（最高48倍）；检索子图平均36.8个节点，整体整合得分最高（4.12）；GBOR-RAGclaim级忠实度最高（0.90）；加入PubMed文献层后整体解读质量由4.17提升至4.75。

Conclusion: 将ORA结果组织为图结构并检索连通证据子图，可在不损失传统结果的前提下显著提速、增强跨库整合与基因级可追溯性，并为LLM解读提供可靠证据基础，提升解读可信度与质量。

Abstract: Over-representation analysis (ORA) is widely used to interpret gene-expression studies, but its outputs are often fragmented across gene-set databases and presented as flat term lists that obscure gene-level links among related enrichment signals. We propose Graph-Based Over-Representation Retrieval-Augmented Generation (GBOR-RAG), an integrated and traceable framework for ORA interpretation. GBOR-RAG represents heterogeneous gene-set databases as a unified term--gene knowledge graph, where shared genes connect related terms across sources. After graph-based ORA scoring, the framework retrieves a compact connected evidence subgraph that preserves both cross-database relationships and gene-level support. The subgraph conditions an LLM to generate biological interpretations, and we introduce a claim-level faithfulness metric to assess whether generated claims are supported by explicit evidence. Experiments show that graph-based ORA preserves conventional ORA results with high consistency (mean Pearson $r=0.997$) while reducing query time by a median of $37\times$ (up to $48\times$). The retrieved subgraphs average 36.8 nodes and achieve the highest overall integration score (4.12), and GBOR-RAG attains the highest claim-level faithfulness (0.90) among the compared systems. Adding a PubMed literature layer further improves the overall interpretation quality from 4.17 to 4.75.

</details>


### [259] [Bezier-Guided Adaptive Flow for Cross-domain Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30825)
*Ziang Lu,Yu Zhang,Xinzhe Jiang,Lei Sang,Yiwen Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出BGAFlow：结合状态自适应语义演化与用户个性化迁移控制的跨域序列推荐框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨域序列推荐通过跨域迁移偏好知识缓解数据稀疏与冷启动，但用户跨域偏好一致性程度不一；现有方法依赖全局对齐目标或粗粒度迁移策略，易引入无关信息，且难以按用户个性化调整语义表示。

Method: 1) 借助LLM推断用户画像，进行统一的双域预训练，构建共享语义空间；2) 基于潜在兴趣重叠度估计个性化"视界"(horizon)，确定每个用户的迁移程度；3) 以条件Bezier曲线作为几何参考，学习残差流场，依据中间偏好状态逐步精化轨迹；4) 引入动态偏好反馈调整迁移方向，并用推荐监督与轨迹正则化约束适配的有效性与平滑性。

Result: 在真实数据集上的大量实验表明，BGAFlow持续优于当前最优基线。

Conclusion: 验证了个性化迁移控制与状态自适应语义演化的有效性。

Abstract: Cross-domain sequential recommendation aims to alleviate data sparsity and cold-start problems by transferring useful preference knowledge across domains. However, users exhibit varying degrees of preference consistency across domains, while existing methods often rely on global alignment objectives or coarse-grained transfer strategies. Such approaches may introduce irrelevant information and provide limited flexibility in adapting semantic representations to individual user preferences. To address these challenges, we propose BGAFlow, a personalized framework that combines state-adaptive semantic evolution with user-specific transfer control. BGAFlow first establishes a shared semantic space through unified dual-domain pretraining with user profiles inferred by a large language model (LLM). Based on latent interest overlap, it then estimates a personalized horizon to determine the appropriate transfer extent for each user. To enable semantic adaptation, BGAFlow constructs a conditional Bezier curve as a geometric reference and learns a residual flow that progressively refines the trajectory based on intermediate preference states. The learned flow incorporates dynamic preference feedback to adjust transfer directions, while recommendation supervision and trajectory regularization encourage effective and smooth adaptation. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that BGAFlow consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, validating the benefits of personalized transfer control and state-adaptive semantic evolution.

</details>


### [260] [UserArena: Benchmarking Interactive User Simulation for Long-Horizon Shopping Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30932)
*Jujia Zhao,Jiakai Tang,Jing Yu,Wen Chen,Jian Wu,Han Zhu,Fei Sun,Suzan Verberne,Zhaochun Ren*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该文提出 UserArena——基于真实电商数据的长程交互式推荐基准（64,729 条会话轨迹、11,010 名用户、566,102 个商品、13 类动作），并配套交互环境与对生产 A/B 结果的离线验证，支持五级评估；同时提出 On-Policy Trajectory Distillation (OPTD)，用轨迹级意图在 student 自生成 rollout 上提供稠密教师监督以提升模拟器保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic 推荐需通过一串依赖上下文的决策达成购物目标，因此开发与评估此类策略依赖高保真的推荐世界模型，需同时刻画交互式购物环境与用户长程行为响应。现有资源缺乏长程、细粒度真实用户轨迹，也缺乏轨迹级评估与对生产 A/B 结果的离线验证能力。

Method: 构建 UserArena：以真实电商会话轨迹为核心，配套可交互环境；设计五级评估体系（输出有效性、局部决策、序列行为、个性化、最终结果）；并提供与受控生产 A/B 实验对照的离线验证。提出 OPTD：在 student 模型自生成的 on-policy rollouts 上，以轨迹级意图作为稠密教师目标进行蒸馏，缓解长程交互中的保真度衰减。

Result: 在 UserArena 评估协议下，OPTD 在所评估的模拟器中取得最佳整体保真度，并在离线模拟中最接近复现生产策略的真实效果。

Conclusion: UserArena 为 agentic 推荐提供了具备真实长程轨迹与生产级验证能力的基准，OPTD 有效提升长程模拟器保真度，二者共同支撑更可信的离线策略评估。

Abstract: Agentic recommendation pursues shopper goals through sequences of context-dependent decisions. Developing and evaluating such policies requires faithful recommendation world models that capture both the interactive shopping environment and users' behavioral responses over long interactions. Existing resources rarely provide long-horizon, fine-grained real-user trajectories or support trajectory-level evaluation and offline validation against production A/B results. We introduce UserArena, a real-world e-commerce interactive benchmark containing 64,729 session trajectories from 11,010 users, spanning 566,102 items and 13 action types. UserArena pairs these trajectories with an interactive environment and supports offline validation against outcomes from a controlled production A/B test, enabling five-level evaluation from output validity and local decisions to sequential behavior, personalization, and outcomes. To improve simulator fidelity over these long-horizon interactions, we further propose On-Policy Trajectory Distillation (OPTD), which uses trajectory-level intent to provide dense teacher targets on student-generated rollouts. Experiments show that OPTD achieves the strongest overall fidelity among the evaluated simulators under UserArena's evaluation protocol and most closely reproduces the production policy effect in offline simulation.

</details>


### [261] [PAIR: Preference-Aligned Intent Retrieval for Premium People Recommendation at LinkedIn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30941)
*Zhongkai Shangguan,Yanbin Kang,Yihan Cao,Ruijie Jiang,Qiannan Yin,Chang Yue,Zijian Zhou,Girish Kathalagiri,Changshuai Wei,Zhengming Xing*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出PAIR（Preference-Aligned Intent Retrieval）检索框架，将DPO的偏好学习思想实例化为"曝光条件化的多负样本对比目标"，在专业网络（如LinkedIn）中用互补意图偏好替代画像相似度进行用户推荐，并用带位置编码的点击历史聚合实现恒定开销的用户表示更新。0.6B模型在LinkedIn Premium检索任务上MAR@100达0.45。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用embedding基于画像相似度训练，会误排专业网络中的候选人：专业平台的交互是目标驱动、跨角色的，相关性本质上是互补而非对称的（如求职者更愿与招聘者互动，而非与资历相似的同行）。基于画像相似度或社交信号（共同连接）的推荐在好友型社交网络中有效，但在LinkedIn这类专业平台上失效，因此需要建模用户意图偏好而非画像相似度。

Method: 1）借鉴Direct Preference Optimization的偏好学习视角，但将偏好学习实例化为面向embedding的"曝光条件化多负样本对比目标"，无需显式参考模型，训练简化且可高效扩展到大候选集；2）引入用户交互历史，对点击历史做带位置编码的聚合以捕捉时序顺序，在固定历史窗口上实现恒定成本（constant-cost）的用户表示更新，新交互无需重新编码历史；3）服务阶段个性化更新仅需轻量CPU操作，无需重复LLM推理。

Result: 在LinkedIn Premium检索任务上评估PAIR：0.6B参数模型取得MAR@100 = 0.45。

Conclusion: 在专业网络中应以互补意图偏好取代画像相似度；PAIR通过曝光条件化多负样本对比目标与位置编码历史聚合，在无需参考模型、无需服务期LLM推理的条件下实现可扩展、低成本的个性化检索，并在真实工业场景中验证了有效性。

Abstract: General-purpose embeddings trained on profile similarity often misrank candidates in professional networks, where relevance is inherently complementary rather than symmetric. For example, job seekers usually engage with recruiters rather than peers with similar credentials. Current user recommendation systems are often built on profile similarity or social signals such as shared connections, which are effective in friend-oriented social networks where users tend to connect with similar people or within existing social circles. However, this does not hold in professional platforms like LinkedIn, where interactions are often goal-driven and cross-role interactions, requiring matching based on complementary intents rather than similarity or social proximity. In this paper, we propose Preference-Aligned Intent Retrieval, a retrieval framework that is designed to model user intent preference instead of profile similarity. Our approach is inspired by the preference-learning perspective of Direct Preference Optimization, but instantiates preference learning as an exposure-conditioned multi-negative contrastive objective over embeddings. This formulation simplifies training and scales efficiently to large candidate sets without requiring an explicit reference model. Furthermore, we incorporate user interaction history by aggregating click histories with positional encoding to capture temporal order, which enables constant-cost updates of user representations over a fixed history window, allowing new interactions to be incorporated without re-encoding history. As a result, personalization can be updated efficiently using lightweight CPU operations without repeated LLM inference during serving. We evaluate PAIR on LinkedIn's Premium retrieval task. A 0.6B parameter model achieves a MAR@100 of 0.45.

</details>


### [262] [Formal-Informal Mathematical Alignment through and beyond Human Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31011)
*Noah Gießing,Ankit Satpute,Hamid Rahkooy,André Greiner-Petter,Moritz Schubotz*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该文构建了连接 Stacks Project 非形式化陈述与 mathlib 形式化的数据集，并评估检索、LLM 重排序与 LLM 辅助验证以发现新的非形式化—形式化链接，最终将新对齐集成进 MaRDI 知识图谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化数学库（如 Lean 的 mathlib）提供机器可验证的数学知识表示，但与非形式化数学资源之间的连接稀疏且缺乏系统性，限制了发现概念间隐式联系与复用现有形式化的能力。

Method: 利用专家标注构建 Stacks Project 非形式化陈述到 mathlib 形式化的链接数据集；在此基础上评估检索方法、基于 LLM 的重排序以及 LLM 辅助验证，用于发现未标注的非形式化—形式化链接。

Result: 构建了专家标注的对齐数据集，并对检索、LLM 重排序与 LLM 辅助验证流程进行了评估以发现新链接；新发现的对齐被集成到 MaRDI 知识图谱中，支持数学家查找非形式化内容对应的形式化版本。

Conclusion: 该工作为形式化与非形式化数学的更佳整合迈出第一步，并讨论了使用 LLM 连接两类数学内容所面临的困难与挑战，同时开源了全部代码与数据。

Abstract: Formalized mathematics libraries such as Lean's \texttt{mathlib} provide machine-verifiable representations of mathematical knowledge, including definitions, theorems, and their relationships. If effectively connected to informal mathematical resources, formal mathematics could help uncover previously implicit links between mathematical concepts and improve access to existing formalizations. However, connections between informal and formal mathematics libraries are currently sparse and unsystematic. In this paper, we make first strides towards better formal integration. We construct a dataset linking informal statements from the Stacks Project to their formalizations in \texttt{mathlib} by exploiting expert annotations. We evaluate retrieval, LLM-based reranking, and LLM-assisted verification to discover new unannotated informal-to-formal links. To facilitate exploration and reuse of formalized knowledge, we integrated the newly discovered alignments into the MaRDI knowledge graph, enabling mathematicians to find formal counterparts to informal mathematical content. We discuss the difficulties and challenges in using LLMs for linking formal and informal mathematical content. All code and data necessary for reproducing this paper can be found at: https://github.com/AnkitSatpute/Formal-Informal-Mathematical-Alignment

</details>


### [263] [The Delayed Countdown Method for Validating Review-Oriented AI Workflows in eDiscovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31311)
*David D. Lewis,Tara Emory,Lenora Gray,Mark Noel,Jeremy Pickens,Eugene Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对eDiscovery中"以审阅为导向"的AI辅助工作流，本文剖析了其有效性验证存在争议的根源，并提出一种简单实用的评估方法——Delayed Countdown，可嵌入现有工作流与软件、兼容任意停止规则，并能给出有效的频率学派置信区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以审阅为导向的工作流已成为eDiscovery中AI辅助文档审阅的主流方式，但如何验证这类工作流的有效性在法律技术辅助审阅（TAR）领域长期存在混乱与争议。作者旨在厘清争议来源，并提供可落地的评估方案。

Method: 提出Delayed Countdown方法：其可便捷融入既有审阅导向工作流与软件，兼容任意停止规则（stopping rules），并在有效性评估上生成有效的频率学派置信区间；文中还给出概念基础阐释、示例计算，并讨论逻辑上不可能的召回率（recall）取值的处理方式。

Result: Delayed Countdown在保持与现有审阅流程和软件兼容的同时，可对任意停止规则下的审阅有效性给出统计学上有效的置信区间，从而为原本混乱的评估实践提供统一、可操作的框架。

Conclusion: Delayed Countdown为审阅导向的TAR工作流提供了简单实用的有效性验证途径；未来工作方向为降低审阅成本。

Abstract: Review-oriented workflows have become the dominant approach to AI-assisted document review in electronic discovery (eDiscovery). How to validate the effectiveness of review-oriented workflows has, however, remained a confusing and controversial topic in technology-assisted review (TAR) in the law. We examine the sources of this controversy, and propose a simple and practical evaluation approach for review-oriented workflows. Our Delayed Countdown method is easily incorporated into existing review-oriented workflows and software, is compatible with arbitrary stopping rules, and produces valid frequentist confidence intervals on effectiveness. We explain the conceptual foundations of the method, provide example computations, discuss the handling of logically impossible recall values, and suggest future work on decreasing review costs.

</details>
